Chapter 1101: If The Reward Is Like This, It Is Worth Receiving (1)

“Ugh.”

Hyun Yonug pulled back the blanket and sat up. Habitually turning his head to look at the window, he slightly frowned.

‘Has the sun risen?’

Today, he had overslept a little.

Normally, oversleeping was impossible, but while his disciples went to the Southern Lands, he hadn’t slept a wink for several days and nights, so the accumulated fatigue seemed to hit him all at once.

“Oh, my joints.”

He patted his shoulder. Though he had mastered martial arts and kept his body in good shape, age was something he couldn’t overcome.

After fully removing the blanket, he sat cross-legged right there. Only after finishing his habitual breathing exercise did he get up and change clothes.

“Hmm.”

Before stepping out the door, he closed his eyes briefly.

Mount Hua has been achieving success after success. But precisely because of that, he must be more cautious.

Fortune always comes with misfortune. If one becomes arrogant or intoxicated by success, they inevitably pay the price.

‘I must be careful.’

These are things young disciples easily overlook. He, being older by even a year, must first be vigilant and soothe them.

Hyun Young wore a slightly wicked smile.

“Then shall I find some fault today?”

Determined to be a strict spirit for his disciples today, Hyun Young firmly opened the door and stepped outside. But his resolve collapsed neatly in less than a short while.

“...What is all this...?”

Standing at the entrance of the estate, Hyun Young muttered with a dazed expression.

Piles of luggage stacked like mountains blocked his view. Not only inside the estate but also at the entrance and beyond, unknown things were piled up in a chaotic layered mess.

“...Did the Demonic Cult invade here? Are they evacuating people?”

“Ah, no. That’s not it...”

Baek Sang wiped the sweat flowing down his forehead and answered.

“Since early dawn, people have been coming in crowds, thanking us for driving away the Demonic Cult...”

“Th-this?”

“Yes.”

“All of this?”

Hyun Young’s pupils trembled wildly as if an earthquake had struck.

As he raised his head following the piled luggage, he grabbed the back of his neck before even seeing the top.

“Oh, my neck...!”

“Are you alright?”

“...Is this all?”

“There’s no more space inside, so the disciples are receiving and stacking goods outside the estate now.”

“What?”

Flames suddenly lit up in Hyun Young’s eyes.

“Is this guy sane or not? You accepted all that gladly? You took without thinking that these are things brought by commoners who struggle to survive day by day while others rake in money?”

Baek Sang was shocked and waved his hand.

“Oh, Elder. That can’t be. We naturally refused politely. But when they insistently give it, what can I do?”

“...Insistently?”

“They say it’s nothing, why don’t you accept even this, is what they said? They say our life is worth only that much. What can I say? No matter how much I block, they just throw it over and run away...”

“...Ha, haha.”

Hyun Young couldn’t help but laugh in disbelief.

It’s not new for things sent from various places to pile up at Mount Hua. Hyun Young had seen it many times. But this is different.

The gifts that came to Mount Hua so far were from powerful figures in Shaanxi to gain favor. Even at best, they were hardly sent with pure hearts; to put it bluntly, they were bribes.

But the things piled here have a completely different meaning, don’t they?

Mount Hua will soon leave the Yangtze River region and go far to Shaanxi. What good would it do for the locals to curry favor with them?

That means all these are truly goods given by the local commoners as pure tokens of gratitude.

“Hehehe...”

Hyun Young, smiling with an unreadable meaning, approached the piled luggage as if entranced and unpacked one bundle from the bottom.

Inside the roughly tied bundle was grain, not particularly precious, packed tightly. Hyun Young quietly looked at it and gently touched it with his fingertips.

“...This precious thing...”

Though gifts from powerful figures might be expensive, they were not ‘precious’ to Hyun Young. How could things sent by the wealthy just to show off have great meaning?

But this grain was different.

The people living in the Yangtze River region had just barely escaped the Pirates’ grip. They struggled daily just to eat. These people took some of the grain they needed for winter and brought it as a token of gratitude.

How could this grain be considered as less valuable than such treasures?

“There are many small things, mostly food.”

“...”

“But the quantity is too large to store easily. We need to find suitable merchants to sell them...”

“What, you fool?”

Hyun Young’s eyes blazed as he sharply turned his head, glaring at Baek Sang with fierce anger.

“Sell what?”

“Th-the grain...”

“Are you crazy?”

Hyun Young’s eyes were bloodshot with fury.

“What is this! What kind of goods are these that you talk about selling? Are these things you can trade for money? The leftovers here are parasites who eat like oxen; feed them all! Why would you sell this!”

“E-Elder, grain can be stored and eaten later, but fresh meat or vegetables brought in now will spoil soon...”

“Eat it all!”

Hyun Young spat fire from his mouth, and Baek Sang quietly shut his mouth.

“Don’t leave a single grain; eat it all! If you don’t, you all will die!”

Baek Sang’s face turned pitch black.

Of course, those here eat five or six times more than an ordinary man, causing Hyun Jong stomach pain. But even so, how could they possibly eat enough to fill ten huge granaries in just a few days?

“Ah, no...”

“Shut up! Start feeding them from lunch today! Make them eat it all!”

“...”

“Do you understand?”

“Yes, yes...”

“Tsk.”

Hyun Young clicked his tongue in displeasure and looked at the piled gifts with a renewed gaze.

Indeed... young disciples would find it hard to understand.

For some, chivalry is a matter of choice, but for others, it was a desperate hope too dazzling to even look at.

When Mount Hua was once on the brink of collapse, Hyun Young couldn’t even dream of chivalry. For some, it might be a task they can skip if lazy despite ability, but for Hyun Young and his generation, chivalry was something dazzling.

What kept them going through that harsh life was the vain hope that someday they too would fight as proud warriors for the suffering commoners and make Mount Hua’s name known far and wide.

As his hair gradually turned white in longing, and as he realized youth doesn’t last forever, he slowly let go of that hope.

Hyun Young carefully put down the grain he had been gently touching.

“...I was not wrong.”

“Yes?”

“No.”

Hyun Young answered curtly and turned his head. He didn’t want to show his face to the disciples now.

After taking some time to compose his expression, Hyun Young looked again and asked.

“So, are there still visitors outside?”

“Yes. The disciples are receiving the goods now.”

“Let’s go see.”

“Yes!”

Baek Sang quickly stepped forward. Following him, they soon saw people lined up carrying bundles tightly wrapped.

After waiting, a large man reached the disciples, snorting, and suddenly thrust the items in his hands forward.

“I don’t know if the masters eat meat, but I couldn’t think of anything else, so I brought this.”

“Thank you. We do eat meat.”

“That’s a relief.”

The gruff man almost threw the bundle into the disciples’ arms.

“...It looks like a lot.”

“It’s not much for someone like me!”

“Th-thank you.”

The disciples wiped sweat from their foreheads and accepted the goods despite his gruff attitude.

“Where do you live...”

“Enough. What’s the use in knowing that?”

“...Ah, yes.”

The man turned as if without regret but glanced at the Mount Hua disciple.

“Thank you.”

Chung Myung, holding the bundle, looked at the man with questioning eyes.

“I have an elderly mother. If we had to evacuate, she wouldn’t have survived the harsh outside winds, so she would have died here helplessly.”

“...”

“Thanks to you, she lived. Thank you.”

Chung Myung stared blankly at him for a moment, then smiled warmly.

“I just did what I had to do.”

“Ahem.”

The man nodded, then turned and shuffled away.

Hyun Young stared at the man’s back, then turned his gaze aside. The bottom of the bundle was damp, perhaps from freshly butchered meat.

“...Baek Sang.”

“Yes, Elder.”

“Go and bring more disciples. The weather is cold, and the line is too long. If they can’t refuse, at least reduce their waiting time.”

“Yes? But Chung Myung is currently beating... no, training the kids, so he won’t let them go.”

“Tell him I called for them and bring them out. No, bring Chung Myung and all the trainees to receive the goods first.”

“That training is...”

“Hurry.”

“Yes! Elder.”

Baek Sang hurried inside. Even though it was Chung Myung, no one would argue with the sect leader and Elder Hyun Young’s orders, so there should be no problem.

Hyun Young silently watched those lined up.

From the shaggy-haired strong men to the elderly bent old women, and the women holding children’s hands with rough hands, all held something in their hands and looked at Mount Hua’s disciples with admiring eyes.

“That...”

Hyun Young opened his mouth as if to say something but closed it again. As he turned his head, a voice brushed past his ear.

“Isn’t it a good sight?”

Hyun Jong glanced back and said gruffly.

“When did you arrive?”

“Just a moment ago.”

Hyun Jong was smiling warmly at him.

“The sect leader is quite free. What is there to see that he comes all the way here?”

“No matter how busy, there are things one must see.”

“Then why not lend a hand?”

Hyun Young’s words made Hyun Jong smile quietly.

“We are not the ones who should welcome them, right?”

Hyun Young silently nodded at those words.

“Chivalry should not be performed expecting reward. The moment you expect reward, it ceases to be chivalry and becomes a transaction.”

“...”

“But...”

Hyun Jong said.

“If the reward is like this... yes, it’s worth receiving.”

Hyun Young just pouted his lips. He felt that if he spoke, nonsense would come out, so he couldn’t bring himself to open his mouth.

At that moment, disciples came running breathlessly from afar.

“Work!”

“Damn! We get to work!”

“I’ll do it! Please just let me!”

Seeing the disciples excitedly running as if work was better than training, and Chung Myung trudging behind with his mouth open, Hyun Young chuckled.

“Move quickly! You lazy ones!”

Hyun Young’s clear voice was filled with vitality rarely seen for a while.

Chapter 1102: If The Reward Is Like This, It Is Worth Receiving (2)

“It will be alright now.”

After finishing the acupuncture, Tang Soso smiled gently and neatly arranged the needles.

“Thank you.”

“Still, your body hasn’t fully recovered yet, so you need to eat well.”

“...Yes.”

After removing all the needles, Tang Soso poked the cheek of the child sleeping in the woman’s arms.

“Haki too, well...”

At that moment, the woman instinctively pulled the child she was holding tightly toward herself.

“Ah...”

The woman’s eyes trembled.

“S-sorry...”

“No, it was my thoughtlessness.”

Tang Soso scratched the back of her head awkwardly.

The body heals with acupuncture and medicine, but emotional wounds are not so easily cured by external things. Considering what this woman had gone through, one should have approached delicately and carefully, step by step.

“I’m sorry.”

Tang Soso bowed deeply, and the woman, unsure what to do, shook her head.

“No, really... really not.”

A deep embarrassment appeared on the face of the woman holding the child.

“You treat me so well, but I, I am uneducated and fearful of...”

“Please don’t say that.”

Tang Soso was also at a loss. Though she had learned medicine, she did not know how to handle such situations.

‘What should I do?’

If she retreated, the woman might blame herself for a long time, but trying to cheer her up might make things uncomfortable. Helpless, pinching her own thigh, she was at a loss.

Thunk!

A rough knocking sound echoed on the door.

“Hmm?”

Bang.

Turning around, the door suddenly swung wide open. But that was all; no one appeared. All that was visible was the manor’s scenery beyond the door.

‘What...’

Squeak!

“...Ah.”

Tang Soso’s gaze dropped down, and then further down.

“...It was you?”

A pure white weasel wagged its tail, wearing an expression of utter dissatisfaction (?).

Tap! Tap!

Baek Ah, as if scolding for not recognizing him sooner, tapped the floor twice with her tail, then picked up a bowl as big as herself with her front paws and entered the room.

The bowl contained the medicinal decoction she had asked Chung Myung to bring. Tang Soso’s face twisted.

“No, this person...”

Good heavens, he was too lazy to do such a simple thing himself and made a beast do it? And what kind of beast actually obeys such an order?

At this point, it was hard to tell whether the problem was the person or the beast.

Baek Ah trudged in on two legs, placed the bowl of decoction in front of Tang Soso with a thud, and sighed deeply like a human.

“...You’ve worked hard.”

Though it might not be appropriate to say to a beast, somehow seeing that figure made her feel she had to say it.

Baek Ah glanced at Tang Soso, then suddenly turned around, quickly ran to close the open door, and flopped down in a corner of the room.

A moment of silence passed. Baek Ah slightly lifted her head and stared at Tang Soso, her gaze seeming to say, “We’re both struggling, so don’t be stingy with me.”

“...Yeah. You must be tired too.”

Perhaps she was the one who suffered and was tired the most here. After all, she was the beast that stuck with Chung Myung all day. Better to return quickly before she gets unnecessarily tormented.

‘Come to think of it, it’s really strange.’

Why on earth does that beast stick so closely to Chung Myung?

In the Southern Barbarian Beast Palace, she seemed to live as a spirit among spirits, enjoying all kinds of luxuries, but here, she was treated worse than an ordinary weasel.

She got kicked when the man got bored, used as a scarf when feeling chilly despite being a spirit, served as a neck pillow when sleeping, became ferocious when out of alcohol so one had to bring drinks in advance, and took on all sorts of errands...

‘My sahyung must be lucky...’

Is there no little one around?

“A-a weasel...”

At that moment, a startled voice escaped from the woman. Her wide eyes stared intently at Baek Ah.

“Ah...”

Indeed, to an ordinary person, a weasel bringing a medicinal bowl, opening and closing doors, and walking on two legs would seem strange.

“Don’t worry. He’s good... no, just a spirit beast.”

“S-spirit beast?”

“Yes. Um... just think of him as a very intelligent beast.”

The woman looked at Baek Ah with curiosity. Feeling the gaze, Baek Ah slightly turned her head to meet hers.

A subtle exchange of glances occurred between human and beast. Thinking something, Baek Ah suddenly stood up, trotted over to the woman, then jumped up and wrapped her body around her neck.

“Ah...!”

The startled woman reflexively grabbed Baek Ah around her neck, but soon relaxed her grip at the soft touch.

“So warm...”

“Wow.”

Tang Soso opened her eyes wide in surprise.

Though she seemed easy to bully, Baek Ah was originally a spirit beast with an unusually strong pride. How many Mount Hua disciples had tried to tame that pure white, soft fur and ended up with claw marks on their faces?

She never let anyone but Chung Myung near her, yet now she approached a human on her own.

“Amazing.”

Still somewhat displeased, Baek Ah tilted her head, then slowly slid down under the woman’s neck. She nestled between the child and the woman, settled comfortably, and closed her eyes.

“Is it dangerous...?”

“It will be fine.”

Tang Soso muttered quietly, ‘He wouldn’t want to die by senior’s hand,’ but the woman did not hear it.

The relieved woman gently stroked Baek Ah’s back. Seeing that she was a small beast posing no threat to humans seemed to comfort her.

Seeing the woman’s expression soften, Tang Soso nodded unconsciously.

‘She does have some use.’

No, actually she was very useful. But only to senior Chung Myung.

The woman spoke in a slightly softened voice.

“It’s the first time I’ve seen a weasel follow a person’s words.”

“Oh, really?”

Actually, it’s my first time too. Though whether he truly follows a ‘person’ is another matter.

“...They say animals only follow good people.”

At the woman’s words, Tang Soso’s expression became too complex to describe.

“Y-yes, of course.”

But in the end, she tightly closed her eyes.

‘Sorry. I think that’s a lie.’

Otherwise, Baek Ah wouldn’t be by Chung Myung’s side. But what meaning does truth have in such matters? As long as the heart is at ease.

Still, thanks to Baek Ah’s appearance, the awkward atmosphere seemed to have eased, which was fortunate.

“I’m sorry for earlier.”

“Ah, no. It’s nothing serious.”

“I was just worried... really... will it be alright?”

“Yes?”

A look of worry appeared on the woman’s face.

“From now on, I have to protect and live with him... but with such fear, will it be alright?”

Tang Soso could not answer easily. She knew that careless reassurance of ‘it will be fine’ was just a way to evade the situation.

“I...”

Just as Tang Soso opened her mouth to speak.

“Soso, are you inside?”

A familiar voice came from outside the door. Tang Soso turned around in surprise.

“Yes, Sect Leader! I am inside the disciple’s quarters.”

“May I come in?”

Tang Soso glanced at the woman’s expression. She looked slightly uneasy but slowly nodded. Tang Soso immediately ran and politely opened the door.

Soon, Hyun Jong entered the room with slow steps. When his eyes met the woman’s, he smiled warmly... then asked in a somewhat reluctant voice.

“Why is that guy doing that over there?”

“...I don’t know.”

Baek Ah was now stretched out with an expression of utter boredom. The look was exactly...

“They say animals resemble their owners.”

“That’s right...”

Hyun Jong shook his head and quietly sat down. To Tang Soso, every movement showed careful consideration. Every gesture and step was relaxed to avoid seeming threatening. To an outsider, he might look like an old man too weak to lift a spoon.

“Are you feeling well?”

“Yes? Ah...”

Seeing the flustered woman, Hyun Jong smiled gently.

“I am Hyun Jong of the Mount Hua sect.”

“The sect leader of Mount Hua...”

Just as Tang Soso was about to add something, Hyun Jong raised his hand slightly to stop her, signaling not to say anything that might burden the other.

“After going through many hardships, you probably needed time to sort your heart. So I didn’t seek you out. But now, if I don’t come, it would be rude, so I came. Please forgive me if I was impolite.”

“Ah, no. Absolutely not.”

The woman, flustered, shook her head vigorously.

“Everyone has treated me so well... I’m not someone who deserves this... I don’t know how to respond to such warmth.”

She kept bowing her head in thanks. Seeing this, Hyun Jong suddenly looked at the child in her arms.

“Your child is really cute.”

“Yes...”

Hyun Jong smiled gently.

“Do you have a place to go with the child?”

At the question, the woman’s face stiffened awkwardly. After hesitating for a while, she spoke in a faint voice.

“Anywhere... I have to go. Anywhere...”

“...”

“I’ve caused too much trouble. I’m sorry... I have no shame.”

Tang Soso was momentarily startled and looked at Hyun Jong, as if he was scolding the woman.

Still, Tang Soso did not speak for two reasons: one, she dared not criticize the sect leader here; two, she believed Hyun Jong was not someone to say such things thoughtlessly.

“For a woman to raise a child and live away from home is not easy.”

At Hyun Jong’s words, the woman lowered her head with a sad expression.

“The world is both incredibly warm and incredibly cruel. It won’t be easy to find a place to go immediately.”

“Yes...”

Hyun Jong sighed lightly and shook his head.

“It is the Justice faction’s duty to aid civilians, but even we cannot care for everyone without limit.”

The woman nodded slightly. Her expression and movements were calm, but her lips trembled, unable to hide her despair.

How could she not know? How much trouble she had caused these people. After all, medical treatment was a luxury only the wealthy could afford.

Lying in a warm room without work, being served meals and receiving treatment was a life she could never have dreamed of. Now it was time to return to reality.

“I will definitely... surely repay this kindness...”

“Do so.”

“Well, Sect Leader...”

Tang Soso tried to say something but stopped herself. Hyun Jong paid no attention to her and calmly stared only at the woman.

“You are a mother with a child. You must stand on your own feet.”

“...Yes, taoist.”

Just as the woman nodded again, Hyun Jong asked.

“Do you have anyone you know nearby?”

The woman closed her eyes briefly, unable to bear the heavy heart. It sounded like she was being told to leave and rely on someone she knew.

Would she have acquaintances after coming all the way from Southern Lands here?

It was indeed a careless question, but she did not resent the people before her. They had already given her so much.

Therefore, the woman spoke calmly.

“Yes. I... do. Don’t worry.”

“Ah, then does that acquaintance know how to cook?”

“Yes?”

The woman looked at Hyun Jong as if to ask what he meant.

Then Hyun Jong said with a troubled expression.

“Unexpectedly, they’ve been staying at the manor longer, and since those guys eat so much, it’s hard to find a cook. The disciples’ cooking is hardly edible. So we’re looking for a cook. Someone who can cook without ruining ingredients, just enough for people to eat. Since this place is far from the village, we plan to provide a room and decent pay to the cook... If you know anyone...”

“I, I...!”

“Yes?”

The woman looked at Hyun Jong urgently and raised her voice.

“I’ll do it, I’ll do it! I’m good at cooking!”

“...”

“I can work really hard. Please hire me! I’ll do my best!”

“Hmm.”

“Please, Taoist! Please...”

Hyun Jong frowned with a troubled look.

“But you look too unhealthy to work for so many people... and you don’t seem to be taking your medicine well...”

As soon as he finished, the woman hurriedly grabbed the medicine bowl on the floor and drank it all at once. Setting the bowl down, she looked at Hyun Jong as clearly as possible and said.

“See? I’m healthy now!”

Hyun Jong sighed with difficulty and looked at Tang Soso.

“What do you think? Can this person work...?”

“She can, Sect Leader!”

“...”

“Two days is enough. In two days, I can restore her to be able to work. No problem!”

“Is that so?”

At Tang Soso’s spirited answer, Hyun Jong slowly nodded.

“...Finding a cook is not easy, so it can’t be helped. From now on, this person is not a guest living here for free, but a proper paid worker of Mount Hua. Make sure she is treated accordingly.”

“Yes! I will definitely do so, Sect Leader.”

“Then.”

Hyun Jong quietly smiled and stood up. The woman bowed her head in a daze, her face mixed with all kinds of emotions.

“Th-thank you...”

Then Hyun Jong, half standing, asked.

“Ah. By the way, how should we address you from now on?”

Understanding the meaning of the question, the woman spoke in a faint trembling voice.

“...My name is... Chu Young.”

“So you are Lady Chu.”

“And... this child is...”

Chu Young quietly looked at the child in her arms. Raising her head again, her voice no longer trembled.

“Haki. Lee Hak.”

Hyun Jong smiled gently and replied.

“Then, I look forward to working with you, Lady Chu.”

Chapter 1103: If The Reward Is Like This, It Is Worth Receiving (3)

A dazzlingly white sun hangs halfway on the horizon. Just looking at the pure white sun gives an endlessly warm feeling, and it trembles slightly...

“Aren’t you going straight in?”

Kiiii...

No, it’s not the sun. The white marten, which was curling its back, quickly straightened up its lowered back at Chung Myung’s voice.

Of course, it’s not strange for a marten to curl its back. When feeling threatened or stretching, animals naturally lower their upper body and lift their back with all their strength.

But what was strange was that it was not the cute front paws touching the ground, but its small and adorable head... no, its head.

And on top of its body, about the size of a human forearm, was a huge sack that looked ten times its size, resting heavily — beyond strange, it was grotesque.

Kkii...ii...

When Baek Ah, trembling from the weight of the sack on its back filled tightly with fist-sized stones, let out a whimper and slightly lowered its back again.

“Wait, but this bastard?”

Startled by the ghost-like voice, Baek Ah raised her back. But despite the effort, Chung Myung quickly ran over and kicked Baek Ah hard.

Kiiiiiiii!

Baek Ah flew high into the sky, flailing its front legs as it fell to the ground. Spinning in midair, it landed resolutely, then hurriedly dragged the scattered sack and slung it back on her shoulders at a speed like a whirlwind. Then she quickly lowered her head again.

“Who said that even beasts know the grace of being fed and raised? Who said that!”

“...”

“No!”

Chung Myung’s eyes gleamed fiercely.

“Fine, you don’t have to know gratitude. But if you eat the food, you have to pay for it! I asked you to run an errand as payment for feeding and raising you, and you can’t even resist slacking off?”

Kii...!

Before Baek Ah could retort, Chung Myung kicked her away again.

Ppeoeeeoong!

Baek Ah flew spinning into the sky again and quickly repeated the previous action.

Thud!

Baek Ah slammed her head down like a whirlwind and desperately tried to clasp her short front paws behind its back. It seemed certain that if it provoked the wrong mood now, it would become the only precious scarf in the Central Plains.

“Whether it is a human or beast...”

Chung Myung clicked his tongue in displeasure and glared at Baek Ah. Receiving that gaze, Baek Ah’s eyes filled with transparent tears.

Of course, the tears were not from pain or sorrow. Their meaning was purely injustice.

If Baek Ah could speak, she might have shouted, “Conscientiously, when did you ever feed me? You beast are worse than an animal!”

To be fair, what Chung Myung actually did was just roughly wrapping around Baek Ah. Baek Ah hunted for her own food, went to the kitchen to find food, stole food... well, except for that. Anyway.

But since he bragged so much about feeding and raising it, from Baek Ah’s perspective, it was maddening.

But what could she do? She couldn’t express her frustration in words... and even if she could speak human language, would that guy even understand?

At times like this, she could only lie flat and say, “I’m dead.”

“If you’re also stationed at Mount Hua! Huh? If you die, die together; if you live, live together! The sworn brothers are rolling in training, and you’re slacking off alone? Can’t you hold your back straight?”

Kiii...

Baek Ah quickly lifted its back high.

Yoon Jong and Jo Gul, watching from the side, looked at each other.

“Since when did we become sworn brothers with a marten?”

“...I’m hearing this for the first time too.”

“Anyway... isn’t this animal abuse at this point?”

Baek Cheon shook his head as if to say that was wrong.

“It’s abuse of a spiritual beast.”

“Abuse is abuse, isn’t it?”

“I don’t agree with that. As far as I know, Chung Myung loves animals more than anyone in this world.”

“Huh?”

Jo Gul looked at Baek Cheon with eyes saying, “What nonsense is this?” Baek Cheon spoke calmly.

“Because it’s an animal, it ended at that level. If it were us, we’d be thrown into the Yangtze River now, asking the fish how they’re doing.”

“Ah, that’s true.”

Jo Gul immediately understood. He realized Baek-a was treated very specially. Slacking off during training and only getting headbutted and kicked? If it were them, they’d be beaten to near death and hung upside down without food or water for three days.

“...Chung Myung surprisingly has a kind side.”

“Right?”

“And when you think about it, it’s very reasonable. Even animals get punished without mercy if they slack off.”

“On the flip side, that bastard probably thinks we’re no different from beasts.”

“...That’s so like Chung Myung.”

Hearing the conversation, Yoon Jong unconsciously stepped away from them.

‘These guys aren’t normal either. They’re completely crazy.’

But unlike the Mount Hua people who found an unexpected kindness in Chung Myung, the Green Forest and Tang family people could only gape in shock.

“...What kind of marten is this...”

“Exactly...”

Their pupils trembled.

But even as they were shocked, it was hard to explain exactly what they were shocked about. Was it because the weasel was being punished like a human? Or because even the weasel was being harassed and scolded by a person?

“Hehehe.”

“We’re something else too, really...”

“But first, we should think about which of the two is the real beast...”

At that moment, Chung Myung, who was giving a long lecture to Baek Ah, suddenly turned his head.

‘Eek!’

‘Don’t make eye contact.’

‘Hold your breath, hold it!’

The Green Forest and Tang family people who met his gaze quickly looked down. It had been proven over the past few days that just making eye contact with that man made life difficult.

“Are you laughing?”

“...”

“You’re laughing now, right?”

Chung Myung’s eyes gleamed fiercely.

‘Wow, look at those eyes, those eyes.’

‘Better if the Evil Sect bastard was human.’

‘It’d be easier under Jang Ilso, damn it.’

The flavor of Mount Hua. The unique flavor of Mount Hua, only felt at the very top of the towering Mount Hua in Shaanxi, was now recognized by famous mountains across the Central Plains and the distant Sichuan region.

“Anyway, whether human or beast!”

“...”

“The beast doesn’t know the grace of being fed and sheltered and just slacks off! The humans who carry swords haven’t trained properly and collapse panting after barely rolling around.”

“...”

“Oh dear. What fate is this, what did I do in my past life... No, yes. I committed sins. I did commit sins!”

Yoon Jong looked at Baek Cheon and asked.

“What is that bastard saying now?”

Baek Cheon smiled contentedly.

“He’s just talking nonsense as usual.”

“Ah.”

“Just let it be.”

“Yes.”

Meanwhile, Chung Myung, with a very displeased face, propped his chin and looked at the Green Forest and Tang family people sprawled around.

‘It’s more serious than I thought.’

After pushing them around a few times, it became clear what Mount Hua had missed in the past.

Basically, their martial prowess is by no means low. The prestigious Sichuan Tang family, and the rulers of the Central Plains mountain range, Green Forest, and among them the elite Green Forest pirates — if they were weak, that would be stranger.

The problem is that strength is never made up of just one factor.

In terms of ‘murim skill,’ they might be somewhat lacking, but the real problem is their stamina.

“A martial artist being weak... what kind of fish drowning nonsense is this?”

Internal qi and stamina are similar but not perfect synonyms. No matter how abundant one’s internal qi is, if they roll nonstop for three days and nights, stamina runs out first.

And when a person loses stamina, their mental strength diminishes. Such people can exert their power at the start of a fight, but as the fight continues, they end up unable to use even half their ability.

Huh? Then doesn’t that mean they get stronger?

Of course. Fighting repeatedly, risking life even when unable to use half their power, will train both stamina and mental strength.

‘If they survive, that is.’

The problem is that half of them die before that.

The reason cultists are scary is because they are fanatics. And the reason fanatics are scary is not just because they don’t fear death.

Haven’t you already experienced it?

Cultists don’t get tired. Their bodies may tire and struggle, but their minds are encased in armor so strong that ordinary people can’t even imagine.

War never ends quickly. Sometimes, hellish situations of killing and being killed continue for over seven days and nights.

Even if fighting doesn’t continue, war means enduring each day plagued by anxiety over when the enemy might strike. Even those who believed in their great skill often couldn’t withstand the horrors and lost their lives.

“Tsk.”

Chung Myung scratched his cheek irritably.

“I criticized those so-called prestigious sects so much... but in the end, I was from a prestigious Justice faction myself.”

“Huh? What do you mean?”

“No.”

Chung Myung sighed deeply.

‘I’m not without fault either.’

A conversation with Chung Mun from the past suddenly came to mind.

Chung Myung, why do you think Wudang’s skills are lacking?

Because they have no talent.

...Then what about Southern Edge?

They are immature people.

...Then Namgung?

They are rude.

...Is there no way to fix them?

Ah, sahyung. Does carving stone turn it into gold? At best, it becomes a pretty stone. How can you change those born that way? Leave them be.

...

Ah... thinking about it, there really was no answer.

Chung Myung covered his face with both hands.

‘No, back then it really looked like that. What was I supposed to do?’

If you learn the same martial arts but don’t improve, it means you have no talent. Chung Myung didn’t know what other reasons there could be then, nor did he want to know.

But only after scolding, kicking, grabbing by the collar, and pulling up these immature brats did he realize.

That if you carve stone well, it can become gold.

But the past Chung Myung, the Plum Blossom Sword Saint, didn’t know and didn’t need to know. It was easier to just go and beat them all up than waste qi.

‘That method won’t work anymore.’

Chung Myung raised his head and glared at the Tang family people.

“...Greenhouse flowers.”

“...Huh?”

Chung Myung’s brows furrowed sharply.

He had often called the Southern Edge and Wudang’s young disciples greenhouse flowers before. They were half-hearted fighters who couldn’t show their true strength even when facing the Evil Sect or the Demonic cult.

The part Chung Myung was wrong about was exactly that.

‘It wasn’t just the youngsters who were the problem.’

What happens if greenhouse flowers grow well?

What else but more greenhouse flowers. They look healthy and splendid on the outside, but after a few cold winds, they wither. In the end, they become decorations useless outside the warm indoors.

Believing without basis that those who grew like that would fulfill their roles as they aged and improved was a mistake. If even the Tang family and the Green Forest bandits are in this state, what hope is there for others?

“No wonder the rear collapses no matter how much we fight upfront...”

A sigh escaped Chung Myung’s lips as he recalled unpleasant memories.

No wonder when fighting desperately here, the other side collapses, and when fighting desperately there, the front collapses again.

Looking back, the time Chung Myung wasted moving battlefields all over the Central Plains after receiving Chung Mun’s urgent order was almost as much as the actual fighting time.

Whether the opponent was the Evil Sect, the Demonic cult, or the Nine Great Sects, it was the same.

If he wanted to attack properly, those in the rear had to at least hold out. Without that, no matter how much Chung Myung rampaged, the past hell would repeat.

Then what’s the solution?

“...Is there really any other way?”

Chung Myung’s eyes burned like hell.

“Stamina comes from rolling, and mental strength comes from getting punched and scolded.”

As he muttered, everyone felt an ominous feeling and opened their eyes wide. Chung Myung clapped his hands.

“Alright, everyone. Let’s start again.”

“Um, um, Taoist Chung Myung.”

“Yes?”

Tang Pae awkwardly pointed at the sky.

“Um... the sun is already setting?”

“So?”

“Ah... Taoist Chung Myung, you might not know, but we also have duties in each sect. At night, we have to do paperwork and other tasks for the sect to function...”

“So?”

“Th-this is something that must be done...”

“So?”

Tang Pae, lost for words, stared blankly at Chung Myung.

Chung Myung tilted his head as if he couldn’t understand at all.

“So, you’re saying you don’t have enough time for other tasks now?”

“Y-yes! You understand.”

Tang Pae’s face brightened, but Chung Myung tilted his head again and said.

“That’s a strange thing to say.”

“...Huh?”

“If you don’t have enough time, just don’t sleep.”

“...”

“War usually goes on for ten days without sleep. Perfect timing. Let’s get some experience now. I was thinking to stop at midnight today, but since it’s come to this, let’s add one more hour.”

Tang Pae suddenly realized something was terribly wrong. All the Green Forest bandits and Tang family people around glared at him as if to kill. He had no choice but to bow his head deeply to avoid their murderous stares.

“Alright, let’s start! You have to get stronger for me to survive! So grit your teeth!”

The Mount Hua fire across the Central Plains was progressing steadily.

Chapter 1104: If The Reward Is Like This, It Is Worth Receiving (4)

Thud.

Tang Pae collapsed right there and fell face down.

“Ugh...”

He was so exhausted that breathing properly was difficult. Dust rushed in through his nose, but he didn’t even have the strength to turn his head to the side.

“Tsk.”

Soon, Chung Myung clicked his tongue in deep displeasure, the sound brushing past his ears.

“So weak and frail...”

That voice seemed to pierce both his ears and body.

“What? You say poisons and secret techniques must be handled delicately?”

“...”

“Nonsense you’re spouting. Poison flies on its own, and secret techniques dance by themselves if left alone? In the end, poisons and secret techniques are used by people. How can those whose hands tremble from lack of stamina use secret techniques like assassination?”

“Ugh...”

“It’s not that delicate martial arts don’t require stamina training; the more delicate the martial art, the better the stamina must be. Tang family’s martial arts can be completely ruined by the slightest tremor. Even I, watching from outside, know this—so why don’t you who use those martial arts understand it?”

Chung Myung looked around at those who had fallen and then swiftly turned his body.

“When the sun rises tomorrow, we start again. Anyone who shows up late can expect to die.”

Tang Pae glanced briefly at Chung Myung’s retreating back as he shuffled away, then buried his face in the ground again. A worried voice came from beside him.

“...Hyung.”

“...”

“Hyung... are you okay?”

“...Do you think I am?”

“...”

“Ugh...”

Tang Pae painfully moved his legs and returned to the lodging.

He wanted to just sleep right there, covered in dirt, without washing his face or anything. The only thing that barely stopped that impulse was the responsibility of being the head of the Tang family.

No matter how hard it was, a person like the head of the Sichuan Tang family couldn’t just lie down and sleep on the training ground floor, right?

“...I’m dying, dying.”

“Me too.”

“You startled me!”

Tang Pae shouted in surprise. Then he saw Tang Zhan lying on the bed. Tang Pae grabbed his pounding chest and scolded.

“What are you doing? Without saying a word in someone else’s quarters!”

“Ugh.”

Tang Zhan struggled to get up. His arm supporting the bed trembled, showing he was also quite exhausted from the training.

“It’s nothing else.”

Tang Zhan began and furrowed his brows sharply.

“The younger siblings and uncles have quite a lot of complaints.”

“...”

“It seems everyone finds it hard to speak directly in front of the young lord, but lately, whenever there’s time, complaints pour out behind the scenes.”

“Hmm...”

Tang Pae sighed deeply.

“What kind of complaints?”

“Well... we know this training is necessary, but the appearance is just too bad, isn’t it?”

At Tang Zhan’s words, Tang Pae silently frowned. He understood what Tang Zhan meant. Even necessary training must have proper form. For the Sichuan Tang family members to be rolling on the floor under the orders of Mount Hua’s third class disciple certainly doesn’t look good.

“Every time passersby whisper, their pride is hurt a lot. Especially the uncles.”

“Hmm.”

“So, about that...”

Tang Zhan glanced cautiously at Tang Pae. Judging by his furtive looks, Tang Zhan hadn’t come here entirely by his own will. Surely, the uncles had pushed him.

“Could you please tell the lord?”

“To the lord?”

“Yes.”

Tang Zhan nodded.

“Most of the training Taoist Chung Myung is conducting can be done by the lord as well. Of course, we understand the family lord is busy with official duties, but still...”

“Impossible.”

Before Tang Zhan could finish, Tang Pae cut him off. As if there was no room for discussion.

“It’s not even worth mentioning. Never bring up such talk in front of me again.”

“Hyung.”

“Even if I say don’t speak.”

“But...”

Tang Pae firmly shook his head.

Actually, forbidding mention of certain matters isn’t the role of the lord of the Tang family. Listening to words that cannot be said to the solemn family head and conveying them is also part of the head’s duties.

But in this matter, he had no intention to yield.

“Are you doubting our father’s ability now?”

“N-no way. I just...”

“So, do you think father is just watching, unable to understand the family’s complaints?”

“That is...”

Tang Pae’s eyes narrowed.

“There is a suitable person for every matter. If father thinks Mount Hua’s Sword Saint is the right one for this, the family members should simply follow that.”

Tang Zhan silently nodded. To argue would be to challenge the family lord’s authority. He didn’t know about elsewhere, but in Sichuan Tang family, it was unthinkable.

Though he had softened since meeting Chung Myung of Mount Hua sect, the Poison King Tang Gunak was originally a symbol of absolute authority within the Tang family. Especially after dissolving the elders’ council and taking full control, no one dared deny his authority.

But even as he nodded, Tang Jan’s expression clearly showed he was still unconvinced.

Seeing that, Tang Pae tightened his lips slightly.

“Zhan, you don’t understand.”

“It’s not that... hyung.”

“Is that petty pride really that important to you?”

“...Hyung?”

Tang Pae looked at Tang Zhan with cold eyes.

“Does being criticized in front of people and rolling on the dirt floor damage the family’s honor? Does running around under the orders of Mount Hua’s third class disciple embarrass you?”

“...I...”

“You’ve got fat on your belly.”

Tang Zhan closed his mouth. Tang Pae’s cold gaze rendered him immobile.

“Pride isn’t protected by wearing fine clothes and acting arrogantly. You feel shame when your clothes get dirty and you look ridiculous, but why don’t you feel humiliation when Mount Hua, once not even considered a rival, surpasses us?”

Tang Zhan could not answer.

“Our family is Mount Hua’s foremost ally.”

“Yes, hyung. I know. So...”

“But on this trip to the Southern lands, the Tang family did not accompany. It was Namgung and Green Forest who stayed by Mount Hua’s side, not us. Do you understand what that means?”

“...”

“Of course, the family lord could have gone personally. But... do you really think it was right for the family lord to assist that Mount Hua’s Sword Saint and head to the Southern Lands? Really?”

“N-no!”

“Right. That must never happen. Then I’ll ask. Why did the Tang family fail to participate in this Southern land’s trip, more important than anything else to block the Demonic Cult and protect the Central Plains?”

“Well...”

Again, Tang Zhan could not answer. He knew the answer but lacked the courage to say it. But Tang Pae spoke firmly, as if pouring salt on the wound.

“Because we are weak, you and I.”

“...”

“Isn’t that so?”

“...Yes.”

Tang Zhan bowed his head deeply.

Of course, the elders of the Tang family could have stepped forward. But the talent Mount Hua’s Sword Saint wants is not just someone strong. It’s someone who can coordinate with their hands and feet.

If Tang Pae or Tang Zhan were as strong as Baek Cheon, would Chung Myung have really left them behind and headed to Gangnam? No.

Both Tang Pae and Tang Zhan already felt it. They were not trusted, so they couldn’t go to Southern Land together.

“After enduring that humiliation... now you say your pride is hurt? Is that something the direct line of the Sichuan Tang family dares to say?”

His voice was full of fury. Tang Zhan dared not raise his bowed head. The anger in the voice was fierce, and he could not refute a word.

“Namgung’s lord, Namgung Do-Wei, recently lost his father but still bore the Namgung name and headed to the Southern Land. He already represents the Namgung name. But...”

A bitter laugh escaped Tang Pae’s lips.

“My younger brother, of the same age, is complaining about being embarrassed and losing face rolling on the dirt floor in front of people. Where does this difference come from?”

“Hyung..”

“Today Namgung underwent the same training as us. But they finished much earlier and returned in good shape. You saw that, right?”

“...Yes.”

“Even after seeing that, those words came easily from your mouth.”

Tang Pae’s cold voice now pierced Tang Zhan’s heart. It was rare to see Tang Pae so furious.

“Is your pride hurt?”

“...”

“My pride is hurt too. But not because of trivial face. Because I have clearly seen with my own eyes that the great Sichuan Tang family and its warriors are falling behind other sects.”

Tang Pae gritted his teeth.

“Is there anything more shameful to a warrior than being weak?”

“But, hyung. We use different martial arts from them... we...”

“Shut your mouth.”

A chill was in Tang Pae’s voice.

“There’s nothing wrong with what the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint says. We’ve always used that excuse to not train. If lacking skill, develop stronger poisons and deadlier secret techniques. So, has our Tang family ever once reached the top in the world with that mindset?”

Tang Zhan was speechless.

“If you can’t learn anything from those in front of you, it’s already over. You don’t have to come to training from tomorrow. No, tell everyone who skips training to stay away. I’ll take responsibility.”

“Hyung.”

Tang Pae spoke firmly.

“But I will follow this training to the end. Rolling on the floor is nothing. I don’t care if I have to shove mud in my mouth. I will definitely become strong. Someday, when I become the family lord of the Tang family, I will never hear that I am weaker than Namgung’s lord or Baek Cheon of Mount Hua, even if it kills me.”

“...”

“Tell them that. But!”

Tang Pae frowned deeply and glared at Tang Zhan. His face seemed to show a glimpse of Tang Gunak.

“If anyone speaks ill of the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, who took on hardship doing what they all didn’t have to, tell them that I, the young lord, will punish them directly without the family lord’s intervention. If someone with the Tang name acts like an ungrateful beast, they will be treated like one.”

“...”

“Do you understand?”

“...I will keep that in mind.”

“Go.”

“...”

“Go now!”

Tang Zhan bowed deeply and left. Tang Pae sighed as soon as the door closed.

It might have been a bit harsh, but it couldn’t be helped. It was time for Tang Zhan and others to face reality.

“...It’s not easy.”

Another long sigh escaped his lips.

“...Hmm.”

A low sound came from Chung Myung lying on the eaves.

“Tsk.”

He raised a bottle of liquor with a face that showed annoyance at everything. Unfortunately, it had long been empty, and no drop fell even when shaken.

“Tsk.”

Just as he was about to get up, grimacing at the bitter taste.

“Here it is.”

“Huh?”

At the sudden voice, Chung Myung turned his head. Tang Gunak had appeared beside him, offering the bottle with a gentle smile.

“...What is this?”

“May I sit?”

“Of course.”

Tang Gunak perched beside Chung Myung.

“Let’s drink together. I want to drink a little today too.”

“If I drink with the family lord, I’m happy.”

“Is that so?”

Tang Gunak chuckled softly, took a sip from the bottle, then looked up at the night sky and spoke.

“You must be having a hard time too.”

At the unexpected words, Chung Myung quietly looked at Tang Gunak.

Chapter 1105: If The Reward Is Like This, It Is Worth Receiving (5)

Chung Myung asked Tang Gunak.

“What is it?”

“Well.”

Tang Gunak smiled mysteriously instead of answering directly.

Chung Myung frowned for a moment. Not because he was upset, but because he saw in that face the expression of a certain person who used to smirk at him.

‘Blood is blood, huh?’

That’s why families are frightening.

Unlike sects where different people carry on the same will, families are connected by blood. That’s why sometimes you can see the faces of past people in them.

“It’s not particularly difficult, though?”

“If so, that’s fortunate...”

Tang Gunak trailed off and glanced at the bottle of liquor in his hand.

“It doesn’t look that way to me.”

“...”

“In fact, from your perspective now, my son is just a small fry not worth worrying about.”

He smiled gently and quietly studied Chung Myung’s somewhat gruff expression.

“But even after exhausting all your strength by pushing and teaching people all day, you still came here to check on the situation, didn’t you?”

“I was just looking for a good place to drink.”

“Is this the place?”

Tang Gunak looked around the empty eaves.

“...Your taste is quite unique.”

“I hear that sometimes.”

Chung Myung smiled bitterly, brushing it off. That’s why you shouldn’t take people from the Tang family lightly—they casually hit the core points.

Of course, that man was an oddball even among the Tang family.

“You’ve done well enough.”

At that sudden remark, Chung Myung glanced at Tang Gunak.

“You’ve grown Mount Hua this much, created the Heavenly Friends Alliance, ultimately stopped the Four Sects Alliance, and even blocked the rise of the Demonic cult. Your achievements alone are enough to call you a hero. If only the fools of the Nine Great Sects hadn’t despised you, your fame would be ringing throughout the world by now.”

“...”

“Sometimes when I look at you, I feel such self-doubt about what kind of person I am.”

“Ah, don’t say that.”

“What can you do if that’s the truth?”

Tang Gunak gave a bitter smile.

“Let’s have a drink.”

“Yes.”

The two lightly clinked their bottles and drank at the same time.

“Ahh.”

Chung Myung pulled away the bottle, wiped his mouth, and smirked.

“Come to think of it, isn’t it rare for the Lord, the Tang Gunak to drink straight from the bottle?”

“...Unless I’m drinking with you, that rarely happens.”

Tang Gunak looked at the bottle in his hand with a strange gaze.

“But it doesn’t feel bad.”

“Then that’s good enough.”

“Exactly.”

Tang Gunak slowly nodded. After quietly handling the bottle, he spoke again.

“Yes, it’s impressive. Even with what you’ve done so far. But...”

He soon shook his head.

“You’re trying to do more than that.”

“...”

“Sometimes I worry that you forget one thing.”

“...What?”

Tang Gunak turned his head and stared intently at Chung Myung.

“That you are also human.”

“No, I’m not some fool.”

“Right. You’re not a fool. So of course you know.”

In the quiet night, the sound of liquor sloshing in the bottle was unusually loud.

“But strangely, it’s not fools who usually forget that fact. Rather, overly smart people tend to forget it.”

Chung Myung did not respond.

“Well, there are many reasons. They might believe they’re fine, or that no one else can do the job... Everyone has their reasons.”

“Hmm...”

“But no matter the reason, the fact remains that you’re overexerting yourself. Isn’t that so?”

As Chung Myung’s expression became strange, Tang Gunak smiled lightly.

“What are you thinking?”

“Well... I was thinking how similar words can feel very different depending on how they’re said.”

“Hmm?”

Chung Myung scratched his head.

Damn, if you don’t run, will those bastards all die? No, that’s not it! What does it matter if they all die or not? If the guys you usually curse to die are about to die, you should be clapping happily! So why are you, hyung, going to save them? Your injured arm and leg are barely hanging on to your body... Hey? Hey, you! Aren’t you stopping there? Hey!

Looking back, that guy was definitely crazy.

Fortunately, that bloodline did not continue. For the Tang family, it was a stroke of luck.

“When I think about it, it’s a bit funny.”

“Yes?”

“There’s no better candidate than you to train the Tang family. That’s something not even worth debating.”

Tang Gunak, while speaking, wore a bitter expression.

He knows his own limits and understands Chung Myung’s abilities. He knows this is the best for the family.

“Um... if that’s the case, would you do it yourself?”

“No. That’s not it.”

Tang Gunak burst out laughing as he glanced at Chung Myung cautiously. He thought how much this person had changed. When he first saw him in Sichuan, he wondered how someone could be so arrogant.

“It’s not about wounded pride but bitterness. We don’t have much time. So we have to proceed most efficiently. The problem is... the person who creates that efficiency can only be you, and as you pursue efficiency one by one, eventually all the work will fall on you.”

“...”

“That’s not a good direction. Isn’t that so?”

Chung Myung, listening, soon chuckled. Though the way of speaking was different, naggers are naggers.

“The reason you’re now trying to teach them is...”

“Mount Hua alone has its limits. So the entire Heavenly Friends Alliance must become stronger. But the guy doing that doesn’t even consider his own limits and tries to take all the work alone. Foolishly.”

“...”

“That’s what you’re trying to say, right?”

Tang Gunak smiled gently.

“Let’s leave out the ‘foolishly.’”

“Ah, you have a bad temper.”

Chung Myung laughed and shrugged his shoulders.

“No need to worry. I’m not that foolish. I was just realizing that.”

“What do you mean?”

“A relationship where only one side gives unilaterally can’t be called friendship.”

“...”

“It’s an obvious fact.”

Tang Gunak nodded.

“You know it well.”

“I was just about to make a request.”

“To me?”

“Yes.”

Tang Gunak looked at Chung Myung with an interested expression.

The reason he came here was because he wanted to share some of Chung Myung’s burdens. Until now, Chung Myung had taken on too many tasks alone. He hadn’t even asked Hyun Jong or Tang Gunak for help.

Tang Gunak came to say that it shouldn’t continue like this. But Chung Myung quickly said he had a request.

“I’m curious.”

Whether he really has something prepared or is just making it up will be clear when Chung Myung speaks.

“What is it?”

At that moment, Chung Myung’s lips curled up wickedly.

“Surely, it’s not easy for the family lord to teach the Tang family. After all, it will be repeating what has been done every day.”

“That’s right.”

“But... if you change your thinking a little, the family lord can easily become a devil.”

“Hmm?”

Chung Myung began to share his thoughts. Tang Gunak’s mouth began to resemble Tang Bo’s in a strange way as he listened.

Leaving the room, Jo Gul stretched widely and looked at the sun just beginning to rise.

“It’s good.”

A whistle came naturally. As he moved energetically, a bored voice came from behind him.

“Why are you in such a good mood so early in the morning?”

“Ah, sahyung!”

Jo Gul turned to Yoon Jong happily and chuckled.

“I’ve been feeling so refreshed lately.”

Yoon Jong was about to say something but shook his head. Actually, he was feeling a bit comfortable these days.

“Because that Chung Myung guy is busy with other things, he can’t bother with us, right? Hahaha.”

“Is that something to laugh about?”

“It’s not really funny. But what can I do if I’m in a good mood?”

Yoon Jong shook his head as if he couldn’t help it.

In fact, even in Mount Hua, the one who suffers the most from Chung Myung’s torment... no, intense training is none other than the Five Swords.

Even when training with other Mount Hua disciples, the Five Swords trained at several times the intensity. How many times has he been fed up with this special “affectionate” treatment that no one envies?

But recently, that Chung Myung guy has been paying attention to the Green Forest and the Tang family, so he hasn’t been able to properly torment them. Thanks to that, Five Swords, who only needs to train as much as the others, has energy to spare.

“Ahh. How great it would have been if it had been like this from the start.”

“It’s not a good thing.”

“I know. I know. You have to train hard to get stronger. But sahyung, shouldn’t there be days like this in life?”

“...That’s not entirely wrong.”

Yoon Jong also ended up smiling slightly.

Jo Gul and Yoon Jong both know well that this situation won’t last long.

Chung Myung’s persistence surpasses common sense. He won’t keep watching the Five Swords slack off. So it’s better to rest comfortably while he can.

Arriving at the training ground, Jo Gul whistled as he saw his sahyungs already out.

“Wow. Everyone is full of energy as expected.”

Thinking of the Green Forest, Tang family, and Namgung Family people who are sacrificed for this, tears come to his eyes... but what can you do? People need times like this too.

Jo Gul greeted Baek Cheon brightly.

“Sasuk, are you out?”

“You’re late!”

“...Ah. Even that Chung Myung guy isn’t out yet.”

Watching Jo Gul’s banter, Baek Cheon sighed deeply. That guy is like a high-energy puppy; if he doesn’t train enough to burn off energy, he becomes uncontrollable.

“Sasuk, your face looks good too?”

“Ahem...”

“On the other hand...”

Jo Geol glanced at those standing on the other side. Green Forest and the Tang family looked completely pale and worn out. The Namgung Family was slightly better, but only marginally; they looked equally exhausted.

“Poor things...”

“Cut it out, Gul. What if that Chung Myung guy starts picking on only us again?”

“Is that a problem? We’ve always been the ones getting hit.”

“Now that you mention it, that’s true.”

“Stop scaring me. I just want to live and die today. Can’t I just say it’s good? Hehehe.”

Baek Cheon gave up and shook his head. He wasn’t someone you could reason with.

It was then.

“Hey, Chung Myung.”

Yu Yiseol said quietly. Baek Cheon turned that way and saw Chung Myung approaching with an expression of utter annoyance.

“Hmm?”

It wasn’t surprising. Chung Myung always looked like that. Baek Cheon was curious because another person was with him.

“Who is that?”

“I think it’s the lord of the Tang family.”

The Mount Hua disciples frowned.

Tang Zhan looked at Tang Pae standing beside him. His gaze asked if the family lord had been informed, but Tang Pae shook his head as if he didn’t know.

“Good morning.”

Tang Gunak arrived at the training ground, greeted lightly, and smiled.

“From today, I will also participate in training, so please take care of me.”

“...Is he going to teach?”

“Then will he learn from us, you bastard?”

Yoon Jong immediately cursed at Jo Geol, who was talking nonsense.

Chung Myung looked at Tang Gunak and said.

“Then please take good care of me.”

“Leave it to me.”

Tang Gunak exchanged smiles with him and strode to stand before the Mount Hua disciples.

Baek Cheon looked at Tang Gunak with a puzzled face and spoke calmly.

“Lord of the Tang family, the Tang family is over there...”

“This is the right place.”

“...Yes?”

Tang Gunak gave a strange smile.

“Do you know why I don’t teach the Tang family directly?”

“I don’t know... that’s...”

“Of course, there are many reasons. One of the biggest is that the Tang family’s martial arts focus on poison and secret techniques. Poison can be neutralized by antidotes, but secret techniques, if misused, can cause irreversible consequences.”

“I see.”

Once secret techniques are unleashed, they don’t return. Misjudging the opponent’s ability even slightly can be fatal.

“So I chose to observe as much as possible. But... Mount Hua’s Sword Saint told me.”

“...What did he say?”

“You all have spent three years learning how to survive enemy attacks, right? So now, even using secret techniques barely leaves a scratch?”

“...”

...What? That’s news to me?

“That’s good, very good for me too. Besides... I also find it hard to get chances to use secret techniques on people.”

Tang Gunak smiled gently and pulled out from his sleeve four blue-glowing daggers.

“If it’s you, I can throw them comfortably. It will be quite helpful to each other.”

“...I don’t think that’s a good idea.”

“I trust you all.”

“You shouldn’t trust us in this thing.”

“Well then, let’s begin!”

“No, please listen! Family lord!”

“Hahaha! Enough whining!”

“Listen up!”

A bright smile spread across Tang Gunak’s face. Chung Myung said it resembled Tang Bo’s smile, but in fact, it was more like his own.

The daggers fiercely sliced through the air.

Chapter 1106: Sometimes He Gives Me Goosebumps (1)

“Alright! That’s it for today!”

Thud! Thud!

As soon as Chung Myung’s voice rang out, the disciples of Mount Hua, including Hae Yeon, collapsed like bundles of straw. The relaxed demeanor they had when arriving at the training ground early that morning was nowhere to be seen.

“Hmm, already?”

“It’s almost midnight now.”

“Hmm.”

Tang Gunak slowly retrieved his daggers, seeming reluctant to stop.

“I was just starting to warm up. Back in my day, once I got going, I’d throw daggers day and night for three days straight... Why stop at a moment like this?”

“Don’t worry. We’ll start again exactly four hours from now.”

At those words, the Mount Hua disciples who had collapsed like corpses suddenly lifted their heads like lightning.

“F-four hours?”

“It was originally six hours, you bastard! Why the change of words now?”

“Oh, was it six?”

Chung Myung clicked his tongue.

“But are these guys full-bellied pampered kids? It’s none other than the great Lord Tang himself training them, and yet they don’t even try to secure a tiny bit more of that precious time!”

“Ugh...”

Honestly, that wasn’t wrong. In fact, it was a rare, very rare correct statement coming from that brat’s clique.

It was none other than Tang Gunak. The Poison King Tang Gunak. He is one of the absolute warriors ruling the current murim world.

Such a person was personally conducting training close to real combat all day long, not just simple sparring or dueling.

“If others heard this, they’d bring millions of coins and beg to spar even once with that man! These guys are living in luxury!”

“Ughhh.”

It’s true... but still...

“...Forget millions, we have to survive first.”

“What’s the use of improving skills if we die?”

“Save me.”

“Amitabha... I see paradise...”

“That’s... hell, monk. You won’t reach paradise.”

The Five Swords shed sad tears.

Is Tang Gunak’s dagger technique truly merciless? No, not to that extent. If he had truly intended to attack seriously and Five Swords had to block all day, how many of them would still be alive?

Naturally, Tang Gunak was holding back out of consideration for their skill. Moreover, they had trained in real combat-like sparring with Chung Myung, who was equal or perhaps superior to Tang Gunak, for three years.

‘Yeah, I know that.’

Baek Cheon shivered. Even knowing all that, the goosebumps on his back showed no sign of fading.

The problem lay in the difference between the sword and the dagger.

They had absolute trust in Chung Myung’s sword. No matter how fiercely he swung it at their necks, they had the ultimate belief that the sword would never truly pierce their throats.

Without that trust, they could never engage in dagger sparring where lethal moves are exchanged. Their absolute confidence in Chung Myung’s skill allowed them to attack and defend with all their might.

But the problem is that daggers are not swords.

No matter how much Tang Gunak is the Poison King of the world, and even if Chung Myung has unmatched skill in throwing and handling daggers, once a dagger leaves the hand, it’s a weapon that is out of control.

A momentary mistake, a simple miscalculation, and that dagger might lodge into the leader.

‘I never thought it would be this chilling.’

Every time a dagger filled with fierce qi brushed past their faces, it felt as if their souls briefly left their bodies and returned.

If anyone wants to experience a near-death experience, just stand them here.

But they repeated this all day from morning till midnight. Who could remain sane?

“Poor things.”

“...Better just to move the body.”

“As expected of Mount Hua folks. We can’t keep up with that.”

You could tell how extreme their experience was just by the reactions of the nearby Green Forest and Tang family. Those who had long looked at Mount Hua with envious eyes now clicked their tongues as if seeing the most pitiful people in the world. Even a Beggars’ Union beggar starved for three days wouldn’t be looked at that way.

“How is it?”

At Chung Myung’s question, Tang Gunak gave a strange smile.

“There’s definitely a difference from throwing daggers alone. I felt this when we fought at Plum Blossom Island before.”

“Right?”

Tang Gunak nodded.

“I started to help, but it seems I’ll be the one receiving help. If we repeat training for just half a month, I think we’ll grasp something.”

“H-half a month?”

“You mean to do this for half a month more?”

“You’re joking, right?”

The Five Swords had a fit.

Even now, their clothes were full of holes as if pierced by needles, and their bodies covered with scratches... They had to do this for half a month more? Half a month?

“And... I think I know what the Tang family lacked. Also the solution.”

Tang Gunak looked alternately at Mount Hua and the Tang family with meaningful eyes. Like discovering an interesting toy, the disciples of Mount Hua and the Tang family members shivered simultaneously. It was strangely chilling.

“The combination is good.”

The most fatal weakness of the Tang family originates from the fundamentals of their martial arts.

Other sects swing swords and spar repeatedly to compensate for weaknesses, but the Tang family cannot do that. Most of the secret techniques they use cannot have substitutes like wooden swords used in sparring to reduce power.

What if you carve feather-light feather needles from wood instead of metal? If made the same, they’re too light to hold qi; if you increase size to add weight, the secret technique’s characteristics disappear, making it meaningless.

Moreover, no matter how blunt the tip is made, an assassination technique is the same. A slight mistake piercing the eye risks blindness or even death.

So the Tang family can train by releasing and controlling secret techniques in the air, but they have extremely little experience truly using them against opponents. This is a natural limitation.

But...

“Anyway, you all blocked my daggers.”

“...Us?”

“We never did.”

“...I was just standing there.”

Tang Gunak smiled faintly.

“Mount Hua disciples are certainly humble. Maybe because they resemble the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint?”

“...Is that an insult?”

“Lord, that’s crossing the line. Apologize to us.”

“That brat being humble? Humble?”

Tang Gunak nodded approvingly at the Mount Hua disciples having fits.

“Still energetic. Truly reliable.”

Though joking, Tang Gunak was actually quite surprised.

‘I didn’t expect it to come this far.’

No matter how much they trained with Chung Myung, he had the fixed idea that early disciples are early disciples. So he thought they wouldn’t use even 30% of their true power.

But their skill easily surpassed his expectations. Though not showing it, he was quite tired.

‘I almost had to use lethal moves.’

No matter how many daggers he threw, they blocked and parried desperately while advancing forward, sending chills down his spine. What kind of training had these young men repeated to reach this level?

They didn’t just defend and endure; they advanced without being deceived by the dagger’s changing movements.

“In other words...”

“Yes?”

Tang Gunak’s smile deepened.

“If they can block my daggers, then the secret techniques my kids throw won’t be much of a threat to you all.”

So it’s possible.

Just as he throws daggers freely at them to test himself, the Tang family members get perfect targets that hardly get hurt no matter how much they attack.

“Mount Hua’s Sword Saint”

“Yes?”

“As long as skills improve, anything is good, right?”

“...”

“Right?”

Under that subtle pressure, Chung Myung glanced at the Mount Hua disciples.

Their eyes seemed to desperately plead, “We don’t care what you want to say, just say no!” Chung Myung nodded reassuringly.

Then he looked at Tang Gunak and said.

“Of course.”

“Hey, you bastard!”

“You rotten bastard! Selling your sahyung?”

“I’ll curse you even in hell! I’ll definitely kill you!”

Chung Myung rubbed his ears.

“Dogs bark like this under the moonlight.”

The Mount Hua disciples glared at Chung Myung with fierce eyes, but it didn’t matter much. No matter what, you can’t kill someone with just a look.

“Alright, let’s go.”

“Sure.”

“How about it? You still seem lively. A drink?”

“Hmm. Since you ask, I can’t refuse. But drink moderately today. I’ll choose the liquor.”

“A bit disappointing, but alright.”

The Mount Hua disciples, watching the two walk away chatting, soon dropped their heads.

“...Yoon Jong.”

“...Yes?”

“Sew that bastard’s mouth shut.”

“Soso went to get needles a moment ago.”

“Good. Quick.”

Baek Cheon staggered and barely sat up.

“...I never thought daggers could be this terrifying.”

“Indeed. I’m truly feeling it to the bone now.”

“First of all, it’s scary that they’re not held in the hand.”

“Exactly.”

Those they had faced so far all wielded weapons or fists directly with their hands. That means as long as they didn’t lose their grip, they could somehow handle them.

But Tang Gunak was completely different.

He was just standing, yet the daggers leaving his hands flew at terrifying speeds, toying with them.

“...No restrictions.”

“Yes. That’s right, sasuk.”

Baek Cheon nodded heavily.

No matter how free a swordsman wants to be, they can’t escape the limitation of holding a sword in their hand. So the sword’s movements are confined by the body’s limits.

But a dagger that leaves the hand has no such limits. That’s why it causes unimaginable changes.

This was another shock to them. Having always lived holding a sword, they never once thought the sword was confined by the body’s limits.

“...There’s so much to learn.”

“The progress is too fast, sasuk.”

“But... one thing is certain...”

Baek Cheon looked at Tang Gunak’s now distant back and continued.

“If we perfectly internalize this, Mount Hua will become stronger by one step. It’s an opportunity no martial artist can miss. I am infinitely pleased.”

A radiant light flowed from his composed eyes.

But those who heard his words were indifferent.

“That’s only if we don’t die first.”

Jo Gul grumbled.

“We could die as soon as tomorrow... What a frozen shit opportunity.”

“...”

“Let’s just go sleep. I’m exhausted.”

“Yeah, let’s go.”

“I’m hungry too.”

“We need to eat something.”

One by one, they shook off their places and headed to the lodging, leaving Baek Cheon behind.

“Anyway, that’s a sickness too.”

“Leave him be. He’s our sasuk.”

“Taoist Baek Cheon sometimes takes things too seriously. That’s also an obsession.”

“Sometimes he just gives me goosebumps. Sigh.”

Left behind, Baek Cheon blankly watched their retreating backs.

“Hey... guys?”

A cold wind from the riverbank swept past Baek Cheon.

He shivered and quietly stood up.

“Ahem.”

He lightly cleared his throat and awkwardly followed the others.

“Let’s go together, guys. Guys? Hey?”

Baek Ah, who had finished her own training and was watching from behind, sighed deeply and shook her head.

Chapter 1107: Sometimes He Gives Me Goosebumps (2)

Tang Pae stared blankly at his own hand, the empty chopsticks twitching twelve times per second in the air.

“Heh heh...”

His eyes were half-dead, the light within them extinguished.

Looking around, he saw his brothers who had given up trying to pick up food with their hands, burying their faces in their bowls.

They couldn’t endure without eating, and since it was impossible to scoop rice with their hands, they had thrown away all pride and were just somehow forcing food into their mouths.

“Stamina...”

Tang Pae perfectly understood at this moment why Chung Myung said stamina was essential for the Tang family’s martial arts.

How could one possibly perform any secret techniques with hands that couldn’t even scoop rice?

It wasn’t wrong to say the Tang family’s martial arts require precision, but that precision could only be demonstrated on a foundation of unwavering stamina in any situation.

What good is precision if one can’t even control their own hands?

“I know that...”

He remembered what Baek Cheon once told him.

Tang Pae had once asked curiously why Baek Cheon cursed whenever Chung Myung spoke, even though Chung Myung seemed to be right. Baek Cheon had answered clearly:

“...You’ll understand when you experience it later.”

Yes. Now he understood. He understood painfully well.

Because they spoke the truth, there was no room to rebel. But if he just shut up and obeyed, he was as good as dead.

The problem was if he resisted here, he would become the only one seen as weak and lazy. Who would have imagined that pushing with justification could be so terrifying?

“Ugh...”

“Damn it...”

Endless groans escaped from the mouths of the Tang family people.

“No wonder Mount Hua got so strong. With training like this, even a rabbit would take only a few years to beat a tiger.”

“Hy-Hyung. Are we really not going to die from this?”

“Don’t worry. You won’t die.”

“No, how do you know?”

“Look over there.”

“Yes?”

“They’re not dead, are they?”

Tang Zhan looked up and followed Tang Pae’s finger. There, the Mount Hua people, their faces darkened as if dead, were half-slumped in chairs, staring blankly at the food before them.

“...Why aren’t they eating?”

“They threw up all the breakfast they ate.”

“...”

“I didn’t know either. I never thought the family lord would get so excited and rampage like that on them.”

“I’ve never seen such a sight in my life.”

“It’s really fortunate.”

“Yes? What is?”

“That we saw such a sight for the first time... it’s better not to have seen it at all.”

Tang Gunak found water.

He seemed like a restless spirit who died bitterly because he couldn’t throw his throwing knives; whenever he saw Mount Hua people, he threw knives at them as a greeting. Of course, those receiving the knives didn’t take it as a greeting.

Mount Hua disciples, threatened dozens of times a day, were visibly drying up before their eyes.

“That’s what they mean by life force draining from the body.”

Tang Pae put down his chopsticks and sighed.

“Still, aren’t we in a slightly better position? So don’t complain.”

“...Didn’t you hear?”

“What?”

“...After the morning training, Chung Myung said, ‘The basics are slowly set. Now let’s raise the intensity and do it properly.’”

“That crazy bastard... cough cough!”

Tang Pae quickly cleared his throat, cutting off the words about to escape his mouth.

‘No. What the...’

At this point, wasn’t it a competition between Chung Myung and Tang Gunak over who torments the opposing sect’s disciples better? The innocent shrimps’ backs were the ones getting broken in this whale fight.

“...They say acting on what you know is the hardest thing in the world.”

Tang Pae sighed deeply and suddenly looked warmly at the Mount Hua disciples.

“Still, I’m really glad those people are here.”

“What?”

“...Imagine if only the Tang family suffered like this. By now, complaints would have multiplied tenfold, wouldn’t they?”

“That... is true, isn’t it?”

Tang Zhan nodded.

No need to guess. Until a few days ago, voices condemning Mount Hua and Chung Myung never ceased, but after Tang Gunak started directly handling Mount Hua disciples, those words disappeared.

And instead of subtle hostility, a sense of comradeship began to form.

After all, they were comrades suffering the same pain, and they had been undergoing this training all along, hadn’t they?

“If the burden is shared, it becomes a little lighter.”

“...Isn’t it just that the burden has increased?”

“Let’s just agree to what I said.”

Tang Pae looked at the Mount Hua disciples with a strange gaze.

‘Did the family lord and Taoist Chung Myung consider all this?’

Thinking about it, the closeness between the Tang family and Mount Hua was almost an illusion.

In reality, the close relationships were between Hyun Jong and Tang Gunak, and between Chung Myung and Tang Gunak.

Since they represented Mount Hua and the Tang family, it wasn’t entirely wrong to say the sects were close, but the members themselves had little interaction.

They weren’t hostile, but they weren’t close enough for private conversations.

‘In other words, if the current leadership steps down, the relationship between Mount Hua and the Tang family won’t be the same.’

Tang Pae would try to maintain relations with Mount Hua, but no matter how he thought about it, he couldn’t become a king-like family lord like Tang Gunak. So he would inevitably have to listen to others’ opinions.

But if situations like this repeated, a comradeship might arise between Mount Hua and the Tang family, and not only the leadership but members might maintain good relations.

‘Is that why those two volunteer to be the villains?’

Perhaps the leadership was drawing a bigger picture he couldn’t see. Thinking so, Tang Pae nodded unconsciously.

Unaware of Tang Pae’s thoughts, Tang Zhan said with a gloomy face.

“...What about the afternoon training?”

“Don’t complain. Don’t we get time to sleep?”

“Sleeping doesn’t fully recover fatigue. These days, even with qi cultivation, my body doesn’t feel lighter. It feels like carrying a lump of iron on my shoulders.”

Hearing this, Tang Pae nodded and looked at Tang Zhan.

“That’s the key.”

“...Eh?”

“From now on, we must endure not short battles but the war. You understand that, right?”

“Yes.”

Tang Zhan nodded.

“War doesn’t end overnight. Everyone felt that during the Plum Blossom Island incident. It might be a few days or several months of continuous fighting without proper rest or sleep.”

“...”

“Look.”

Tang Zhan turned his head at Tang Pae’s words. The Tang family people were still unable to eat properly and were dying.

“There too.”

Following Tang Pae’s words, Tang Zhan’s eyes widened slightly. The Mount Hua people who had been lying like corpses moments ago were now regaining energy and stuffing food into their mouths.

“Do you understand what would happen if we were sent to war in this state?”

“...Yes, hyung. I probably wouldn’t be able to show even a quarter of my ability.”

“That’s also wrong.”

“Yes?”

“Ability that can’t be demonstrated isn’t ability at all. It’s just an excuse. Only the strength that can be fully used in real combat can be called ability.”

“...”

“If you think that way, everything changes. The public opinion that the Tang family is stronger than Mount Hua is also wrong. Right now, we’re worse than them.”

“H-Hyung. That’s...”

Tang Pae looked back at Tang Zhan with a cold gaze.

“Why? Do you also want to ride on the public’s superficial evaluation to raise yourself?”

“...”

“Even after seeing the Namgung Family, once considered stronger than us, suffer so miserably?”

“That’s not the case, hyung.”

Tang Pae nodded.
