“In my view, Chung Myung and the family lord don’t act based on just one thing. They must be considering at least three or four factors. Though it’s tough now, if we trust and follow them, a moment of light will surely come. Surely they wouldn’t torment us intentionally.”

“...That’s true too.”

“Right. So eat. To endure, you must eat.”

Tang Pae somehow grabbed the food bowl and began stuffing food into his mouth.

But at that moment, Tang Pae didn’t know.

No matter how good the intention, or how benevolent the act, the method can be more terrible than acts done with malice.

“...What did you say?”

“You don’t understand this?”

Chung Myung smiled brightly.

Though it was a refreshingly bright expression to anyone, to Tang Pae’s eyes, that smile didn’t look good. Rather, it looked like the grin of a demon risen from hell.

“You’re tired too.”

“...Yes.”

“You want to rest, right?”

“Well...”

“So I’m giving you a chance to rest.”

Tang Pae was momentarily speechless.

No, Taoist? I’m not an idiot; of course I understood that.

“But the method is...”

“Kuh. Yes. That’s the key.”

Chung Myung snapped his fingers sharply.

“Rest is good. Very good. But people are like that; they don’t value things just handed to them easily.”

“What kind of bull... no, what do you mean?”

“I’m saying I’ll make you feel that value.”

Chung Myung smirked.

“You’re proud of your skills as Tang family members, right? That you can beat anyone.”

“...Yes, but.”

Chung Myung nodded as if that was obvious.

“Right. But they feel the same way.”

Chung Myung gestured toward the Mount Hua disciples. Their eyes were filled with nothing but killing intent, nodding like hungry murderers.

“The murim world’s law is simple. The strong possess, and the proven obtain. So...”

Chung Myung snapped his fingers again.

“Those who win get to skip tomorrow morning’s training. Simple, right?”

“W-wait a minute. What is that?”

How could they be told to split into teams and fight among comrades who trained together? How could such an act be acceptable?

And if the Tang family and Mount Hua fought now, wouldn’t the budding comradeship be destroyed? This must not be done...

“Ah, that stingy bastard talks too much. Well, yeah. You’re not used to it yet. I understand. But the solution is really simple.”

Chung Myung smiled brightly.

“Once you get hit, you’ll snap out of it.”

“Yes?”

“The last one standing wins. Get ready.”

“Yes?”

“Begin.”

Chung Myung clapped his hands.

Tang Pae chuckled bitterly. Who would follow such nonsense...

“Aaaaaaah!”

At that moment, a desperate scream was heard. Tang Pae startled and turned his head sharply.

The Mount Hua disciples, who felt like blood brothers... no, Mount Hua converts, were glaring fiercely and charging madly.

“Kill them!”

It was the moment the alliance between Mount Hua and the Tang family shattered completely.

Chapter 1108: Sometimes He Gives Me Goosebumps (3)

Thud.

The knee that had held out until the very end finally gave way and touched the ground.

Blood flowed from the corner of the mouth, hands trembling uncontrollably. Eyes filled with hatred and resentment.

The mouth, heavy with all the hatred and grudge, slowly opened.

“Why... is it... like this...?”

“Heh heh heh.”

The handsome man in white, standing upright, looked down at the man kneeling before him with a chillingly expressionless face.

“Do not harbor too much resentment towards me.”

“...”

“Isn’t the murim world all like that?”

“Y-You...”

Before the voice filled with rage could fully come out, the curly-haired man with a sly expression standing beside the handsome man stepped forward with a twisted smile.

His eyes poured out contempt and mockery.

“This... why would someone who should know better act like this?”

Someone trusted.

Seeing the despicable face of someone trusted even more because they were from the same hometown felt like being stabbed in the chest with a sharp knife.

“Is there any word more futile than trust in this world? Heh heh heh. Consider it the price of your foolishness.”

“Fucking...”

The man slowly collapsed forward.

“Fucking... bastard...”

Thud.

The moment the last Tang family member fell, Mount Hua disciples shouted “Hooray!” like thunder.

“We won!”

“Tomorrow we get half a day off!”

“Mom! I beat the Tang sect!”

The Mount Hua disciples, guaranteed half a day of rest, hugged each other and jumped up and down in place cheering.

“Half a day! Half a day! Oh my!”

“It’s the first time I’ve had half a day off since Chung Myung came to the sect!”

“Tomorrow I’ll sleep in! I really won’t get up no matter what!”

“It’s drinking time! Tonight is drinking time!”

It was almost like they were going crazy with excitement.

The Tang sect members watched the scene with faces boiling with rage.

Among the Mount Hua disciples tumbling around like somersaults, a shining bald head stood out unusually.

‘...That one is also a monk.’

Of course, it’s not that the other Taoists did well, but still, a man who is supposed to be a monk beating people down and rejoicing about drinking—where on earth is this world going...?

Huh? ...Soso?

You shouldn’t be so happy. I’m your older brother-in-law...

You look the happiest of them? Huh? Soso?

“Kya ha ha ha! Rest! Rest! Sago, let’s go play at the demonstration tomorrow!”

“I never thought a day like this would come in my life.”

“Rest. Recharge. Rest. Good things.”

Tears streamed down the eyes of the Tang faction members watching the jubilant Mount Hua disciples.

‘I trusted them, those sons of dogs.’

What? Comrades? Sense of solidarity? I shouldn’t have expected that from those who aren’t even human.

‘It’s unfair. This is unreasonable.’

It was a fight doomed from the start.

No, seriously, does it make sense for sword users and secret technique throwers to start fighting right next to each other? Moreover, the Tang family was lined up tightly packed!

Basically, assassination techniques require distance to be unleashed. Maintaining proper distance and securing space is necessary to exert their full power.

It’s a delicate weapon completely different from swords that you can just rush in and swing!

But if those you trusted as comrades attack without giving distance or time to prepare mentally?

‘This is what happens.’

The Tang family members, completely smashed and rolling around, trembled with humiliation and anger.

“Th-This kind of cowardice...”

“Huh? Sasuk? What are they saying over there?”

“Huh? What words?”

“They say it’s cowardly.”

“You bastard!”

Baek Cheon sternly scolded Jo Gul.

“Those are Tang family members. Do you think they are petty cowards making excuses after defeat? Where do you get such nonsense!”

“Ugh. Sasuk. I was short-sighted.”

Tang Pae’s expression blanked for a moment.

He just realized what it means when a person is so angry that words won’t come out.

But Tang Zhan seemed less angry and suddenly raised his head, shouting with bloodshot eyes!

“No! That’s...!”

“Sasuk, I clearly heard it.”

“That can’t be!”

“Then what is this nonsense?”

“Probably just the sound of the river wind.”

“Ah.”

“...”

Those sons of dogs really...

Tang Zhan gritted his teeth and shouted.

“Enough joking around, Taoist Baek Cheon! No matter how realistic the sparring is, at least it wasn’t supposed to be a surprise attack! You should have given us time to prepare mentally!”

“Ah...”

For a moment, Baek Cheon’s gaze toward Tang Zhan became indifferent.

Tang Zhan, unable to contain his anger, slammed his fist on the ground and shouted.

“Is attacking from right beside us truly the conduct of an honorable Justice faction member? Answer me!”

Baek Cheon’s face hardened seriously.

At that moment, Tang Zhan was ready to counter all the arguments Baek Cheon would bring. The match was already decided, so at least he wanted to make them feel uneasy.

But the words Baek Cheon spoke next completely crushed his enthusiasm.

Baek Cheon scratched his head and shrugged.

“If that’s not the way of the Justice faction, then let’s just say we’re not Justice faction.”

“...”

“Let’s just say we’re the Evil Sect.”

Baek Cheon openly shrugged at the stunned Tang Zhan.

“If you’re going to prepare everything and fight politely in a non-real match, then you might as well be the Evil Sect.”

“Ugh. Our Sasuk’s tongue is especially smooth today.”

“Chung Myung’s divine skill is in full swing.”

“Sasuk, then we should go join the Four Sects Alliance now...”

“Don’t cross the line!”

Jo Gul, hit by Yoon Jong’s fist, rolled on the ground. Yet even as he was knocked away, his face was full of happiness, unlike the trembling, furious Tang family members.

“Ugh...”

“Th-That...”

The Tang family members’ eyes reddened with fury.

The pain from the blows was bearable. What truly broke the Tang family was those damn Mount Hua bastards who cowardly rushed in to seize victory and then showed the happiest faces in the world.

‘I actually thought those kinds were my comrades, even if just briefly.’

‘They seem like the type to betray even friends if it benefits them!’

‘Those rootless bastards!’

As the Tang family members’ gaze toward Mount Hua turned into that of mortal enemies,

“The match is decided.”

At that moment, everyone sharply turned their heads at the voice. Tang Gunak was smiling gently and nodding.

“It’s regrettable from my standpoint, but a promise is a promise, so Mount Hua will rest from training tomorrow morning.”

“Hooray!”

“Ugh! Wise words, Family lord!”

As the Mount Hua disciples cheered, Chung Myung clicked his tongue in displeasure. But a promise is a promise. He knew well that breaking it easily would make it hard to be used next time.

“Ugh. Lucky you, family lord, getting to rest too.”

“Me?”

“Yes. You won’t have anyone to train.”

“Ha ha ha. That’s funny. The one resting from training isn’t me, but you.”

“Huh? Me?”

“Exactly. Aren’t you also from Mount Hua? This was definitely a match where the winner rests.”

“...That’s true, but I’m an exception...”

“It’s fine. Take good rest tomorrow morning.”

“No. I’m fine...”

“Rest.”

“No...”

“I said rest.”

At the increasingly firm voice, Chung Myung closed his mouth and glanced at Tang Gunak. The usually solemn and calm corners of his eyes twitched slightly.

‘...He’s pissed off.’

If that man can’t control his facial muscles, it’s more than just anger. Usually, in Mount Hua, this is called “eyes going crazy.”

‘Well...’

Still, the trusted people were smashed without doing anything. It would be stranger if the family lord Tang Gunak wasn’t angry.

For others, excuses about not being able to show their skills might work, but is Tang Gunak someone who would accept that?

“Huff.”

Tang Gunak took a short deep breath, then smiled gently.

“I suppose I was a bit complacent.”

At that moment, a cold sweat ran down Chung Myung’s spine. How long had it been since he felt this from a person?

“The victor deserves to enjoy victory. You shall rest well with Mount Hua tomorrow. As long as possible.”

“No, just the morning...”

“Rest long. It’s not good for a person to run without rest. Didn’t I clearly say so?”

“No... that’s already settled...”

“Listen to me.”

“Yes.”

Tang Gunak nodded clearly and raised his voice slightly.

“Now then, Mount Hua members, please leave the place.”

“Ugh. Family lord, we won...”

Thup!

Baek Cheon quickly covered Jo Gul’s mouth who was talking without tact, and Yoon Jong struck Jo Gul’s lower abdomen with a palm strike.

“Ugh...”

Baek Cheon, carrying the foaming mouthed Jo Gul, bowed politely to Tang Gunak.

“Then we shall be going now.”

“Rest well.”

“Let’s go, quickly!”

“Yes!”

“L-Let’s run!”

Tang Soso, face pale blue, urged the others.

Except for Jo Gul, the Mount Hua guys with sharp senses ran swiftly and disappeared from the training ground. Even Baek Ah ran so fast that her feet seemed on fire, quickly chasing after the disciples.

“Hmm...”

Tang Gunak, leisurely watching the Mount Hua disciples flee as if seeing ghosts, turned his gaze to the Tang family members.

“F-family lord...”

“I hope you don’t misunderstand.”

“Yes?”

Tang Gunak quietly shook his head.

“Losing is never shameful. If your skill is lacking, you can lose. I too have not always won in my life.”

“Ah...”

“So don’t make such faces. What’s important is not to avoid defeat, but what you learn from it.”

The family lord, who was expected to shout orders immediately, spoke in a gentle voice, bringing hot tears to the Tang family members’ eyes.

It felt like both rage and grievance were flowing out at once.

“Family lord...”

“Young lord.”

“Yes!”

“What have you learned?”

Tang Pae gritted his teeth and answered.

“That there is no one to trust in this world.”

“And?”

“Also, that you must never let your guard down anywhere or anytime.”

“Good.”

Tang Gunak quietly nodded. His thick sword-like eyebrows twitched pleasantly.

“However.”

“...Yes?”

“That’s not all, right?”

Suddenly, Tang Gunak’s face lost its expression.

“No matter how much you were ambushed, as disciples of Sichuan Tang, you should never collapse so easily. Your biggest problem was giving up the fight the moment your formation broke.”

Emotion vanished even from his voice.

“And that is ultimately because of a lack of fighting spirit. Do you know why?”

“Th-That is...”

“Because you have nothing to lose. Because even if you lose, you won’t die, and even if you lose, you won’t suffer any loss.”

“...”

“Watching you and Mount Hua clash today, I too realized my mistake.”

“Wh-What...”

At that moment, Tang Gunak’s hand hidden inside his sleeve slowly emerged.

“I hadn’t considered that I didn’t need to use secret techniques to train you.”

“...”

“This is definitely my fault. So I will reflect first...”

Crack.

Tang Gunak’s hand outside the sleeve slowly clenched. Veins bulged on the back of his hand.

“Never again... will you suffer such a miserable fate. I will do my best to help you.”

Tang Pae, staring blankly at Tang Gunak approaching with clenched fist, suddenly cast his gaze to the distant sky.

‘Damn it... rotten.’

Looking back, the bad relationship with those Mount Hua bastards started from the beginning.

Comrades my ass, they’re the kind I’d kill on sight.

Chapter 1109: Sometimes He Gives Me Goosebumps (4)

“Auuuuuu!”

Jo Gul stretched his body vigorously and got up.

“It feels so good to wake up!”

How long had it been since he slept in like this?

Since that demon bastard entered Mount Hua, the word “oversleep” had completely disappeared from the lives of the Third-class disciples. The phrase “night’s sleep” itself barely held meaning, so how could oversleeping even be possible?

But today! On this historic day, even that damned guy couldn’t do anything to them.

Jo Gul, having straightened his attire, stepped out of the room with confident strides.

“Are you awake?”

“Had a good sleep, sahyung!”

“Yes.”

Yoon Jong smiled broadly as he faced Jo Gul. His face also gleamed with a healthy glow. Usually, his face was so dry it seemed like it would crumble at a touch...

“...It’s truly amazing that a person’s body can improve so much just by sleeping soundly.”

“Exactly! If my body stays like this, I might even be able to take on that Chung Myung guy in a fight.”

“No. That’s going too far.”

Yoon Jong chuckled and tilted his head as if recalling something.

“Wait a minute. Then...”

“Yes?”

“...Does that mean everyone else has been living like this?”

For a moment, the two looked at each other with blank faces.

“So this is my original physical condition?”

“Isn’t that so?”

“...Damn, only the others knew how good this was. Why was I the only one unaware?”

Yoon Jong wiped the moisture welling up at the corner of his eyes.

Jo Gul said, “It’s not that you didn’t know, but that you forgot. And soon you’ll forget again. As long as a demon lives in Mount Hua...”

Jo Gul cracked his neck.

“Anyway, my energy is overflowing. If I were told to rest for a whole day, I might have felt guilty and sneaked out to train, but since it’s just half a day, I think this much is okay.”

“I feel the same way.”

Yoon Jong chuckled and walked toward the training ground. The sun was high in the sky, so it was time to train.

“The Tang family people must have had a hard time, right?”

“That’s true, but...”

Yoon Jong shrugged his shoulders.

“Don’t underestimate a prestigious sect. They were caught off guard suddenly, but a prestigious sect is prestigious for a reason. It’s a different caliber; their original training wouldn’t have been worse than this.”

“No way.”

“Hmm?”

Yoon Jong turned around. Tang Soso, who had approached quietly, was smiling meaningfully.

“If that were true, I wouldn’t have been so shocked when I first entered Mount Hua.”

“...”

“It’s true the caliber is different, but the intensity is also different. There’s not a single sect in the world that forces such insane training like Mount Hua.”

“R-Really?”

“Other sects can’t even make their disciples undergo this kind of training, even if they want to.”

“Why?”

“Because people can’t endure it.”

Yoon Jong tilted his head in puzzlement.

“But we endured it, didn’t we?”

“Look at this, sahyung.”

Tang Soso looked at Yoon Jong as if he was being ridiculous.

“No, search the whole world. Do you think there’s a sect that feeds their disciples spiritual pills like Mount Hua does?”

“...”

“Mount Hua’s Heavenly Violet Pill is a medicinal pill that rivals Shaolin’s Great Vitality Pill. In some ways, it’s even more remarkable than the Great Vitality Pill. You know that, right?”

“Y-Yes.”

Because it’s the miraculous pill of spiritual pills.

“Even in Shaolin, the Great Vitality Pill isn’t given to just anyone. It’s carefully selected among those with proven talent, and only one pill is given at a time.”

“That’s right.”

Tang Soso furrowed her brows deeply.

“But Mount Hua feeds them like they’re eating some health tonics.”

“...”

“Even Monk Hae Yeon has taken more Heavenly Violet Pill at Mount Hua than the Great Vitality Pill at Shaolin.”

The two nodded silently as if their mouths were full of honey.

By that logic, Hae Yeon is bald and only uses Shaolin martial arts, but in reality, he should be considered a disciple of Mount Hua. People owe loyalty to the side that feeds them more, isn’t that right?

“That’s why only Mount Hua can endure this. Other sects would have had many cries by now. When young, the body can’t endure the training, and when older and skilled, the inertia of training makes it hard to change. It’s also an age hard to control.”

“Indeed...”

Training a ten-year-old and a thirty-year-old can’t be the same. Naturally, there would be resistance.

‘If only he weren’t someone like Chung Myung.’

If you rebel, you die. No, dying is better. And you can’t even think of running away.

That’s why such a person is bullying the entire Mount Hua, including the Tang family, Green Forest, and Namgung Family...

“Anyway, this isn’t something you can do just because you want to. So your brothers and sisters must be dying right now.”

“...I see.”

“People think the core of Mount Hua is Chung Myung sahyung, but in my view, Heavenly Violet Pill is no less important.”

“...Isn’t that what you mean?”

“Huh? What do you mean?”

“Because Chung Myung made Heavenly Violet Pill.”

Tang Soso thought for a moment and then nodded.

“That’s right...”

At that moment, Jo Gul, who had been silent as if thinking, spoke.

“Soso.”

“Yes?”

“Then something’s strange. Why don’t other sects distribute their spiritual pills like that?”

Tang Soso looked at Jo Gul with eyes that said, ‘What a clueless person.’ Receiving that look, Jo Gul shrank his neck.

“Hey, sahyung, are you really the son of a merchant family?”

“W-What does that have to do with this?”

“Do you think the spiritual pills can be made just by willing it? Those spiritual pills require at least the inner core of a rare spirit beast or a medicinal herb that appears once in decades to be made.”

“R-Really?”

“Even Tang Family’s Heavenly Poison Pill requires rare poisons that are hard to find. What makes the Heavenly Violet Pill amazing is that it can be made from materials that a person can obtain if they really want to.”

“Ah...”

Only then did Jo Gul understand everything and nodded.

“The old Heavenly Violet Pill from the practical days might have been more effective than the current one, but making one pill back then would require the effort of making hundreds of the current Heavenly Violet Pill.”

“So, to conclude...”

“Hmm?”

Jo Gul grinned.

“It means even the Tang family people are on the verge of collapsing from lack of stamina.”

“...That’s true, but...”

If you cut off the front and back, that’s what it means.

“Hehehe. That sounds like an interesting sight... Shall we go take a look?”

Yoon Jong frowned at Jo Gul’s words.

“Maybe it’s not something to say in front of Soso... But why do you have such a grudge against the Tang family? Being from Sichuan.”

“It’s because I’m from Sichuan.”

“Why?”

Jo Gul clicked his tongue and looked back at Yoon Jong.

“Do you like the Southern Edge just because you’re from Shaanxi?”

“...No way.”

“It’s similar. Since childhood, I’ve been so scared of Tang Family that it became a habit, so I developed a subconscious resentment towards them. I’m only now overcoming it.”

Yoon Jong shook his head vigorously.

‘You’ll never fix that in your lifetime.’

Just look at Soso; he’s still scared of her. How could he ever overcome Tang Family?

No... Is Soso even scarier than the Tang family??

Jo Gul chuckled, Yoon Jong shook his head, and Tang Soso wore a complicated expression as they entered the training ground. And...

“Ugh... It’s cold.”

“Suddenly, such a chill...”

The three shivered simultaneously.

“...Is it already winter?”

The three squeezed through the Mount Hua disciples already lined up in the training ground. Upon spotting a familiar back, they pressed close behind.

“Sasuk.”

“Uh... yes?”

“Why is the atmosphere like this?”

“Well... it’s because.”

Baek Cheon opened his mouth as if to say something, then gestured forward.

“See for yourself.”

“Yes?”

The three looked at the scene Baek Cheon pointed to. Their mouths dropped open simultaneously.

“Uh...”

“Wow...”

“Huh...”

There was nothing particularly different. The Tang family people were simply lined up in front of them, just like when training usually started.

If there was one minor difference...

“Oh no, brother...”

Tang Soso shivered.

The face of Tang Pae, the young leader of the Sichuan Tang family, was black and blue with bruises. It was so vivid that one might think it was painted on purpose.

“What state is...”

“What on earth...”

The swollen faces of the Tang family people looked ridiculous, but no one could laugh upon seeing them.

Because through their swollen, nearly shut eyes, a poisonous glare leaked out one after another.

“Mount Hua...”

“Bastards...”

“I’ll kill them all.”

Jo Gul flinched and stepped back.

Excuse me? We didn’t make them like that, so why are we getting slapped for no reason...?

“Traitors...”

“Rotten Shaanxi bastards.”

Wow... Even when facing the Four Sects Alliance, they wouldn’t look at them with such eyes.

“Kuh, ahem.”

Baek Cheon coughed loudly.

“It seems the Tang family lord is very angry.”

“...I guess so.”

“No matter how angry, to treat people like that...”

Tang Soso immediately rebutted.

“It’s okay. Sichuan Tang Family’s medical skills are the best. They were beaten just enough to be healed.”

“...”

At times like this, you clearly realize where Tang Soso’s bloodline comes from.

“Are we there yet?”

At that moment, Chung Myung, hands behind his back, walked out indifferently. He glanced over the Tang Family and Mount Hua facing each other.

“Uh... I originally had other things to do this afternoon.”

No sooner had he said “other things” than the Tang Family people’s gazes turned sharply to Chung Myung. If eyes could kill, Chung Myung’s body would have been torn apart.

“...That might be a bit... uh... difficult.”

Chung Myung averted his gaze slightly and looked at the distant sky.

‘...I thought it was fortunate that your bloodline didn’t continue.’

Come to think of it, since they’re relatives, where else would the blood go?

Chung Myung quietly turned around. Tang Gunak, with a stern face and arms crossed, was staring at him fiercely.

He was clearly showing, “I will never say a word and will never interfere with your exclusive right to education, but this situation where you say you might be doing other training is very uncomfortable.”

“Uh, well... haha. It would be a shame to end this without a fight, right?”

No sooner had he finished speaking than the Mount Hua guys desperately shook their heads.

They acted as if they were very, extremely, absolutely fine with it. As if there was not a shred of regret.

Of course, Chung Myung pretended not to notice.

“So, no choice. We’ll fight again like yesterday... The winner gets half a day off tomorrow.”

“W-Wait, Chung Myung. This doesn’t seem right.”

“Can’t you see their eyes? They are going to kill us.”

In the eyes of Mount Hua disciples, they saw Chung Myung looking embarrassed, uncharacteristically so, and Tang Gunak smiling contentedly as if finally pleased.

“Then, let’s start...”

“Kill them!”

“Beat them all to death!”

“Bury them! Those apostates!”

Before the words ended, the Tang Gunak people drew their razor-sharp hidden weapons from their sleeves and charged.

“Hii, hiiiiik!”

“Dodge, dodge!”

Watching the hidden weapons rain down like hail from the sky, the Mount Hua disciples realized that something had seriously gone wrong.

Chapter 1110: Sometimes He Gives Me Goosebumps (5)

Swaaaaah!

Thin, thin flying needles, imbued with clear internal qi, filled the sky as they flew in. Baek Cheon’s pupils trembled involuntarily.

“D-Defend!”

The disciples of Mount Hua were startled and quickly drew their swords.

“Hiyah!”

They emitted sword qi, creating an impenetrable defensive barrier.

Kagagakang!

Soon, with the sound of metal clashing against metal, the flying needles that struck the swords were deflected in all directions.

The sword qi was so flawless it could block even falling rain, but the needles shot by the Tang family were far sharper and fiercer than ordinary needles.

“Ugh!”

“Ah, that hurts!”

Screams echoed here and there.

If they had prepared earlier, they could have formed a denser sword barrier. But since the sword qi was hastily deployed, tiny gaps existed, and the Tang family’s needles mercilessly pierced those gaps.

The fortunate thing was that the needles lost power trying to penetrate the sword qi, so they only scratched the skin without piercing deeply.

“These bastards are cowardly ambushing us?”

Sparks flew from Jo Gul’s eyes.

Thinking about yesterday, it was shameless nonsense that shouldn’t be spoken, but naturally, Jo Gul didn’t consider that fact.

“Kill them!”

“Cut them down!”

The strength of bandits is sometimes judged by how large and high a mountain they occupy. The bandits of Mount Hua, one of the Five Great Mountains of the Central Plains, with their eyes wide open, charged fiercely at the Tang family.

No, they tried to charge.

“Smash them all... grr, grumble...”

“Ugh!”

“Guh!”

“W-What?”

Hearing screams from behind, Baek Cheon violently turned around. His disciples were foaming at the mouth and falling backward rapidly.

Seeing their faces turning pitch black, Baek Cheon’s eyes widened in shock.

“P-Poison?!”

No, what is this? Crazy, they’re using poison in martial arts training?

Baek Cheon, momentarily unable to comprehend the situation, shouted urgently.

“Raise your internal qi to resist the poison! Those bastards are using poison!”

After shouting, Baek Cheon turned sharply to look at the Tang family. His eyes bloodshot, he raised his voice to protest. No, he tried to protest.

But before him, someone else was cursing loudly.

“Using poison in martial arts, you cowardly bastards! What a filthy poison attack, poison attack! You dirty and despicable bastards!”

Huh... hehe...

Ah, right. That’s true. Yeah, that’s what I wanted to say.

But you know... don’t you think it’s a bit strange that you’re the one saying that, Soso?

I’m infinitely happy that you’ve become a full-fledged disciple of Mount Hua now. But... I think your brother is over there too... isn’t your sense of belonging a bit too much?

“Exactly! Using poison in martial arts training!”

“Cowardly!”

“Since when did the Tang family become such a dishonorable place!”

Hearing the pouring “praise,” the Tang family naturally responded with all their might.

“Get downnnnn!”

Startled, the Mount Hua disciples flattened themselves on the ground as shurikens imbued with internal qi whizzed overhead. They looked slick and polished, clearly coated meticulously with poison.

“Hyung, where is that noise coming from?”

“Sounds like dogs barking.”

Hearing that voice, the Mount Hua disciples’ eyes reddened with fury.

“But those bastards?”

As they flared up, the Tang family members chuckled.

“Oh dear, you’re really angry.”

“Those who kept saying martial arts should be like real combat are foaming at the mouth just because we used a bit of poison.”

“Fine, let’s be cowards then.”

“Since we’re being cowards, let’s be properly cowardly. Throw it on them!”

As the Tang family people flicked their sleeves, a thick, acrid grayish-white smoke billowed out. The smoke quickly rode the wind and engulfed the Mount Hua sect.

“What is this?”

“I-It’s Qi Dissipating Poison!”

“Don’t breathe! Internal qi will scatter!”

Even a little inhalation disrupts internal qi control; the Qi Dissipating Poison rose like clouds and covered Mount Hua disciples.

Words like “dirty” and “despicable” rose in the throat, but now was not the time to say such things.

Swaeaeaeaeak!

Piercing through the thick poisonous smoke, flying daggers swarmed densely. The Mount Hua disciples rolled on the ground to dodge the maddening combined attack, their eyes wide open.

“Argh! You bastards!”

“Kill them!”

Screaming, the Five Swords leapt forward, shaking his sword. The Plum Blossom Sword qi they deployed swiftly struck down the flying daggers.

Of course, the poison the Tang family used now was not lethal.

Moreover, Mount Hua disciples generally have high internal qi and, thanks to their constitution changed by the Heavenly Vitality Pill, have high resistance to poison, so if they had been prepared, getting hit by one or two needles wouldn’t have caused such fuss.

Those who fell were the ones who couldn’t even think about the poison and raised their internal qi all at once.

But that’s that!

“These bastards fight dirty in martial arts training!”

“You wanna try it once?”

“Those rotten poison-using bastards, I never liked how they looked down on people claiming to be prestigious! Those country bumpkins from Sichuan!”

Hearing that, the Tang family also widened their eyes.

“But those bastards have loose lips?”

“Asking a sect that uses poison to fight without poison is dirty! How is that different from playing chess without the chariot and elephant?”

“Then you shouldn’t use swords! How dare you talk about cowardice!”

“Since when were you a prestigious sect!”

The hostility was fully confirmed. The accumulated grudges were fully confirmed.

Then only one thing remains.

“Beat them to death!”

“Kill them!”

With poison in their eyes, Mount Hua madly charged at the Tang family. Seeing this, the Tang family scattered their hidden weapons like rain while spewing acrid poisonous smoke in all directions.

“Wild boar bastards are coming!”

“Trample them all!”

“Aaaaaah!”

Endless shouts, mournful screams, and the clashing of weapons began to swirl in the misty battlefield.

“Dieee!”

Jo Gul’s sword struck the side of a Tang family warrior retreating backward.

Crack!

Of course, he struck with the flat of the blade, not the edge, but once internal qi is applied, even a thin sword becomes harder than an iron club.

The one hit directly on the side was flung away without even screaming.

“They can’t do anything when engaged!”

Having cleanly dealt with one, Jo Gul quickly searched for the next target.

“Hiyah!”

As he charged recklessly at the opponent in his sight, the targeted Tang family member immediately leapt backward, scattering flying daggers.

“It’s useless, man!”

Jo Gul’s sword moved swiftly like a venomous snake, deflecting the flying daggers. No matter how agile the Tang family’s daggers were, compared to Chung Myung’s wildly swinging sword, this was nothing.

Swaeaeaeak!

But at that moment, poison-coated shurikens covered Jo Gul’s entire body.

“I told you it’s useless!”

Behind them were hair-thin cow-hair needles.

“Useless...”

After blocking those, poisonous sand and poison powder followed.

“U-Useless...”

After barely dodging those, poisonous irritants were scattered on the ground, and black metal balls rained down like rain.

“Aaaah! You bastard!”

Jo Gul’s eyes rolled back.

These bastards really fight dirty. I didn’t know when they were allies, but now as enemies, there’s no one dirtier.

Those who were revered as masters of poison and hidden weapons, cold-blooded like venomous snakes and grim reapers, once the fight started, just ran backward throwing spit and scattering poison!

“Fight me properly! Properly!”

As he shouted desperately, a Tang Coin Mace (Dang Gunak’s coin-shaped hidden weapon) flew straight into his wide-open mouth.

Startled, Jo Gul rolled his body to avoid the coin. After rolling once on the ground, he looked up, searching for the chuckling sound.

A Tang family warrior was smirking. With a sneer that rapidly raised Jo Gul’s blood pressure, he said:

“You’re like a wild boar that’s been slapped first.”

“Tang Zhan!”

Jo Gul gritted his teeth and gripped his sword tightly.

“You bastard... I never liked you from the start.”

“Haha, if someone heard that, they’d think I liked you. You’d have been crawling under me if you were in Sichuan.”

“Oh, right. Of course you would have.”

“Huh?”

Jo Gul smirked.

“So it’s really fortunate you left Sichuan. I can beat you up properly now. I wonder how the proud Tang family’s young master would feel rolling on the ground crying with his head smashed by my sword?”

“But, this bastard!”

“What! What am I to do if you glare at me, you bastard!”

Jo Gul raised his sword and charged at Tang Zhan. At that moment, dozens of hidden weapons poured out from Tang Zhan’s fluttering sleeves.

Kagagagakang!

Jo Gul deflected the flying hidden weapons and relentlessly pursued the retreating Tang Zhan. But Tang Zhan calmly stepped back, only scattering his hidden weapons.

Seeing Tang Zhan almost caught but never caught, Jo Gul shouted in frustration.

“Aaaah! You fight dirty in martial arts! Fight properly, you cowardly bastard!”

“Thanks for the compliment!”

“You, I will definitely kill you!”

Jo Gul, eyes rolled back, completely lost his reason and charged at Tang Zhan.

Others were the same. Swearing and shouting echoed everywhere. With so much hostility, both groups lost reason and rampaged as if mortal enemies.

Chung Myung, watching the chaotic carnage from a distance that could no longer be called martial arts or sparring, smiled contentedly.

‘It’s a total mess.’

He saw Baek Cheon rampaging and Tang Soso grabbing someone’s collar with wild eyes, twisting their jaw.

Guys... of course, uh... I do want you to fight hard... but honestly...

‘I didn’t expect it to go this far.’

No matter what, there’s a limit, you bastards...

I thought you’d keep some face and show some mercy. Did I ever think you’d fight like those slapped Evil Sect bastards, cursing nonstop? Huh? Guys?

“Well...”

Chung Myung glanced sideways at Tang Gunak.

“Is this... okay?”

“Hmm? What do you mean?”

“It seems a bit overheated...”

Tang Gunak heard that and smiled gently.

“I don’t think it’s something to worry about. Kids usually grow up by fighting, don’t they?”

“...”

Excuse me? They’re not growing by fighting, they’re dying by fighting.

“If they get hurt like that...”

“So what do you want to do?”

“We should stop them a bit...”

“Huh?”

At that moment, poison filled Tang Gunak’s eyes.

“Ah, so you want to win and then run away?”

“...”

“That might be the rule in Shaanxi, but not in Sichuan. Understand? If you win and run away, be prepared to have a dagger plunged into your neck while sleeping.”

Chung Myung was speechless and thought.

‘This guy isn’t normal either.’

Chung Myung often said that no martial artist who reached the pinnacle was normal. But the problem was he thought Tang Gunak was among the better ones. This man was ultimately a descendant of that crazy bastard’s blood.

“Uh...”

Unintentionally turning his head, Chung Myung saw Yu Yiseol kicking Dang Pae’s face with his foot. Chung Myung couldn’t help but smile brightly.

‘I don’t know anymore.’

Yeah. Kids grow up by fighting, after all.

Hehe. Hehehehe. Hehehehehe!

Chapter 1111: Can I Do Well? (1)

It’s easy to overlook, but the concept of ‘normal’ is never absolute.

When someone is called a normal person, it means they don’t deviate significantly from the social and cultural norms of their environment.

This implies that someone considered normal in one place might be seen as strange if dropped into a completely different setting.

Now, Namgung Do-Wei was painfully realizing this fact.

‘Am I the one who’s wrong?’

He didn’t know how things had been in the past, but now he prided himself on not being stubborn. Yet, what was happening right before his eyes was hard even for him to accept.

No, no matter how much he thought about it, it wasn’t his fault.

Who could have imagined such a scene?

A disciple of Mount Hua was straddling the son of the Tang Family lord, punching his jaw repeatedly, while a female swordsman of Mount Hua was mercilessly breaking the arm of the Tang Family’s young lord.

Well, let’s say that’s understandable. It could happen. Someone with a heart as vast as the ocean might calmly accept such things as “Well, that can happen.”

But when a monk who has taken a disciple’s vow stamps his foot on a Tang Family member’s face, and a female swordsman from the Tang Family stabs her own brothers with a sword, even those people would have to see things differently.

Huh? It seems like one person is missing...

“Step on him! Step on him!”

“Kill him!”

“Aaaaaaah!”

Oh dear, Taoist Yoon Jong. Why are you getting stepped on there? Oh my, being trampled so enthusiastically...

Namgung Do-Wei, watching the chaotic scene before him with eyes full of disbelief, soon squeezed his eyes shut. After seeing a white marten dressed in Mount Hua’s black martial uniform leap up and sharply strike a Tang Family member’s cheek, he no longer wanted to think.

‘What is happening to the world?’

The champions of Shaanxi and Sichuan were clashing here at the Yangtze River.

It sounds grand and magnificent, but in reality, it was like a street brawl... No, more like a gang fight in the back alleys.

The Namgung Family members, pushed to the corner of the training ground to avoid the brutal and terrible fight, watched the brawl as if it were a rare spectacle.

“Mount Hua definitely has good stamina.”

“There was a perception that the Tang Family is weak in chaotic fights, but that’s not necessarily true. In such a brawl, it’s hard to avoid poison attacks.”

“For that, the Mount Hua folks are fighting well.”

“They’ve been trained by someone more poisonous than the Tang Family’s poison.”

“Ah, I see. Understood.”

Don’t understand! Why would you understand that!

It’s like ‘one who stays near ink gets stained black the most’—the Namgung Family seems to be getting stranger. Those who would have frowned at such a sight now genuinely admire and even applaud.

But what could Namgung Do-Wei say? After all, he was the one who brought them into the Heavenly Friends Alliance and made them like this.

“Ah, no. Is it really okay for things to be like this?”

Though it was a bitter assessment for him, the current Namgung Family was hard to compare to those two sects. Anyone who knew the Heavenly Friends Alliance would consider those two sects its core.

Yet those two core sects were now fighting while hurling curses at each other.

‘Won’t this only build up bad feelings?’

He found it hard to understand the meaning of this training. But the bigger problem was that there was no way to stop Chung Myung or Tang Gunak. So the remaining method was...

“Um, um...”

“Yes?”

“Well...”

Namgung Do-Wei swallowed dryly and looked at Im So-Byeong. Unlike Namgung Do-Wei, Im So-Byeong was calmly observing the situation with the attitude that nothing happening in this household was surprising. He slightly raised his chin and met his gaze.

“Why do you ask?”

Namgung Do-Wei’s expression became extremely awkward.

He understood that. In the Heavenly Friends Alliance, there were no boundaries between sects or distinctions between righteous and evil. Anyone who could trust each other with one heart could enter the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s fold.

And the Green Forest had proven itself worthy of being a proud member of the Heavenly Friends Alliance. From the moment its leader, Im So-Byeong, risked his life to join the fight against the Demonic Cult, no one could doubt his true intentions.

‘I know... I definitely know that.’

Even if he understood intellectually, it wasn’t easy to speak to Im So-Byeong.

He was the lesser head of the Namgung Family. He had never once imagined a day when he would speak familiarly to the leader of the Evil Sect. To him, the Green Forest King was nothing more or less than a sacrificial lamb whose neck he would one day have to cut with a declaration.

No, to be honest, who were the Green Forest? Leeches who occupied a decent mountain and sucked the lifeblood of passing civilians...

“Young lord.”

“Yes?”

“Right now... it seems like you’re insulting someone with your eyes after calling them here?”

“N-no way.”

Namgung Do-Wei was a scion of a prestigious family. Rarely in the Heavenly Friends Alliance, he understood what basic manners were. He calmed himself and began to speak.

“Well... shouldn’t we calm things down a bit?”

“Why bother talking?”

“Excuse me?”

Im So-Byeong shrugged as if annoyed.

“Taoist Chung Myung will handle it. The Tang Family lord is also here.”

“Ah, no. I know that... but I worry that it will only build up bad feelings.”

“Oh?”

At that moment, Im So-Byeong looked at Namgung Do-Wei with a meaningful gaze.

“Ah, so you have insight that sees further than the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint and the Poison King?”

“Yes?”

“So you alone have noticed what they didn’t notice and are now telling me the countermeasures? And you want to use me, who has some status, as a messenger so they won’t feel uncomfortable?”

“W-wait...”

Namgung Do-Wei was visibly flustered. Could it really be interpreted that way?

“In other words, those two are very uncomfortable, but the bandit boss is ridiculously easy to deal with?”

“...I-I didn’t say that!”

“Ah, the bandit leader in front of you is so stupid he can’t catch on unless you say it directly?”

“...”

Can a person be this twisted?

‘Was he always like this?’

When they went to the Southern land together, despite frequent grumbling, he thought this man was very sharp and broad-minded...

This reaction was clearly strange even to the Green Forest members, as one bandit tilted his head and quietly asked Im So-Byeong.

“Boss, why are you so pissed? It doesn’t seem like he said anything wrong.”

“There’s nothing he can’t say.”

“Yes?”

“The problem is the person who said it.”

The bandit who spoke to Im So-Byeong glanced at Namgung Do-Wei.

“...Is there a problem?”

“Problem? There’s no problem on their side. The problem is with me!”

“...No. What do you mean? Keep...”

“Why?”

At that moment, Im So-Byeong glared at the bandit with shining eyes.

“Why? Born in a bandit’s family, I even took the civil service exam to rise in the world but failed, ending up as a bandit. Do you think I’m twisted with rage seeing a young master born into a prestigious family living in comfort?”

“...”

“Ha! What a ridiculous thought! I’m not envious at all! Of course, no matter how much I recite the principles of the martial alliance, I’m just a bandit, and that guy lives a comfortable life where if he kills a decent person on the street, people just say ‘Ah, the dead must have done something wrong.’ But I am not envious at all. Why would I envy him?”

“...Please take a breath and speak.”

Not only the Green Forest members but even the Namgung Family became somewhat solemn at those words.

Once Im So-Byeong started speaking, he didn’t stop.

“Ah, I was wrong. It was my fault. A noble person entrusted me with a task, and a lowly Evil Sect member must somehow fulfill the order! How about I go and deliver the message now?”

Maybe it could have ended awkwardly like that.

If Namgung Do-Wei had just briefly apologized for his short-sightedness here, it might have been so. Of course, he intended to and was about to apologize immediately.

But things rarely go as planned in life; small fires often quickly turn into big ones.

“Hey, you’re taking it too harshly.”

Startled by the voice from behind, Namgung Do-Wei turned around.

One of the Namgung Family swordsmen standing behind looked at Im So-Byeong with a displeased face and muttered.

“Being so twisted like that.”

“S-shut your mouth...”

Namgung Do-Wei hurriedly tried to cover the swordsman’s mouth. Despite appearances, Im So-Byeong was the Green Forest King. No mere Namgung Family warrior could speak so freely to him.

But before his words ended, someone responded.

“Twisted?”

“...!”

Namgung Do-Wei’s head sharply turned the other way. A fierce-looking Green Forest member behind Im So-Byeong spat on the ground.

“Oh dear. Seems like you have no hardships in life and no respect to show. How dare a lowly grunt talk back to the Green Forest King?”

“What? Do these bandits think words are candy to throw at people?”

“Bandit? Yeah, I’m a bandit. And you? You still think you’re the old Namgung Family? If it weren’t for Mount Hua, guys like you would’ve been killed long ago.”

“Oh, really? Who? You? Surely you don’t mean you could do that? Know your place, bandits.”

“Who doesn’t know their place?”

The Namgung Family swordsmen gripped their sword hilts and slowly moved. The Green Forest members also gripped their waist swords and stepped forward threateningly.

“Consider yourselves lucky. If this weren’t the Heavenly Friends Alliance, you’d all be dead.”

“Who says that! If Taoist Chung Myung weren’t keeping you as his servants, we’d have exterminated you ourselves.”

“What? Servants? You abandoned your own family and ran away here!”

“What? Running away? The bandit has the nerve to spout nonsense?”

The atmosphere became extremely tense.

Just as the flustered Namgung Do-Wei hurried to stop everyone.

“Oh, nice. The atmosphere.”

“Ch-Chung Myung, Taoist!”

Chung Myung appeared, strolling over. He glanced at the Green Forest members gripping their swords and the Namgung Family swordsmen, frowning deeply. Namgung Do-Wei stepped forward to try to calm the storm.

“I’ll handle this...”

“Perfect timing.”

“Yes?”

Chung Myung shrugged.

“No need to explain, setting the mood beforehand was good. You were bored just watching anyway, right?”

“...”

“But there’s one thing I don’t like.”

“Uh, what is it...?”

“You’re still the Namgung Family and the Green Forest. If you have complaints against one another, you should fight with swords or fists. Why are you just talking instead of using those good swords?”

“Yes?”

Chung Myung folded his arms behind his back and gestured.

“See over there? They’re fighting.”

“...Yes.”

“Yeah, no difference. The side standing last today rests tomorrow. The losing side works until dawn.”

“...”

“Start on your own. The winner comes to report to me.”

“W-wait. Taoist Chung Myung? Taoist Chung Myung!”

After finishing his words, Chung Myung left without looking back.

Namgung Do-Wei, staring blankly at his retreating back, turned around with a pale face.

A Green Forest member gripping his sword approached the Namgung Family with a smirk, and the Namgung swordsmen looked at them disdainfully.

“Wow, I really didn’t want it to come to this.”

“Unintentionally, really.”

“Can’t be helped. I have no ill feelings at all.”

I’d rather be cursed at, honestly.

“You all heard that, right? Let’s show those gentlemen how terrifying the murim world is today!”

“Let them know exactly what happens when bandits come down from the mountains!”

At that moment, Namgung and the Green Forest, who usually disliked each other, unleashed all their grievances and charged at each other.

Chapter 1112: Can I Do Well? (2)

“Kuh, cough.”

Baek Cheon trembled.

His hands, soaked in poison, had been convulsing for some time. Even if the poison hadn’t invaded his body, he would have been trembling anyway. He was that exhausted, with no strength left at all.

“Cough!”

Baek Cheon coughed dryly several times from the surging poison and painfully lifted his head to look up at the sky.

Staring at the sky that was already beginning to darken, he barely raised the sword he was holding above his head.

Then, with a voice barely alive, he shouted with difficulty.

“We won...”

Before he could even finish his words, Baek Cheon’s body collapsed forward.

“We... won...”

Thud.

The last remaining person crumbled like a rotten old tree.

It was a sight that was, in some ways, comedic, and in others, tragic.

Among those who had fought fiercely as if to destroy the training ground, no one remained standing. Regardless of whether they were from Mount Hua or the Tang Family, everyone lay sprawled on the ground, groaning or foaming at the mouth, unconscious.

“Ugh...”

“You... bastards...”

“Kill...”

Even amid the groans, curses occasionally slipped out. It was hard to say whether this was impressive or pathetic.

Chung Myung, standing silently in a corner watching the scene, quietly turned his head.

“It seems to be over?”

“...”

“Family lord?”

“...”

“Hey?”

Tang Gunak’s eyebrows trembled. Even the corners of his eyes and mouth quivered slightly.

“That...”

Tang Gunak, speaking, clenched his teeth tightly.

“...It seems so.”

He was clearly trying to appear calm, but the sound of his grinding teeth slipped out involuntarily. It seemed he was very displeased that despite regrouping and fighting again, they ultimately lost.

“Hmm. Was that not enough?”

“...They’ll die if you keep that up.”

“I don’t think that’s something you should say.”

“... Even I think that was harsh enough.”

Tang Gunak frowned deeply at those words. It seemed that if Chung Myung hadn’t stopped him, he would have severely reprimanded them again today.

‘He’s really not normal.’

Chung Myung shook his head and stepped forward. Standing before the fallen, he spoke.

“So originally...”

He scratched the back of his head reflexively.

“The losing side had planned to train until dawn.”

“Ugh...”

“Oh dear... oh dear... I’m dying...”

Looking at the pitifully sprawled winners and losers alike, Chung Myung shook his head.

“...It doesn’t seem like the right situation for that, so let’s end today’s training here. As promised, the Tang Family will come out tomorrow morning, and Mount Hua in the afternoon.”

“...”

“Are you listening?”

“...”

“Hey?”

No answer came. Chung Myung shook his head and turned around.

“...I wonder what happened over there.”

He sighed deeply and trudged toward the place where the Green Forest and Namgung family were grappling.

“I’ll carry it!”

“No, I’ll do it!”

“I heard your body isn’t fully recovered yet...”

“I’m fine! Give it here!”

Mrs. Chu snatched the plate from the servant as if taking it by force.

“Ah, no, you don’t have to push yourself so much already. We can do it.”

“No. This isn’t a free job; it’s paid work. Of course, I have to do my best.”

“If that’s the case...”

Mrs. Chu smiled gently and moved inside.

Since Hyun Jong’s visit, she had been working in the manor’s kitchen. Still adapting, she focused more on chores than cooking but gave her best in everything.

‘I must never be a burden on them.’

She knew she wasn’t much help to them now. But while she could accept being called unhelpful, she never wanted to be accused of working half-heartedly out of pity.

A person who doesn’t appreciate kindness is no different from a beast.

She was feeding the disciples of Mount Hua, who had not only been generously hosted here but also employed to ensure their comfort. There could be no negligence.

For the sake of the child to be born as well.

The work wasn’t as hard as she thought, and those who knew her situation were considerate, so she had no discomfort.

If there was one problem.

Mrs. Chu’s steps slowed as she entered the dining hall carrying plates. As if approaching an awkward place, she reluctantly moved and carefully set the plates on the table.

Then she subtly glanced at the person sitting there.

A pitiful sight... a man who, on a normal day, would have drawn everyone’s attention just by appearing in the hall.

But Mrs. Chu glanced at his face not because he was handsome, but because that handsome face was so worn out it was beyond description.

“...Please eat well.”

“...Thank... you...”

Yes. If there was one problem, it was that Mount Hua didn’t seem like an ordinary place.

‘...The looks of the people...’

If one saw this scene for the first time, they might have fainted thinking they had encountered a jiangshi.

Why not? Everyone had sunken faces, their bodies wrapped in bandages, sitting slumped like corpses at the table.

“You should... eat...”

“Yes...”

Baek Cheon weakly nodded toward Mrs. Chu and feebly picked up his chopsticks. Seeing this, Mrs. Chu left the dining hall.

Baek Cheon spoke in a dying voice.

“...Let’s eat. We have to eat, guys...”

“Yes... sasuk.”

“We... have to eat...”

The disciples of Mount Hua sluggishly moved their bodies to pick up the bowls in front of them. Their movements were so slow even an eighty-year-old would click his tongue.

Honestly, it wasn’t a situation where food would go down easily.

But the disciples of Mount Hua knew from experience that if they skipped a meal now because they didn’t want to swallow food, greater pain awaited them tomorrow.

It’s not just that you have to eat to live, but you have to eat to survive. That was Mount Hua’s unspoken rule.

“Ouch ouch ouch...”

“Ugh. My mouth is all torn up.”

“Ah. It stings...”

Groans escaped from the mouths of the Mount Hua disciples as they chewed on their food.

Food touching the bruised and torn areas caused curses to fly out naturally. But the pain felt in their mouths was nothing compared to the rest.

“Ugh, ugh.”

“Ah... I feel like throwing up.”

“Sasuk, my stomach is queasy; I can’t swallow...”

“Ah... what kind of poison is this?”

The Mount Hua disciples grimaced. Because the poison upset their stomachs, even the smell of food made them nauseous and queasy.

“No. Those filthy bastards, they want to win like that? Using poison in a duel...”

It was at that moment.

In a large dining hall converted from a warehouse originally used to store merchants’ goods traveling the Yangtze River, the Tang Family members, who hadn’t dared to eat and were just watching, all turned their gaze simultaneously. They reacted to the conversation of the Mount Hua disciples.

“What? Cowardly?”

Baek Sang realized what he had said and hastily covered his mouth with his hand.

“Ha. Haha...”

Yoon Jong awkwardly laughed and tried to smooth things over.

“Ah, sorry. We got excited...”

“Do you think words are sweets? If we had really used poison cowardly, do you think anyone would be alive in Mount Hua now?”

“...Zhan!”

Tang Zhan, who had stood up abruptly, sat back down awkwardly after being scolded by Tang Pae.

“Sorry, hyung.”

“Be careful. No matter how true your words are, you shouldn’t offend others. After all, losing is losing, right?”

It was a very orthodox statement.

But if the listener’s heart is twisted, even that truth sounds bitter.

“...Truth?”

Jo Gul glared at Tang Pae and Tang Zhan with wide eyes.

“Oh dear, we didn’t know this. We were happy thinking we won, but it turns out we survived only because of the mercy the Tang Family showed us.”

“Ahem. No. That’s not what I meant...”

“If you’re that resentful, then use poison properly and win. Why are you sticking your tongue out after losing?”

“What?”

“Hey! That’s harsh.”

“No, sasuk! It’s the truth! Those bastards subtly look down on us every time they speak!”

“...”

“If they’re a prestigious family, then so be it! Even the truly prestigious Namgung Family doesn’t act like that. Since when has the Sichuan Tang family been more prestigious than the Namgung Family?”

“What, you bastard?”

At that moment, Tang Pae couldn’t hold back his anger and stood up abruptly.

The Sichuan Tang family was always the second among the Five Great Families. Along with the Hubei Peng Family, they inevitably had a subtle inferiority complex toward the Namgung Family. So being directly poked at on that point naturally made him furious.

“No. Did I say something wrong? Isn’t it true? Namgung so...”

Jo Gul spoke smoothly and turned his head but suddenly shut his mouth.

He had intended to bring in the Namgung Family’s young lord to gain an advantage, but Namgung Do-Wei’s condition was somewhat strange. His usually neat appearance was gone, and his bloodshot eyes glared as if ready to kill someone.

“Namgung lord?”

“Oh dear. That look could kill a person.”

And right in front of Namgung Do-Wei, sitting almost sprawled in his chair directly opposite, was Im So-Byeong. He had his feet on the table and was gently fanning his face with a fan.

“Does that change the fact that the losing fight was lost?”

“This...”

“Ah, if you’re upset, then win. What did I do wrong? The Green Forest! Our Green Forest! Those insignificant bandits are stronger than the Namgung Family now. Oh dear, what can I do?”

Grinding teeth!

Namgung Do-Wei’s teeth grinding echoed throughout the dining hall.

Of course, Im So-Byeong’s condition wasn’t good either. Every time his fluttering fan moved away from his face, his bruised blue eyelids were visible.

But regardless of his condition, Im So-Byeong exuded the composure of a victor.

“Ah, you don’t need to be so resentful. It’s not that Namgung is weak, but the Green Forest is stronger. Hahahahahahaha!”

“Ugh... ugh...”

Namgung Do-Wei’s eyes were bloodshot.

“If it was a fight of skill, we would have won!”

“Who? Who?”

“We were just not used to the chaotic fights. It’s just experience...!”

“Yeah, yeah. That’s right. Of course, there’s no doubt about it.”

“You...”

Seeing Namgung Do-Wei’s hand trembling, Jo Gul instinctively shrank his neck.

‘Someone’s going to die if this keeps up.’

Normally, when such a person loses control, they’re even scarier, but the Green Forest King seems fearless. He’s really rubbing salt in the wound... But how did the atmosphere get so bad over there?

Then Im So-Byeong turned his head toward the Tang Family and chuckled.

“Oh dear. It seems the great Sichuan Tang Family got beaten up pretty badly too. This, this is the state of the Justice faction.”

“That bandit?”

“Do you really want to die?”

“Die?”

Im So-Byeong chuckled.

“Ah. You have that skill? Getting beaten by Mount Hua too?”

“Ugh... aah!”

Tang Zhan, unable to hold back any longer, slammed the table and tried to charge, but Tang Pae shouted.

“Calm down!”

“But hyung!”

“Didn’t you hear? The family lord and the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint said they will punish anyone who draws their sword privately! Endure it!”

“Ugh...”

Everyone openly showed hostility toward each other but couldn’t rush in rashly. Then a quiet voice rang out.

“But that.”

“Huh?”

Everyone’s gaze turned to Yu Yiseol, who had spoken.

“...It means as long as you don’t draw your sword.”

“...”

“...”

Mrs. Chu quickened her pace. Carrying plates in both hands, she swiftly opened the dining hall door.

“More food has arrived...”

But at that moment, someone flew right beside her, crashing through the wall.

“Aaaaaah!”

“I’ve never liked you! You bastard!”

“Who said that nonsense!”

“You rotten bandit!”

“Who do you call a bandit, you snotty brat! Dieee!”

The food she had painstakingly prepared scattered everywhere.

Tables, chairs, and people flew through the air as some climbed on others, throwing punches. The tangled mess was so chaotic that no one could tell who was who, and they were biting each other in the fight.

Mrs. Chu couldn’t even think of dodging and stared blankly at the scene.

“Alliance, my ass! You bastards!”

“Since when did you think you could match us!”

“Even the bandits are going crazy in here! I’ll kill you all!”

“Aaaah! Everyone, attack!”

Watching the pandemonium before her eyes, she squeezed her eyes shut involuntarily.

‘Can... I do it well in here...?’

For the first time, Mrs. Chu thought maybe she hadn’t been blessed but rather badly bitten.

Chapter 1113: Can I Do Well? (3)

“Aren’t you holding your head properly?”

“Ughhh...”

“Ugh...”

Chung Myung’s eyes sparkled fiercely as he looked at the hundreds of people with heavy stones hanging from their necks, bowing their heads to the ground. His sharp gaze mercilessly pierced the backs of the Heavenly Friends Alliance members.

“I showed some consideration that people usually don’t, letting you rest a bit! And you couldn’t stand it and started fighting?”

“...”

“You’re protesting against me, right? You have so much energy you could die, but you rebel just because I let you rest? Huh?”

At that moment, Jo Gul, who was bowing his head, suddenly stood up and raised his hand.

“What?”

“You seem to misunderstand. We didn’t fight because we had leftover energy.”

“Then?”

Jo Gul shrugged his shoulders.

“We really had no strength, but when people get so pissed off, strength comes out on its own.”

“Ah.”

“Isn’t it amazing? Haha.”

Chung Myung nodded vigorously as if he understood.

“I think I get it, sahyung.”

“Right?”

“Yeah. That’s exactly how I feel right now, you bastard!”

Chung Myung kicked off the ground and his foot crashed into Jo Gul’s face.

“Guh!”

As Jo Gul rolled on the ground, Chung Myung climbed onto his stomach. Soon, Chung Myung’s upper body moved energetically from side to side for the first time in a while.

“Die! Die! Please just die!”

“Ah! Aah! Ah! Sa, save me...”

“Die! You bastard! Die!”

Jo Gul fought the Tang family during the day, got beaten up here and there at the diner in the evening, and now was enthusiastically beaten by Chung Myung, but unfortunately, no one here sympathized with him.

‘He deserves to be hit.’

‘Honestly, he has no excuse to say even if he dies.’

‘Chung Myung, who has kept him alive until now, is a true taoist.’

It was a meaningful moment when the four sect members, who had been twisted in chaos, momentarily became one in spirit.

“Hoo! Hoo! Hoo! Hoo!”

Chung Myung stood up, leaving the brutally sprawled Jo Gul behind, his eyes gleaming as he glared at the others.

Everyone quickly closed their eyes and avoided Chung Myung’s gaze. If they met his eyes, they would end up like that too.

“I... yeah. I... was a bit thoughtless.”

“...”

“Everyone is so strong and energetic. I worried for no reason.”

Everyone here realized that even a person’s voice could be so sharp with energy. It was a very unique experience, but also a frightening one.

“It’s all my fault! All of it!”

“...”

“If I had known everyone was so strong and motivated, I would have increased the intensity earlier. Huh? It’s my fault for not knowing you had leftover energy to fight even after all that training!”

“W-wait a minute, Chung Myung!”

Baek Cheon looked up in shock, but it was already too late.

“A man!”

Chung Myung stomped the ground loudly and shouted.

“Cannot keep making the same mistake. Yeah, exactly. Alright, let’s see who dies first, you bastards! Let’s try rolling you without sleep for three days...”

“We-we’ll die doing that!”

“I’m telling you to die, you bastard!”

Chung Myung rolled his eyes and charged at Baek Cheon this time.

The training hall instantly turned into a mess. Hyun Jong, watching from a distance, buried his face in his hands and sighed in despair.

‘How...’

They became the Heavenly Friends Alliance from Mount Hua, but how has nothing changed at all? How...?

Oh, Primordial Heavenly Lord...

“I feel like I’m going to die...”

“I’m already dead.”

“So-Soso. I think my back is messed up.”

“...There’s some needles over there, so take them and stick them properly...”

The dying sounds escaped from the mouths of Mount Hua disciples sprawled on the floor.

The places hit while fighting the Tang family hurt, and the places bruised during the chaotic fight also ached. But the most painful parts were the back and knees overworked by Chung Myung’s venting.

No, how could the places abused while rolling on the floor hurt more than those hit while fighting martial artists? At this point, Chung Myung’s rolling might be considered an art form.

“...Who nagged at that Chung Myung bastard?”

“I think it was Baek Ah? I saw him running toward the door shortly after the fight started.”

“Dirty weasel bastard... betraying the sworn brothers...”

“Ugh...”

While Tang Soso trembled with anger, Baek Cheon struggled to get up and sat on a chair.

“I’m dying...”

Baek Cheon, who was better than anyone at keeping his clothes neat, now had his clothes soaked with sweat and dirt, a complete mess. He was too exhausted even to think about washing up.

Jo Gul grumbled.

“Ugh. Those Tang bastards started trouble over nothing...”

“You better shut your mouth, you bastard!”

“Ah!”

Yoon Jong mercilessly kicked Jo Gul. Looking back, this could have been easily resolved if not for Jo Gul’s meddling.

“Honestly, I just want to...”

Yoon Jong rolled his eyes, but Baek Cheon stopped him.

“Stop it, Yoon Jong. It’s Gul’s fault... no, it’s that bastard’s fault. Yes, he is completely wrong and clearly the root of all this, but it’s not only his fault.”

“There’s a contradiction in your words, Sasuk.”

“Ugh.”

Baek Cheon sighed deeply and summarized.

“It’s our fault for losing our temper.”

“...Actually, they said things that could make anyone lose their temper.”

“That’s true.”

There’s nothing more infuriating than being underestimated after losing.

Baek Cheon clicked his tongue and looked at everyone with a slightly changed attitude.

“Speaking of which.”

Baek Cheon’s gaze turned to Tang Soso.

“Soso.”

“Yes, Sasuk.”

“What do you think about what the Tang family said?”

“What do you mean?”

“That... if they had used poison properly, none of us would have survived.”

“Ah, that?”

Tang Soso nodded calmly.

“Yes. My brother crossed the line a bit. He shouldn’t have said that. From now on, don’t worry about me and just beat them freely.”

“Ah, no, that’s not what I meant.”

“Yes?”

Seeing Tang Soso questioning as if she did nothing wrong, Baek Cheon broke into a cold sweat.

“I’m asking what would have happened if the Tang family had really used poison properly.”

“Ah... so that’s what you meant.”

Tang Soso slightly frowned.

“Well, if you think about it, neither the sasuks nor the brothers did it properly. The essence of Mount Hua swordsmanship... though it’s awkward for me to say, lies in the killing sword, but that can’t be used in regular sparring.”

“That’s right.”

“But in such a situation, the Tang family would have unleashed their secret deadly poisons and forbidden hidden weapons, dangerous weapons banned from use...”

Tang Soso rested her chin as if pondering.

“Hmm. This is a bit difficult...”

When she hesitated to answer, Baek Cheon nodded as if he had guessed.

“The Tang family would have won.”

“Ah, no. Not necessarily.”

“No. I thought the same.”

Then Jo Gul got angry.

“What are you saying, Sasuk! We would never lose! That’s impossible.”

“Listen to me till the end.”

“Yes?”

Baek Cheon sighed and said.

“If we face the Tang family for the first time, we will definitely lose to them. We are not familiar with poison or hidden weapons. Haven’t we ever faced fighters like the Tang family before?”

“Th-that’s true, but...”

“We know the Tang family somewhat. Or maybe we were confident we did. But that’s the Tang family as allies, not as enemies.”

“...”

“Even though we can somewhat resist poison with the Heavenly Violet Pill, many fell in the first ambush. That means if we don’t prepare in advance, we can be defeated even when we could have avoided it.”

Jo Gul, who was the first among the Five Swords to be poisoned by a poison needle, quietly closed his mouth. There was no excuse.

“If we suddenly met the Tang family as enemies on the battlefield, do you really think we could have shown our true strength? The side suffering greater damage would definitely have been us.”

“Ugh.”

Jo Gul, lost for words to refute, groaned.

“So, are you saying we’re still weaker than the Tang family? Even though their elders didn’t participate?”

“It’s a bit different in meaning.”

“Yes?”

“We will definitely lose the first clash, but after fighting twice, we’ll improve, and after ten fights, I’m confident we’ll win. That is, if the Tang family doesn’t evolve from start to finish.”

Yoon Jong nodded.

“I understand what you mean.”

You lose because you don’t know your opponent. But once you get used to them, you can handle them.

At that moment, Yu Yiseol, who had been silently listening, suddenly stretched out her hand with her palm visible.

Everyone looked at her, puzzled by the strange gesture.

“Five times.”

“...”

“Five times is enough.”

A smile appeared on Baek Cheon’s lips.

“If the samae says so, five times will do.”

Tang Soso made a complicated and strange expression with those words. It seemed like a boast to adapt so easily to the poisonous and hidden weapon techniques of the Sichuan Tang family, but on the other hand, it didn’t sound completely wrong.

“As you all know, Chung Myung... that damn demon bastard... that useless stick, a cursed evil spirit crawling up from hell...”

“Calm down, Sasuk.”

“Ahem, yes. Anyway, none of the training that bastard orders is meaningless. This isn’t just about fighting to determine rank. It surely means to let us experience the Tang family’s poison and hidden weapon techniques.”

“...And also chaotic fights.”

“Yes, chaotic fights. And... it probably means to physically feel the clashes that occur when multiple sects are mixed in one place.”

Baek Cheon continued in a meaningful tone.

“Soon, we will have to experience that in a more intense form.”

At those words, the Mount Hua disciples instinctively thought of the Four Sects Alliance and Jang Ilso, their expressions becoming extremely serious. Then Jo Gul spoke.

“No, you’re thinking too positively. With that bastard’s personality, he might have just been annoyed and tormented us.”

“...”

“He probably found it convenient not to have to give orders here and there, and since everyone was dying, it was easier for him.”

Everyone looked back at him.

Then Jo Gul reflexively made a wronged expression.

“Are you going to say I’m wrong again?”

“...No. You have a point.”

“Very convincing indeed.”

“Actually, my gut feeling is closer to that side.”

Everyone sighed as if by agreement.

Of course, it’s something that shouldn’t happen logically, but isn’t it stranger to expect common sense from someone who charges at Jang Ilso in anger and attacks the Shaolin abbot?

“A-anyway...”

Baek Cheon cleared his throat and tried to manage the situation.

“The training we have to do from now on is clear, so now we prepare for that side...”

“From now on?”

“Huh?”

Jo Gul’s face twisted in anger.

“Are you saying we have to keep doing this from now on?”

“...”

“This crazy thing?”

“... I agree it’s crazy, but don’t we need experience to face the Shaolin?”

“Four Sects Alliance? Four Sects Alliance?”

Jo Gul said as if he couldn’t understand at all.

“No, Sasuk. If we keep doing this, the Heavenly Friends Alliance will collapse from internal strife before even facing the Four Sects Alliance.”

“...”

“Two days ago, the Tang family was our ally. But now?”

“Damn those poison dealers.”

“Cowardly bastards.”

“Country bumpkins from Sichuan.”

“I’ll kill them!”

Jo Gul scoffed.

“Look at this. I guarantee that in just a few more days, even eye contact will lead to stabbing. And if the Green Forest King and the easily angered Namgung’s young lord get involved, the Yangtze River will be dyed red in no time.”

“...”

“The Heavenly Friends Alliance is finished now. No dreams, no hope.”

Damn, today he keeps saying things that are right.

What a strange day...

Chapter 1114: Can I Do Well? (4)

“...Ugh. My body feels so battered everywhere.”

Tang Zhan rubbed his aching jaw. That damn Jo Gul’s strike still throbbed painfully.

“Damn bastard...”

Even Jo Gul’s fierce, deadly gaze was still vivid. That was definitely a punch thrown at him with genuine emotion.

Tang Zhan, grinding his teeth, screamed and clutched his jaw. Grinding his teeth only made the pain worse.

“Next time, I’ll definitely kill that bastard!”

Tang Zhan trembled with his fist, raw hatred exposed. He felt foolish for once having good feelings toward someone from his hometown.

“Hyung. Those Mount Hua bastards, they go too far... hyung?”

Tang Zhan casually spoke to Tang Pae but tilted his head in confusion. Tang Pae sat on the bed with a clearly serious expression, deep in thought.

“Hyung, are you alright?”

“Hmm.”

A low groan escaped from Tang Pae’s lips as he pondered.

“It hurts a lot, doesn’t it?”

“...”

“That person is really ruthless.”

At those words, Tang Pae unconsciously rubbed his eye socket. The eye where Yu Yiseol’s palm had struck was now black and bruised.

“Soso noona kept saying ‘Sago, sago’ while following, so I wondered why... Indeed, she is no ordinary person. So don’t make such a face. Sometimes you get hit in life.”

“That’s not it.”

“...Yes?”

Tang Pae suddenly frowned deeply.

Tang Zhan thought he was upset because he got hit by a woman. Of course, the Sichuan Tang Family basically does not teach martial arts to women of the family, so it is rare for them to be struck by women. So depending on perspective, it could be shocking.

But the reason Tang Pae was serious now was not that.

“Zhan.”

“Yes, hyung.”

“...Are you alright?”

“Me? I’m fine. My teeth feel a bit loose, but this much is nothing...”

“No, that’s not what I meant.”

Tang Pae shook his head.

“We lost again, didn’t you?”

“Ah, you mean that.”

Tang Zhan scratched his head.

“No. Well... we did lose, but if you think about it, it wasn’t really a loss, right? If we had used poison and secret techniques for combat, we would have definitely won.”

“Do you really think so?”

Tang Zhan was about to answer yes, but seeing Tang Pae’s expression, he closed his mouth. Tang Pae looked more serious than expected.

“Of course, if we had used our deadly poison and combat secret techniques without regard for the opponent’s damage, the situation might have improved. But wouldn’t Mount Hua be the same?”

“What do you mean by that...?”

“Because it was a sparring match, your jaw hurts and my eye is bruised. If it had been real combat, your neck would have been cut and my eye gouged out.”

Only then did Tang Zhan’s face turn serious as well. Tang Pae sighed deeply and spoke.

“They are swordsmen. Especially Mount Hua’s swordsmanship is said to be more vicious than the Evil Sect. Do you think they would have to strike a person twice in real combat?”

“...”

“The sword I saw on Plum Blossom Island was simpler and more brutal than any sect’s sword. If that sword had been wielded against us today... they wouldn’t have needed to waste time defeating us. The moment we entered the sword’s range, our lives would have ended.”

Tang Zhan could not refute that statement.

If today’s sparring had been a real combat, what he would have seen was not Jo Gul’s face grinning fiercely as he struck his jaw, but the cold back of Jo Gul walking away after cutting his neck.

Thinking that way sent a chill down his spine.

“Of course, if it had been real combat, they wouldn’t have been able to injure us. But... no matter how I think about it, I don’t believe the outcome would have changed.”

“...Hyung.”

“There is nothing more foolish than trying to deny what cannot be denied. The important thing is not to deny the facts but how to solve the problem. What do you think is the problem of the Sichuan Tang family now?”

Tang Zhan fell into thought at Tang Pae’s question. When trying to find the problem, there was more than one.

“There are many things that come to mind... but I think there is one decisive issue.”

“What is it?”

“Real combat experience.”

Tang Pae nodded strongly.

“I was thinking the same.”

There was a reason the two thought the same problem. After this sparring, they realized that the Tang family members had no real experience fighting certain other sects properly.

“...There was no such problem on Plum Blossom Island...”

“That was an illusion.”

At Tang Zhan’s grumble, Tang Pae spoke bitterly.

“Back then, we rejoiced that the Tang family’s dignity was displayed to the world... but looking back, it wasn’t the Tang family’s doing but Mount Hua’s greatness. They blocked all attacks aimed at the us, the Tang family and even cleared the path, didn’t they?”

“They even dealt with the water pirates targeting us underwater.”

“Yes. But we thought that was the skill of Tang family. It was like an archer boasting about his achievements while a comrade risked his life holding up a shield to protect him.”

At that harsh evaluation, Tang Zhan bit his lip slightly.

It was a cold assessment, but he could not refute it. He knew that the one who would feel worse hearing this was Tang Pae.

“Without experiencing real combat, you cannot find the problems. Only after this sparring did I see the problems the Alliance has.”

Tang Zhan nodded. Hearing that, he felt many things.

The Tang family is arguably the best sect in the world when fighting at a distance, but once the distance closes, they cannot demonstrate even half their skill.

The problem is that the battles they will face from now on are likely chaotic fights with enemies attacking from all sides. Without protection in such situations, can you not easily imagine what fate awaits them?

Tang Zhan, deep in thought with a stern face, suddenly raised his head and looked at Tang Pae.

“By the way, hyung.”

“Hmm?”

“Then... did Mount Hua’s Sword Saint anticipate all this and create this situation?”

“Probably.”

Tang Pae gave a bitter smile.

“It probably meant for us to experience and feel it ourselves. Mount Hua’s Sword Saint is unrivaled in real combat, so wouldn’t he have clearly seen the problems the Tang family has?”

“Even the family lord didn’t know... or maybe he did... but still, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint pointed out those parts first?”

“Don’t be mistaken.”

At that moment, Tang Pae’s voice lowered slightly.

“The family lord may be a stronger master than Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, but that is a separate matter from real combat experience.”

“...”

“While other sects were exposed to life-or-death battles, the Tang family always took a step back. Even during the Yangtze River disaster, where even the Nine Great Sects risked their lives, or the recent Hangzhou Ma Hua incident, the Tang family did not participate, did they?”

“...That’s true, but still.”

“In nearly ten years, the only real combat we’ve experienced was on Plum Blossom Island. But Mount Hua’s Sword Saint has been fighting enemies threatening his life for years. Their Main sect was attacked by the Ten Thousand People Clan, they fought the Demonic Cult in the North Sea, fought Jang Ilso in the middle of the Black Dragon Pirates, and now this.”

Tang Zhan felt dizzy and could not say a word.
