“Can’t you see over there? There?”

Hyun Jong, eyes half-rolled back, pointed fiercely to the side. Where he pointed, Chung Myung, now pale like a corpse, was slumped against the wall.

‘What, what is that... Has he starved himself for ten days?’

‘How did the shadows under his eyes reach down to his chin?’

‘Isn’t he going to die like that?’

“Is that even a human figure? That guy came laughing, walking on two feet after beating up the high priest! But look at how he looks now!”

“......”

“They’ve made someone else’s precious son look like that? These people, I swear!”

“P-please calm down, Sect Leader.”

Hyun Young, sweating nervously, stopped Hyun Jong who was rolling up his sleeves.

“Calm down? Do I look like I can calm down?”

Baek Cheon opened his mouth wide at this bizarre situation.

‘I am living to the day to see this. To think I’d see this kind of scene.’

Hyun Jong taking Chung Myung’s side and getting angry at others, while Hyun Young tries to calm him down—how could this make sense unless the world was turned upside down?

Hyun Jong pushed away the clinging Hyun Young and glared fiercely at Tang Gunak.

“Say something, Tang family lord!”

“K-khem.”

“I understand using poison in duels! But! They used poison not in a duel but because of a quarrel? Are they trying to pick a fight with Mount Hua now?”

“Th-that part I cannot comment on. But that poison wasn’t the absolute poison and I already revoked...”

“Ah, so you think beating someone up and then treating them later makes it okay?”

“That’s not what I meant, but...”

Tang Gunak, seemingly at a loss for words, averted his gaze. Surely he didn’t expect poison to be used during a fight in the diner, not a duel.

If this had happened with another sect, it wouldn’t be surprising if it ended in a fight to the death. But since Mount Hua was the victim, it was overlooked to this extent.

“And, Beast Palace Lord!”

“Yes, Alliance Leader.”

“How on earth did you manage those beasts to let them rampage into villages like this!”

“Ahahaha!”

Meng So laughed heartily as if it was nothing.

“That’s why they’re called beasts, aren’t they? If they knew how to be controlled, they’d be humans.”

But Hyun Jong had no intention of letting that joke slide. With bloodshot eyes, he openly glared at Meng So.

“Are you laughing right now?”

Meng So lowered his gaze slightly.

“Well said! If you know beasts are like that, you should manage them! What were the Palace people doing just watching the beasts run wild?”

“I have no comment on that...”

“Those bastards went into villages, ate all the food people had, and devoured the livestock. Chung Myung even got his eardrum damaged while protesting! His eardrum!”

“He looks fine though...”

“What?”

“Ah, nothing.”

Meng So shrugged his shoulders, uncharacteristically small for his large frame. They say gentle people are scarier when angry, and this was exactly that.

“And!”

Hyun Jong’s fiery gaze now fixed on Im So-Byeong. But at that moment, Im So-Byeong took the initiative.

“Wait a moment!”

“...Hmm?”

He confidently opened his fan wide.

“It seems you’re blaming us, but if you look closely, aren’t we the ones wronged?”

“Did you just say you’re wronged?”

“Yes! This time, those Tang guys started the quarrel first. Even though we’re lowly Evil Sect bastards, isn’t it their fault for provoking and spitting first? Should we just take the hits?”

“Well...”

Hyun Jong hesitated as if at a loss for words, and Im So-Byeong seized the opportunity to press on.

“Alliance Leader, aren’t we the ones who decided to wash our hands clean and live properly? Then you should embrace us with kindness, not keep discriminating against us and Tang guys...”

“That’s not it!”

“...Yes?”

“Officials came and went!”

“...Officials?”

Im So-Byeong tilted his head as if puzzled by the sudden remark. His confidently spread fan drooped slightly.

Hyun Jong openly showed his displeasure and continued speaking.

“Yes. Recently, there was a brawl at a gambling den in the back alleys of river mouth. The gamblers beat up all the staff and even robbed the safe before fleeing.”

“...Ah, no!”

Im So-Byeong shouted with a face full of injustice.

“What does that have to do with us?”

“...They say the ones involved wore green clothes with a tree symbol on the right chest.”

“Wow, they wore that? Crazy bastards...”

Bang!

Hyun Jong slammed the table with all his might.

“They came to help the commoners and then gamble? Gamble? And fine, gambling is one thing! Why rob a perfectly fine gambling den? Why!”

Im So-Byeong gave an embarrassed smile.

“Well... we didn’t rob innocent commoners. If it’s the gambling den operators, they’re like leeches sucking the blood of the commoners, so robbing them a bit...”

“Then you should have shared the money! That money all came from the commoners! What happened to that money?”

“...I’ll look into it.”

“Bah!”

Hyun Jong raised his hand, and Im So-Byeong ducked as if something was flying at him, then sheepishly raised his head.

Hyun Jong had hit his own chest. The pounding hand was full of frustration as if his insides were burning.

“Ice Palace!”

“S-sorry.”

Han Yi-Myung, already half intimidated, slowly backed away as soon as the word ‘Ice Palace’ was mentioned.

“Why isn’t the Palace Lord coming?”

“...There are some circumstances he has...”

“Ugh.”

Hyun Jong sighed deeply and glared at Han Yi-Myung again.

“Let’s say other places are like that. You asked not to fight unless during duels, but how can even Ice Palace be fighting? You said you’d listen, but I heard Ice Palace attacked Beast Palace first! What’s going on here?”

“Th-that has a story behind it.”

“What story?”

Han Yi-Myung answered, sweating profusely.

“Actually, our Palace Lord did his best to prevent private fights as requested by taoist Chung Myung. Yesterday, upon hearing a quarrel between Beast Palace and the Ice Palace, he rushed to mediate.”

“The Palace Lord tried to stop it?”

“Yes! You know our Palace Lord wouldn’t break Mount Hua’s Sword Saint’s request. Of course, he tried to stop it!”

“...Then what happened? I clearly heard Ice Palace attacked Beast Palace first.”

“Well... our Palace Lord told both sides to stop, saying ‘Taoist Chung Myung said not to fight.’ But one reckless Beast Palace member said in front of the Palace Lord, ‘I don’t care what that Taoist said,’ so...”

“...So?”

Han Yi-Myung squeezed his eyes shut.

“Well... isn’t it obvious? Our Palace Lord, enraged by the ‘Taoist’ remark, beat that Beast Palace member without question...”

Hearing that, Five Swords applauded.

“Kuh. Our So-Baek has really grown.”

“He even beat a Beast Palace member. Wow, his skills must have improved a lot...”

“Shut up, you guys!”

Hyun Jong’s roar made Five Swords shrink his shoulders. Taking a deep breath, Hyun Jong barely suppressed his anger and asked.

“So that’s why he didn’t come here?”

“Ah, no. He went wild and got beaten up badly, so he’s bedridden now.”

“B-beat the Palace Lord?”

Hyun Jong blinked and looked at Meng So, who awkwardly rolled his eyes and scratched his nose bridge.

“He was so young, we didn’t realize he was the Palace Lord...”

“Didn’t you see him before today?”

“Well... I only saw him briefly...”

They really didn’t recognize him well. Those crazy Beast Palace guys.

“So? Anyway, every sect fought without exception?”

“...Sorry.”

“We have no excuse to say.”

“Meanwhile, the beasts raided villages, and the back alleys where beasts didn’t go were robbed by bandits?”

“Ahem.”

“Ehem...”

“Even if the Four Sects Alliance’s main base was here, this chaos wouldn’t happen! Hey, you...”

“Hey! Sect Leader! Sect Leader!”

“Decorum! You must keep your dignity!”

Hyun Sang and Hyun Young hurriedly covered Hyun Jong’s mouth from both sides. It was extremely rude, but they couldn’t let the Heavenly Friends Alliance leader say those words.

While stopping Hyun Jong, Hyun Young spoke sharply instead.

“How much chaos must have been caused for Chung Myung to lose his mind! I’ve never seen him bedridden before! Do you even have any sense or not!”

Everyone subtly turned their heads to look at Chung Myung.

Seeing him look like he’d given up on everything, a subtle guilt arose, and everyone grimaced.

“Can’t you apologize quickly?”

“Ahem. Sorry, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.”

“Sorry, Taoist.”

“...I’m sorry.”

Hearing the apologies, Chung Myung smiled broadly. His face looked like he was dying, and the bright smile gave off a creepy feeling, but a smile was still a smile...

“Just go and die, you bastards!”

“Ah! Chung Myung!”

“Hold on, Chung Myung!”

“They are sect leaders! You crazy bastard!”

Chung Myung suddenly rolled his eyes and started rampaging, and Five Swords immediately rushed in to pin him down.

“Let go! Sect leader? Sect leaders should go freeze and die! What kind of sect leaders are those!”

“C-calm down!”

Chung Myung even foamed at the mouth and convulsed.

“I won’t do it! I won’t, you bastards! Heavenly Friends Alliance or whatever, I’ll do it all myself! I won’t do it!”

The desperate scream of one crushed by the disaster he brought upon himself echoed mournfully... truly mournfully throughout the manor.

Chapter 1130: This Place, This Is Hell. (5)

Chung Myung’s rampage was subdued only after Hyun Young and even Hyun Jong stepped in. Seeing Chung Myung collapsed in the corner, tightly bound with ropes, cold sweat ran down the backs of the sect leaders.

Hyun Jong looked at everyone as if he had a headache.

“Why is it that you all cannot control this?”

“......”

“We agreed to overlook fighting during sparring matches. But isn’t it a big problem that they fight each other even in daily life?”

Everyone nodded as if they agreed with that statement.

But Hyun Jong frowned as if he disliked that reaction even more.

“Then each sect leader should sort out the situation. Why do these incidents keep happening?”

“Ahem.”

The sect leaders all avoided the question silently.

“Please speak frankly.”

“Well, the thing is...”

“Ahem...”

Despite Hyun Jong’s urging, the sect leaders only coughed repeatedly. Seeing Hyun Jong’s incredulous expression, Tang Gunak finally spoke with a bitter smile.

“This seems to be a problem arising from differences in understanding...”

“You say it’s a difference in understanding?”

What could they possibly misunderstand here?

“The Alliance leader thinks that if we just want, controlling them would be easy... but in fact, our authoritative figure is not as strong as the Alliance leader thinks.”

“...What?”

Hyun Jong blinked blankly upon hearing that.

What nonsense is this?

“How can places like Namgung or Tang family have weak authority? What do you mean? The prestigious families and the Five Palaces Beyond the Great Wall I know have sect leaders with absolute authority.”

Tang Gunak sighed deeply.

“That’s the problem.”

“What?”

“...The problem lies in the perception of authority. Who has the strongest authority in the world?”

“Well... the Emperor.”

“Even that Emperor cannot ignore the feelings of his people, right?”

Hyun Jong was rendered speechless. Because it was true.

Even the Emperor with the greatest authority in the world watches his people’s mood and tries to win their favor.

“Of course, we can suppress them once or twice with our power. But if grievances accumulate and are not resolved, and the same things repeat, authority inevitably weakens.”

“Ah, no, but what exactly...”

Hyun Jong looked at the other sect leaders as if he couldn’t understand, but they only nodded as if agreeing.

“Are you serious now?”

When Hyun Jong asked again, Tang Gunak sighed deeply.

“That’s why it was difficult to say. It’s not something the Alliance leader can easily understand.”

Hyun Jong was silent for a moment, scratching his head. He thought it was a sensitive matter to push too hard.

“...Even so, saying it’s awkward to order disciples not to fight...”

“Sect leader.”

“Yes.”

“Is the authority of the Tang family Lord stronger? Or the Shaolin abbot’s authority?”

“Well...”

Hyun Jong could not answer easily.

It might not be pleasant for Tang Gunak to hear, but comparing the Tang family lord and the Shaolin’s abbot was impossible. The Shaolin abbot was not just the head of Shaolin but recognized as a representative figure in the murim world.

Naturally, his authority within the sect was very strong.

“You must have heard before? That Shaolin disciples rebelled against the abbot’s order and left to return to the main temple.”

“...I have heard, but...”

He thought it was just karma... no, well... even Shaolin has some who think for themselves.

“Think about it, sect leader. Even the Shaolin abbot, who has the strongest authority in the murim world, faces such things. If disciples dislike some decisions, they rebel and return to the main temple, don’t they?”

“...”

“In that case, what great power do we have to command disciples with just a gesture?”

Hyun Jong’s mouth slowly opened.

It seemed to make sense... but also didn’t.

Seeing Hyun Jong’s obvious confusion, Tang Gunak smiled bitterly.

“So, Alliance leader... no, sect leader, I said it would be hard for you to understand.”

“...Why?”

“The reason is simple. Our positions differ. The Mount Hua I know is a sect where the sect leader’s authority is extraordinarily strong in the murim world.”

“Mount Hua?”

“Yes.”

“Mount Hua?”

“...Yes.”

“In Mount Hua?”

Hyun Jong made a face as if asking, ‘What nonsense is this?’ and the sect leaders all smiled bitterly.

Of course, it might sound strange to Hyun Jong. Unlike other sect leaders, he treats his disciples like friends. So sometimes Mount Hua disciples speak and act toward Hyun Jong in ways unimaginable in other sects.

But friendliness and strong authority are completely different matters.

To Tang Gunak, Mount Hua is a sect where the sect leader’s authority surpasses normal imagination.

Imagine a situation where each sect leader sends disciples to a hopeless battle line. A fight with no justification or benefit.

Would the Tang family members willingly risk their lives following Tang Gunak’s orders?

Well, Tang Gunak was skeptical.

At that moment, they would prioritize their own lives over Tang Gunak’s orders. Tang Gunak’s power comes from legitimacy and position, not from faith and trust in the family lord.

Probably, the other sect leaders here think similarly.

But Mount Hua, Mount Hua alone is different.

Mount Hua disciples, if ordered by Hyun Jong, more than half would risk their lives even knowing they might die.

‘That is real authority.’

Authority is like that. No matter how proud you are usually, if you cannot exert it at critical moments, it is not true authority.

Hyun Jong found it very hard to understand but nodded somehow.

“Ugh. So... you mean it’s hard to stop disciples because they might rebel?”

“Hmm. It’s a bit embarrassing to say.”

“What’s embarrassing about it?”

At that moment, Im So-Byeong opened his mouth with a face saying, ‘This is why the Justice faction guys...’

“You guys just get cursed at or cold stares. For us, if things go wrong, knives fly while we sleep, knives!”

“...”

“And you guys have the strongest rules, so even if kids rise up, you can suppress them. But a guy like me becomes a corpse in an instant! A corpse. Do you understand?”

“You’re already half a corpse though?”

“...Still, being half a corpse is better than a full corpse.”

Im So-Byeong grumbled and continued indifferently.

“There are only two sects in the world where you can make disciples do what they dislike with just a finger snap. One is Mount Hua, and the other is...”

“Shaolin?”

“What? Shaolin? No way. It’s the Four Sects Alliance! If I had to add one more, it would be the Demonic Cult.”

Hyun Jong’s face twisted strangely upon hearing this.

“Well... depending on how you hear it, it sounds like Mount Hua is like the Four Sects Alliance or the Demon Cult...”

“Honestly, there’s not much difference. When the sect leader orders, everyone gets bloodshot eyes and rushes in without thinking whether it’s the north or south of the river. What sane person would do that? It’s not normal.”

Hyun Jong was silent. He felt very wronged but found it hard to argue. It had been a long time since he felt like this, since Chung Myung spouted his sophistry.

He sighed and asked Im So-Byeong again.

“So, the conclusion is that even the sect leaders find it hard to stop them?”

“Even if we try to stop it, as soon as things cool down, someone pours oil on the fire. How can we stop that? If we touch it clumsily, everything burns.”

“Even the one who brought the fire got burned.”

“That one got burned badly.”

The sect leaders shook their heads looking at Chung Myung tied in the corner. To them, it was like pouring oil on oneself, setting fire, and then screaming in pain.

“Then how on earth do we...”

At that moment, Han Yi-Myung spoke with a face full of confusion.

“Honestly, I don’t know why we’re having this meeting. If sects could easily mix, why would we divide by sect names and distinguish disciples by clothing? Why do meaningless joint training, which is just a brawl disguised as sparring?”

Everyone looked at Han Yi-Myung with empty eyes.

‘We wonder that too.’

‘When did we understand all that? We just did as told.’

‘That guy came from far away and doesn’t understand the situation at all.’

Not reading the other sect leaders’ expressions, Han Yi-Myung spoke irritably.

“I dare not say this in front of many sect leaders, but if we just stop this training and separate each sect’s quarters, wouldn’t this problem end...”

“Hoorah!”

At that moment, Chung Myung snapped the ropes binding him and jumped up.

“What? Separate?”

“...”

“Where are you saying such weak words!”

The sect leaders all nodded as if impressed.

The one suffering the most from this situation is none other than Chung Myung. Yet now, he refuses to take the easy way out no matter what.

That stubbornness is the driving force that has brought Mount Hua this far, the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint’s spirit...

“Then I’m the loser! Damn it!”

...It wasn’t power. Uh... just a bad personality.

“Ugh. Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.”

Tang Gunak sighed deeply.

“I understand your feelings, but the situation isn’t that simple. The barely calmed situation was completely twisted when Beast Palace and Ice Palace intervened. I know your enthusiasm, but sometimes excessive enthusiasm can be poison. Let’s calm things down first...”

“Calm down?”

“...”

“Who? Them? Or me?”

“Uh...”

Originally, I was going to say to calm them down... but it seems you need to calm down a bit too...

“Okay. I understand what you mean.”

“Huh?”

“So, this way won’t do, right?”

“...”

“Well...”

It’s true... but I think you completely misunderstood what I said...

“I get it. That’s right. I backed down.”

Chung Myung’s eyes began to gleam with madness.

“Then I’ll do it properly.”

“...What are you going to do?”

“What do you think?”

“...”

“Do you really want to know?”

No. I really don’t want to know. Tang Gunak was disgusted to know.

Chung Myung started giggling like a madman.

“I must have lost my mind. Since when did I live so patiently! This isn’t right!”

“...”

“Don’t worry! I’ll make them perfectly united!”

“...”

Cold sweat began to seep down the sect leaders’ spines. They had a creeping feeling they were about to witness something terrible.

Chapter 1131: Let’s Try To Get Along Together! (1)

Confucius once said, “If you befriend the upright, you yourself will become upright; if you associate with the wicked, you yourself will become wicked.”

In other words, a person’s character is influenced not only by themselves but also by the friends around them.

And if that is true, then those gathered in the Heavenly Friends Alliance now are clearly the worst kinds of people who should never be considered friends.

“...You bastard...”

“Today, I will definitely kill you.”

The Five Swords walked toward the training ground with faces full of malice. Their eyes were filled with deadly intent.

“I thought your mouth was just frozen shut.”

“No. Did we burn the pavilion? The Green Forest did! Then the Green Forest should be kicked out, so why are we the ones being caught?”

“Exactly! If they want to make us sleep outside, they should just send out those Ice Palace bastards. They sleep wearing only their underclothes and still complain about the heat, leaving windows and doors wide open!”

“Wow, they open the doors? Then they should sleep outside themselves!”

Baek Cheon shook his head from side to side.

“Enough.”

A fierce fire ignited in his eyes.

“Even if I don’t care about the others, today we’re really burying those Green Forest bastards. These crazy bastards even set fire to the pavilion?”

“Obviously, the Green Forest King ordered this.”

“He should freeze to death, Green Forest King. Just call him a bandit bastard!”

Mount Hua already has psychological scars from fires, especially fires in the pavilion. Thanks to the fair fire that Chung Myung mentioned, Mount Hua narrowly avoided being completely burned down.

“Knowing that and still setting the fire? Are they really human?”

Bloodshot veins appeared in Baek Cheon’s eyes.

He suffered nightmares all night because of Im So-Byeong’s face laughing gleefully as the pavilion burned. That expression seemed to shout, “We’re not trying to win the fight, just to piss off the Mount Hua bastards,” which made his stomach churn even more.

“Im So-Byeong...”

Baek Cheon gritted his teeth, but Jo Gul seemed to think differently.

“No, is this the time to only talk about the Green Forest King? Aren’t the Tang Family bastards more of a problem?”

Jo Gul rolled his eyes and passionately argued.

“No, those crazy bastards are thoughtless bandits who, if they set fire to the pavilion, should at least try to put it out together. But they threw poison bombs on top of the fire?”

“...”

“If it weren’t for that poisonous smoke, we could have put out the fire and saved the pavilion somehow. Don’t you remember the face of that Tang Family bastard admiring the pink poisonous smoke rising, calling it a spectacle?”

“It was quite a spectacle.”

“Kind of like a plum blossom field.”

“Yeah, it was pretty.”

“What are you agreeing to, you crazy bastards!”

“...Jo Gul, calm down. After all, it’s our sasuk.”

“Huh? Sahyung? Why aren’t you hitting today?”

“...It’s hard for you to understand, but coincidentally, I was thinking the same as you.”

Yoon Jong shook his head and continued.

“...And the poisonous smoke aside, if the beasts of the Beast Palace hadn’t rampaged so much, we would have put out the fire before the pavilion was completely burned. But these so-called sacred beasts had their tails on fire and were running wild. What could we do? We’re not Baek Ah.”

“Come to think of it, what happened to Baek Ah? I didn’t see her.”

“She collapsed from exhaustion while desperately trying to control the beasts...”

“So she fell ill? Poor thing...”

“No. She’s mostly recovered from exhaustion, but when she heard the beasts were rampaging, she got pissed and was taken away by Chung Myung.”

“...What does that have to do with anything?”

“If a beast causes trouble, Baek Ah is always held responsible.”

Everyone squeezed their eyes shut. From Baek Ah’s perspective, it must be so unfair. No matter how great she is, she’s just a marten, and what can she do when beasts the size of houses catch fire and go crazy?

This isn’t a matter of ability but of innate size, but the problem is that such excuses won’t work on Chung Myung.

“Amitabha. May he rest in paradise...”

“He’s not dead yet!”

But Hae Yeon shook his head coldly.

“It’s just an empty obsession. How can someone taken by the devil return alive? Next time we see her, she’ll probably be a nice scarf.”

“...How did such a person come out of Buddhism?”

“When I first saw him, he was definitely shy and kind.”

“Then it’s our fault.”

“Actually, that’s hard to deny.”

Everyone became solemn before Mount Hua’s original sin(?). Just by looking at Hae Yeon alone, one can understand the resentment the court holds against Mount Hua and the deeds committed because of it.

From the moment they lured the genius martial artist who would lead Shaolin’s next generation into becoming a drunken monk who only sought liquor at night, the relationship between Mount Hua and Shaolin became irreparable.

“Anyway, it was like hell.”

Baek Cheon shook his head in disgust.

The bandits who set fire to the pavilion, and the Mount Hua guys who screamed and ran around like wild boars shocked by lightning upon seeing the fire.

And the Tang Family bastards who laughed while throwing poison bombs into the pavilion as the Mount Hua guys panicked trying to put out the fire, and the Beast Palace members sweating profusely trying to catch the flaming sacred beasts.

All the chaos and evil in the world were mixed that night. If Asura had seen it, he would surely have shaken his head and muttered, “Master, you’re progressing too fast.”

“...Come to think of it, where were the Ice Palace people then? Their Ice Cold Qi Technique would have helped put out the fire.”

“They ran away without even looking back, saying it was too hot just being near the fire.”

Baek Cheon’s tightly closed lips trembled.

‘How is it that none of them are sane?’

It’s hard to say. Were all the gathered people insane from the start, or do normal sects lose their minds once they join the Heavenly Friends Alliance?

“Anyway, I’ll really kill them all.”

Everyone gritted their teeth.

“...This time, I agree.”

“Me too.”

“Brother or not, I’ll stab a big needle right in the middle of their faces!”

Mount Hua’s disciples, burning with fighting spirit, headed to the training ground. And sure enough, other sects had already set up camp there.

“Oh dear.”

As the Five Swords appeared, Im So-Byeong leisurely waved his fan. Though his face looked more gaunt and corpse-like these days, trying to act relaxed only made him look like someone about to die.

“I wonder if our Taoists slept well last night.”

Gnash!

The sound of Mount Hua disciples grinding their teeth echoed like music. Seeing this, Im So-Byeong chuckled.

Watching the culprit who set fire to the dormitory, a hellfire also blazed in the hearts of Mount Hua disciples. It was a fire that wouldn’t go out unless they caught and burned that bastard to death.

“You have a hole in your mouth!”

“Hahaha. Couldn’t sleep... cough cough cough Sleep... gag!”

Im So-Byeong coughed as if about to spit his lungs out. Each cough sprayed red blood everywhere, causing the nearby bandits to step back with uneasy expressions.

“Cough, cough. Sleep... sleep... cough.”

“...That guy will die even if we just leave him alone.”

“Just leave him? Feels like we’re just making a corpse out of him for no reason.”

“No way. Even if he dies, it has to be by our hands. Beat him to death before he dies a natural death.”

“As expected of the sasuk. Second place in Mount Hua’s personality rankings.”

“What? I’m second? What about Jo Gul?”

Even Baek Cheon couldn’t bring himself to claim he was below third place.

At that moment, several people approached, chuckling. It seemed that not only the Green Forest and Im So-Byeong wanted to mock Mount Hua’s misfortune from yesterday.

“Oh dear, you came out wearing those singed and torn clothes. How undignified.”

“Looks like Mount Hua has no money.”

“The leftover clothes from yesterday are all burned up, so what can they do? Tsk tsk tsk. They should have been careful with fire day and night.”

Baek Cheon glared fiercely at those laughing mockingly.

“You’re really... excited, aren’t you?”

“Isn’t there really no reason to be depressed?”

“Right. We had a good fire-watching.”

“And a good people-watching.”

“Oh, is that so?”

Baek Cheon tilted his neck and grabbed the sword at his waist.

“But what should I do? Those eyes that enjoyed the fire-watching so much might not be in good shape soon.”

“Oh dear. These days, Taoists even threaten people? If you can, try it.”

“You don’t think I can?”

There was no need for long words anymore.

They were already in a relationship where warm fists built up malice and gentle sword strokes piled up grudges. Now, just making eye contact without talking was enough to launch a killing move—a close(?) relationship.

True friendship is not built on words. Now, they prove their friendship through actions rather than worthless words.

“Kill them all!”

“Tang Zhan bastard, today is your funeral...”

Kraaaaaaang!

“Aaaaaaah!”

At that moment, someone flew toward the blue sky at lightning speed.

“What, what is that?”

“Oh dear, Jo Gul!”

“J-Jo Gul sahyung!”

Mount Hua disciples instinctively flinched seeing Jo Gul spinning through the air and the red trail he left in the sky. Other sect disciples who had intended to strike Jo Gul were momentarily stunned and stopped.

‘What is this?’

No one had gone towards Mount Hua yet? The confused looked blinked and looked at where Jo Gul had been. Instead of Jo Gul, a familiar figure stood there.

“Chung... Myung?”

“Mount Hua’s Sword Saint?”

“No, why...”

Drip drip drip.

The blood Jo Gul scattered in the air fell like drizzle. From within it, Chung Myung smiled gently.

Baek Cheon’s heart dropped at that moment.

‘Da-dangerous...’

His mouth smiled, but his eyes stiffened awkwardly. His eyebrows twitched slightly. This was the highest level of danger signal.

“Ch-Chung Myung! I don’t know what’s going on, but... please calm down first...”

“Calm down?”

Chung Myung smiled even brighter. Baek Cheon’s face began to turn pale. At the same time, the perceptive Yu Yiseol quietly stepped back.

Chung Myung spoke.

“No, no. Sasuk, I think there’s some misunderstanding. I didn’t come here with bad intentions.”

“Is that so?”

A reasonable person would ask why he beat Jo Gul then, but at this moment, no one dared to say that. Actually, whether Jo Gul lives or dies is none of their concern...

“I just wanted to ask a bit.”

“Huh? Ask what?”

“Well... lately there’s been talk that sasuk and sahyungs aren’t sparring but fighting. So I wanted to check. Are you fighting now?”

Baek Cheon’s face turned pale. Whether Chung Myung caused all this or not was meaningless now.

“That can’t be.”

“No way!”

“Hey! That’s nonsense, Taoist!”

“W-Who said that? We’re this close!”

“It’s sparring, sparring! Just sparring!”

Those who knew Chung Myung quickly agreed. The Tang Family and Mount Hua, who had been cursing each other as if to kill, now laughed arm in arm like best friends, and the Green Forest also nervously shook hands with the Namgung Family.

It was a historic moment of harmony between the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families, the Evil Sect and the Justice faction, which seemed impossible forever. But the true meaning behind it was utterly tragic.

“Oh, really? Then was I mistaken?”

“Y-Yes!”

“This is all done for each other’s good.”

“There’s no selfishness at all! Hahaha!”

“That’s right, Taoist!”

Even Yoon Jong and Hae Yeon were busy sweating nervously and making excuses.

But when there are many people, there are always those who lack tact.

“No, why are you suddenly pretending to be close? Damn bastards!”

“Who said we were pretending to be close to you! You barbarians!”

“What, you bastard?”

The heads of those belonging to the four sects of central plains turned sharply. The Ice Palace and Beast Palace, not understanding the situation at all, bared their teeth at each other and fought again.

‘Shut up!’

‘Please be more aware!’

‘I’ll definitely kill those bastards later!’

Just when no one dared to speak.

“Ah, so you’re not fighting. Then you’re close, right?”

“Y-Yes!”

“Of course!”

“Hey. Now we can’t live without each other.”

Chung Myung nodded as if satisfied.

“Yes. In the process of getting close, you fight sometimes. Fighting builds strong bonds, right?”

“Exactly!”

“Hahaha. We couldn’t be closer than this even if we wanted.”

The Tang Family and Namgung Do-Wei desperately made up words. Then Chung Myung spoke.

“So I felt a bit left out.”

“...Huh?”

He grinned and licked his lips.

“Come to think of it, I’m the same rank, but everyone is getting close without me. Isn’t that right?”

“N-No way!”

“I’m the closest to the Taoist!”

“That’s not true at all, really!”

“Hey. Don’t just say polite things. So from now on, I’ll try to hang out and get closer too.”

“T-taoist. Wait...”

Chung Myung shook his head enthusiastically.

“So. Then.”

Everyone there saw.

Mount Hua’s Sword Saint. The greatest enemy of the Demonic Cult who defeated the high priest. From the eyes of this great man, a murderous aura poured out that even the Demon couldn’t emit.

Boom!

When Chung Myung stomped the ground, a spiderweb-like crack appeared with a cracking sound.

“Come on, you bastards, let’s try to get along together!”

He rolled his eyes back and charged forward.

“Eeeeeek!”

Everyone was terrified. As the enraged tiger charged, the pitiful(?) sheep’s desperate screams began to fill the training ground.

Chapter 1132: Let’s Try To Get Along Together! (2)

In later days, the members of the Beast Palace recalled that day like this.

“They say a tiger jumped into a flock of sheep, but actually, tigers don’t cause such a fuss just by targeting sheep.”

“That’s right, exactly.”

“Usually, when a tiger comes, the sheep rush to one side, and the tiger just takes one sheep from the edge of the group and that’s it.”

“In the first place, predators rarely attack animals they don’t even eat on purpose.”

“That’s right... definitely so.”

So the scene of that day could not be explained with such words.

“If I had to find something similar...”

“Can you really find something similar to that?”

“No. It wouldn’t be similar... but when I tried to find words to explain to those remaining in the palace, there was only one thing.”

“What is it?”

“Once, I saw a weasel crawl into a chicken coop. That weasel was so ferocious that it killed all the chickens, even though it didn’t eat them.”

“That’s right. Weasels are like that.”

“Meanwhile, the chickens were trapped inside the coop and couldn’t escape... it was truly chaos.”

“...That’s right.”

“It was exactly like that. We were the chickens...”

“Is Mount Hua’s Sword Saint the weasel?”

“No... now that I think about it, this analogy is a bit wrong. Comparing him to a weasel is just not right. Um, yeah. It was just Mount Hua’s Sword Saint invading the chicken coop.”

“...”

“I still have nightmares about that day. The demonic Mount Hua’s Sword Saint rolling his eyes and charging at me...”

“S-stop it. Please stop...”

“I still seem to hear the screams from that day. Like auditory hallucinations...”

“Kyaaaaaaaaah!”

The disciples of Mount Hua are brave.

No one can deny that fact. Mount Hua was the sect that mobilized the most people to the dangerous southern rivers, and those who stood firm against the feared high priest of the demonic cult were also from Mount Hua.

In fact, even without such examples, no one would deny that Mount Hua is the bravest sect in the murim world now... more so, a sect with guts that burst out of their chests.

But those brave disciples of Mount Hua are now screaming as if they’ve seen ghosts and are scrambling to flee.

“Get out of the way!”

“No! Sasuk! What will happen if Sasuk runs away?”

“Shut up, you bastard! Do you think I have two lives?”

A long-time victim... no, having endured Chung Myung for a long time, they know better than anyone what state he is in now. When Chung Myung is like that, there is no way to deal with it. If you want to live, you have to run first.

But unfortunately, their backs were also too familiar to Chung Myung.

Kwahaaang!

“Aaaaaaah!”

“Eek!”

Baek Cheon, startled by the desperate screams behind him, instinctively rolled his body on the ground. At that moment, something fierce brushed past over his head.

“Y-you intend to kill me... Gyaaak!”

Before he could finish his words, Chung Myung struck Baek Cheon’s jaw upward. Baek Cheon flew into the air in a perfectly straight posture.

Even while flying from the blow, his dignified posture naturally drew admiration as ‘That’s Baek Cheon.’ Of course, the one being hit wouldn’t be happy to hear that.

Having sent Baek Cheon flying with one strike, Chung Myung’s bloodshot eyes gleamed as he searched for the next victim. Beast-like sounds escaped from his mouth.

Yoon Jong lowered his stance and cautiously reached out one hand.

“Ch-Chung Myung. L-let’s calm down first, okay? Be good...”

“Calm down?”

“Uh...”

“Caaaalm doooown?”

“Eek?”

Chung Myung’s foot slammed right into the center of Yoon Jong’s face. Unable to even scream, Yoon Jong spun like a well-hit top and was thrown beyond the Tang Family and the Green Forest to the corner of the estate wall.

Shiiiiii.

White steam rose from Yoon Jong’s fallen face. Seeing the clear footprints on his face sent chills down everyone’s spine.

“Baek Cheon’s and Yoon Jong’s stances...”

“In one blow...”

Those present here had fought fiercely for many days already. They knew each other’s abilities very well.

Baek Cheon and Yoon Jong could no longer be called mere skilled disciples. If thrown into the murim world right now, they would be considered peak masters at least.

But regardless of their skill, their fate was equal before Chung Myung.

“Ugh, why me...”

At that moment, Jo Gul, regaining consciousness and sitting up, looked around with dazed eyes. Puzzled by the strange atmosphere, he suddenly froze. His gaze met Chung Myung’s, whose eyes were almost rolled back.

A subtle silence fell.

Jo Gul smiled faintly.

“Ah... I should have just died earlier.”

And at that moment, Chung Myung flew like a tiger, pouncing on Jo Gul and mercilessly throwing punches.

“You guys!”

“Kyaaaaah!”

“Are you human? Are you human?”

Fist-like boulders rained down on his face. Jo Gul’s head spun wildly.

“Die! Die, you bastards! Die!”

As his head spun and his view changed, he alternately saw the terrified faces looking this way and Chung Myung’s eyes rolled back.

“Huh...”

Even as his consciousness faded, a small smile lingered on Jo Gul’s lips. He saw Yoon Jong unconscious nearby, also faintly smiling.

Of course, it wasn’t madness or losing his mind.

“You guys... are all done for now...”

Until now, only Mount Hua suffered, but if others suffered together, it would be worth dying for.

“Just... try experiencing it once...”

Even with his face swollen, Jo Gul smiled happily before his head lolled sideways. Chung Myung slowly stood up, leaving Jo Gul sprawled.

Drop.

A drop of blood pooled at his fist’s tip fell to the floor. That small sound spread widely in the silent training ground.

“...If you were human.”

Those staring blankly at unconscious Jo Gul shuddered and turned their gaze to Chung Myung.

“There should still be some decency.”

“...”

“Set fire?”

“That’s Green Forest...”

“Poison the food?”

“That’s the Tang Family. I saw it!”

“The palace lord was the first to fall ill after fighting, and those who dragged beasts here couldn’t even manage them, letting them raid villages?”

“...That’s Ice Palace.”

“Beast Palace...”

“Are you humans?”

Of course, even with ten mouths, there’s nothing to say.

No matter how much Chung Myung caused the situation, everyone was responsible for letting it escalate. Just because the higher-ups relaxed, if you spread a blanket and hold the game pieces there, you can’t complain if you get wrapped up in that blanket.

“I was short-sighted. I thought you were at least human. Because you’re human, I thought you’d behave like humans. But... you weren’t human?”

“...”

“Then there’s a fitting way. In the past! The great sect leader of Mount Hua said this.”

“W-what did he say...?”

“To dogs, madmen, and disobedient disciples, the rod is medicine!”

I never said that to dogs and madmen!

“Shut up!”

Though no one made a sound, Chung Myung suddenly shouted into the air and charged toward Tang Pae nearby.

“S-stop him!”

“Protect the young lord!”

The Tang Family’s guards shouted in panic. They threw needles and sprayed poisonous smoke at the charging Chung Myung.

“Uraaaah!”

Chung Myung drew his sword and spun it like a windmill as he surged forward. The strong wind pressure pushed away the poison smoke, and the needles bounced off in all directions.

“Eek!”

Tang Pae, pale as a sheet, hurriedly retreated and scattered hidden weapons from his sleeves wherever he could.

“Ah! Poison Sting! Feather Needle! Ghost King’s order! Blood Sucker! Ah! Again! What else! Ah! Seven...”

Snap.

But despite his struggles, Chung Myung reached him and grabbed Tang Pae’s wrist.

“Seven, what?”

“...”

“Seven Steps Soul Chase?”

“Ha... haha.”

Cold sweat streamed down Tang Pae’s forehead.

“T-taoist! I would never do such a heinous thing to you...”

“Really? Then what is this in your hand?”

Chung Myung held his wrist and lightly shook Tang Pae’s hand. The poison pouch in his hand shook weakly.

“...This is... yes. Just ordinary mountain poison. Very weak...”

“Oh, really?”

“Y-yes...”

“Is this bastard playing with me right now?”

“Bam!”

Chung Myung’s fist plunged into Tang Pae’s abdomen without hesitation. The impact made Tang Pae’s mouth snap open. Chung Myung opened the poison pouch in his hand and shoved it directly into the open mouth.

“If it’s ordinary mountain poison, it shouldn’t be a big deal.”

“Ugh ugh! Ugh ugh!”

“It’s okay. You won’t die, won’t die. At worst, it’s Seven Step Soul Chase poison. Surely you won’t die from that?”

Tang Pae foamed at the mouth and fell backward. The Tang Family trains from childhood to build poison resistance, so the young lord wouldn’t die from his own poison, but having taken that much Seven Step Soul Chase poison means he won’t function properly for a while.

“Grrr...”

As expected, Tang Pae, fallen backward, began convulsing and twisting his body strangely.

Those who saw this were all half in shock. Where in the world would you see a Tang Family member collapse from their own poison? Especially the young lord of the Tang Family.

“Ah, you all like poisoning, huh?”

At Chung Myung’s question, the Tang Family’s disciples desperately shook their heads. But Chung Myung searched Tang Pae’s sleeves and began pulling out many things.

“T-those are dangerous if handled carelessly...!”

“But where have you used those...? Why are you so natural with holding them...?”

Chung Myung, holding many poison pouches from the Tang Family in both hands, began chuckling.

“Ah, have I used them before?”

“...”

“Come on, where would I have used these? Of course, this is my first time.”

“...I don’t think so.”

“You’re very familiar with them...”

“It’s my first time, first. So... just try dying, you bastards!”

“Your words don’t make sense!”

Chung Myung threw the poison pouches and released his qi. Green, black, and pink poisonous smoke spread instantly where the Tang Family stood.

“Eeeek!”

“No, what did that guy bring to have such equality!”

“D-don’t breathe! You’ll get poisoned!”

In the poisonous smoke feast that seemed unreal, Chung Myung tore off his ill-fitting clothes and laughed maniacally as he charged.

“Hehehehe! This is exciting!”

The Tang Family’s disciples trapped in the thick poison smoke shot up into the sky like fireworks. Pitifully.

It was the Heavenly Friends Alliance realizing only now the simple truth that all recklessness must pay a price.

Chapter 1133: Let’s Try To Get Along Together! (3)

“Waaaaaah!”

Something burst forth like fireworks amidst the colorful smoke.

“Aaaaaaah!”

One, then another, soared higher and higher.

Those that rose wrapped themselves in colorful smoke, drawing a circular rainbow in the sky.

It was truly a rare and splendid sight. If one didn’t know that the colorful smoke was Tang Family’s poisonous smoke and that the things jumping up were people, it would seem magical.

Of course, whether aware or not, to those watching from outside it might seem quite comical... but for those inside the poisonous smoke, there was no greater tragedy.

“Kweeeaaak!”

Another person was struck by Chung Myung’s fist and flew far away.

“Run away!”

“Oh, here he comes!”

Those who turned their heads in fright saw a terrifying sight: Chung Myung charging through the black poisonous smoke, his eyes glowing red one after another.

‘Oh my god.’

It’s scary enough to haunt dreams. No matter how terrifying the Heavenly Demon or whatever, nothing could be more frightening than that.

Chung Myung, enraged like a wounded tiger struck by an arrow, kicked a fleeing Mount Hua disciple from behind.

“I’m pissed off, you bastards!”

“Kyaaaaa!”

With a bang, the struck disciple spun wildly and was flung forward. Everyone’s mouths dropped open at the incomprehensible scene.

“Die! You bastards! Die!”

Chung Myung grabbed another victim, threw him to the ground, climbed on his back, and began pounding with his fists.

“Sa-sago!”

Tang Soso, watching the scene, grabbed the sleeve of Yu Yiseol, their last hope.

“Uh, please do something. Sahyung has completely lost it.”

“How?”

“Sahyung listens to Sago sometimes. We have to try to stop him!”

Yu Yiseol tilted her head slightly to the side.

“That guy?”

“...”

“Him?”

Uh... that’s a bit of an overexpectation. Yes, sago.

“Soso.”

“Yes?”

“Run.”

With that expressionless command, Yu Yiseol quickly fled. Her face showed no change, but her feet looked very urgent.

“...No hope then.”

Tang Soso muttered weakly and quickly followed behind her.

Meanwhile, those who understood this chaotic situation the least were the Beast Palace disciples.

“What kind of ridiculous situation is this?”

“I don’t get why everyone’s so worked up. It’s just one person. Even if he’s Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.”

The reason was simple. To them, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint was merely a friend of the Beast Palace, a descendant of Plum Blossom Sword Saint, recognized by the Beast Palace Lord.

Of course, having witnessed Mount Hua’s power at the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s initiation, they didn’t intend to ignore Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, the core of Mount Hua. But there was no reason to fear just one person.

So to them, the whole scene seemed nothing but ridiculous, like watching a well-rehearsed opera.

“Maybe he’s just pretending to be scary?”

“Should we play along too?”

“No need to go that far.”

“I think it’s just because of that poison. Tang Family’s poison seems worse than we thought.”

The Beast Palace disciples didn’t feel particularly threatened even as Chung Myung charged at them.

“Hey, hey. Don’t hurt him too much. He’s still a precious person, cherished by the Palace Lord.”

“Just hold him down properly.”

Those who scoffed stepped forward slightly. Though Mount Hua’s Sword Saint was a renowned warrior for his age in the Central Plains and couldn’t be handled alone, the number of Beast Palace disciples gathered there easily exceeded a hundred.

Besides, since this wasn’t a real fight, there was no risk of death, so why be afraid?

“Alright, first I’ll...”

One Beast Palace disciple sneered lightly and stepped forward. He planned to grab Chung Myung’s foot first.

Though Chung Myung charged like a beast, handling beasts was the Beast Palace’s specialty. If Chung Myung charged like a wounded boar, they would dodge sideways and then target his legs...

It was at that moment.

As Chung Myung pushed off the ground running ahead, his figure blurred and suddenly appeared right in front of the Beast Palace disciple.

“Huh?”

Before he could doubt if he had seen wrong, his world went pitch black.

‘What is this?’

Why did the world suddenly darken... Ah, no. It’s not the world darkening, but something in front of his eyes.

What is this...?

“Fi...”

Kwahhh!

Before the word “Fist” could even finish leaving his mouth, Chung Myung’s fist slammed into the Beast Palace disciple’s face. The disciple was struck in an instant and flew twistedly like a wooden puppet kicked by a child.

Only the blood splattered along his flight path proved that the one hit and flying was not a puppet but a person.

“Uh...?”

The Beast Palace disciples who saw the scene had their eyes pop out.

One hit? Just one hit?

Of course, the one who was hit wasn’t considered a great master by the Beast Palace standards. But if he was sent flying with one blow like that, wouldn’t he not even be recognized as a Beast Palace disciple?

But what is this...?

“Huu...”

Chung Myung, who had sent the disciple flying with a single blow, gleamed his eyes and grinned. The white teeth revealed between his slightly parted lips looked strangely ominous.

“Oh, I see. You guys still don’t understand the situation.”

“...”

“Today, you’re all going to die! I have to get close to you too! This is so exciting!”

“Your words don’t make sense...”

Like a flying tiger, Chung Myung dashed forward and smashed the jaw of the dazed man standing there.

Bang!

The struck Beast Palace disciple flew like a mouse kicked by an elephant. In this absurd situation, the Beast Palace disciples finally grasped what was happening.

But unfortunately, they had to learn a great lesson today: in the murim world, late comprehension always comes with a heavy price.

“Diiiiieeee!”

Chung Myung beat and kicked everyone in front of him indiscriminately. His eyes flashed with wild madness at every moment.

And the fate of those caught in that madness was miserable. Faces battered beyond recognition, bleeding from their noses, they collapsed heavily.

Even a skilled farmer cutting ripe rice with a sickle couldn’t lay them down more smoothly.

“Eeeek!”

The Beast Palace and Ice Palace disciples, sensing danger late, began to flee without looking back.

‘If caught, we will die!’

‘What kind of monster is that...!’

Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint. They had heard the name so much it was drilled into their ears. Of course, they knew he was strong. But who could have imagined he was this strong?

Without that poisonous smoke, they might have managed somehow, but the poisonous smoke covering everywhere combined with the rampaging Chung Myung became a disaster.

“We just have to go over there! Over there!”

“Just climb over the wall! You idiots! Why are you going to the entrance?”

Tang Family and the Beast Palace were the fastest. They saw the wide-open gate of the manor and a not-so-high wall.

“Just climb over there... huh?”

Those moving hurriedly suddenly widened their eyes.

Because just as they were about to climb the wall, familiar figures appeared.

“Family lord!”

Tang Zhan shouted joyfully. Tang Gunak had appeared leading the elders before them.

“F-family lord! There! That Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, no, that crazy Taoist has completely lost it! Quickly subdue him...!”

Having reinforcements like a thousand troops, how could they not be emboldened? Tang Zhan pointed behind with a brightened face.

But.

Thud!

Tang Zhan suddenly collapsed to the side.

Twitch. Twitch.

His body lying on the ground trembled convulsively.

Something popped off his forehead and rolled away. It was a wooden coin. Those who saw it blankly turned to look at Tang Gunak.

Tang Gunak lightly tossed a wooden coin in his hand into the air and caught it, speaking softly.

“Of course, I don’t agree with everything the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint says...”

As he spoke, the elders of Tang Gunak guarding behind stepped forward with grim faces.

“We need to verify. It’s not that I don’t beat him because he lacks authority, but that authority disappears because I don’t beat him.”

“...Yes?”

What do you mean by that...?

“There are no personal feelings involved.”

Tang Gunak shook his head solemnly.

“Of course, I too have been displeased, frustrated, annoyed, and exasperated with you.”

Um... you seem full of personal feelings in that?

“Those are things one who ascends to the family lord’s position must naturally bear. It’s not something to be resolved with personal feelings. So... I hope you understand this is not a decision made out of personal sentiment.”

Tang Gunak curled his lips into a smile and snatched the wooden coin he tossed high into the sky.

As everyone shivered sensing that smile resembled Chung Myung’s, it was then.

“Step on him!”

“Yes!”

As soon as Tang Gunak gave the order, the elders guarding behind him gleamed their eyes and charged toward Tang Gunak.

“You insolent brats!”

“You thought the elders’ council was gone and this was your world, so you acted arrogantly!”

“You don’t even know manners! Back in my day...!”

After the elders’ council collapsed and power shifted to the younger generation, the sidelined elders took this chance to vent their long-suppressed anger.

It was the moment the pushed-around old-timers launched a counterattack on Tang Family.

Not only Tang Family, but similar things were happening everywhere.

“Eek! Palace Lord! Why are you doing this?”

“Hahaha! This is all training! Training!”

Meng So, with his huge frame, moved like lightning, striking fleeing Beast Palace disciples, while Seol So-Baek, who appeared suddenly, gave orders to the elders to efficiently beat down the Ice Palace disciples.

So the two were the ones feeling wronged.

“No...”

Namgung Do-Wei’s face turned pale. Standing beside him, Im So-Byeong groaned with a dark, deathly face.

They clearly saw the sect leaders beating their own disciples.

“Why are we here...”

Shouldn’t they be over there together? Why were only the two of them getting beaten here?

And at that moment, someone slowly walked toward the two.

“What?”

“...”

“I think I know why. Want me to tell you?”

The faces of the two, who met the person they least wanted to see, turned pitch black.

“Um...”

“Ah, no. Maybe we don’t need to know that Taoist..”

“No, no. You have to know.”

Chung Myung smirked. At the same time, a grinding sound came from his clenched fist.

“Even if no one else, you two should know it. Right?”

Bang!

Chung Myung’s foot dug into the ground.

“From now on, I’ll teach you clearly.”

“...”

“Make sure to engrave it properly on your body.”

“Um... if the Taoist beats me, I’ll die.”

“It’s okay. I’m beating you to death.”

“...You aren’t even human.”

Chung Myung chuckled as he charged at Im So-Byeong.

And so, after a moment.

As the poisonous smoke that filled the vast training ground began to dissipate, only one person stood in that wide training ground.

“Hooah...”

Chung Myung wiped his face with his sleeve, looking completely refreshed.

“I feel so relieved inside.”

“...”

“How about it? Don’t you think we’re a bit closer now?”

The half-bodies piled in layers on the ground seemed to hold back tears.

Chapter 1134: Let’s Try To Get Along Together ! (4)

“Ha...”

Chung Myung stretched with an endlessly refreshed face. His face even looked shiny.

“Now I feel like I can live a bit.”

“...”

“So you should have taken it easy, sasuk.”

At Chung Myung’s glance downward and words, Baek Cheon’s eyes welled up with tears.

‘You told me to do it, you bastard.’

When it was time to say to beat without mercy, now say it was excessive. What rhythm am I supposed to dance to?

Of course... Baek Cheon also felt uneasy inside. It was a bit excessive. A bit... no, actually very excessive.

“Anyway, those who use swords don’t know moderation. If you let them loose, they just climb up accordingly.”

Crack. Crack.

Chung Myung twisted his neck left and right and looked around at the sprawled people.

Seeing the pitifully sprawled figures, he felt proud as if he had accomplished something great.

If they were all in normal condition, no matter how much Chung Myung cooperated with other sect leaders or elders, he wouldn’t have been able to sweep them away so neatly.

But these crazy guys hadn’t slept for almost ten days, repeatedly hitting each other, so they would fall over if anyone just touched them lightly. So they couldn’t face strong opponents who preserved their stamina by watching leisurely.

“Especially you! You bastard!”

Chung Myung poked and kicked the one collapsed at his feet.

Im So-Byeong, half out of his mind, rolled weakly as Chung Myung kicked him.

“You should do it moderately! Setting fire where people are sleeping?”

“Fire attack... has been... a strategy used since ancient times...”

“You still have energy, I see. You’re persistent. Die already!”

Chung Myung, having kicked Im So-Byeong away, turned around with a relieved face. Tang Gunak approached with a mysterious expression. Chung Myung greeted him.

“Thank you for your hard work.”

“Hmm.”

Tang Gunak, uncharacteristically, scratched his cheek slightly with a suspicious face.

“You did as you were told... but I’m not sure if this was really the right thing to do.”

“For someone who says that, you have quite a joyful expression.”

Tang Gunak blushed slightly and cleared his throat. Of course, he thinks this now, but when he was throwing coins at the Tang Family guys earlier, he felt quite relieved.

“Kids usually grow up by getting hit a bit.”

“Kids...”

Tang Gunak looked at the sprawled ones with a subtle expression. They are at an age where they would have already started families and had children if they were from a family sect. Can these guys really be called kids...?

“Well, we didn’t act out of emotion; it’s a kind of training.”

“...That’s true.”

Tang Gunak nodded at Meng So’s words. Of course, he felt a bit guilty about the part of not acting out of emotion.

“Everyone listen carefully.”

Though it was doubtful if the unconscious could hear well, Chung Myung shouted without concern.

“Tomorrow we’ll train the same way. Don’t whine, everyone come out.”

“...”

“Looks like you have leftover energy with nowhere to release it, so I’ll let you release it properly. That’s all!”

“...”

“Let’s go.”

“Y-yeah.”

“Ah, I feel refreshed. Hehehe.”

Chung Myung, accompanied by the leaders of each sect, moved away from the training ground.

Jo Gul, covered in dust and lying on the ground, muttered quietly as he watched.

“...Son of a bitch.”

Everyone strongly agreed with that in their hearts.

“Are you really okay with this?”

“What?”

“Everyone seems to have exceeded their limits, but repeating the same training in this situation...”

What Tang Gunak worries about is something else.

The “same training” Chung Myung mentioned is not the sparring among themselves. From tomorrow, Chung Myung, other sect leaders, and elders will push them hard in training.

“Is there a problem?”

“...Shouldn’t they be given some rest?”

“Ah. The family lord is overconfident.”

“Hmm?”

“Do you think we could win if they weren’t in that state?”

Tang Gunak fell silent as if deep in thought, his expression hardened.

“It wouldn’t be easy.”

“We have to beat them thoroughly now so it’s easier to deal with them later.”

“...That intention seems a bit impure.”

Chung Myung replied indifferently.

“That’s the point.”

Tang Gunak tilted his head.

“What do you mean by that?”

Chung Myung chuckled.

“Now everyone has figured out the skill level of those they are with. They saw the bottom by punching each other.”

“That’s right.”

Though the problem is they saw it a bit excessively.

“I thought that far. It’s just a bit excessive than expected... a bit, um. Yes, quite a lot.”

“Very much indeed.”

“...Honestly, I didn’t expect them to be that crazy. Mount Hua is one thing, but why is Tang family like that?”

“Have you heard the saying ‘You become blackened by ink’?”

“Well, Tang family is a bit dark indeed.”

Tang Gunak was speechless and looked back with a displeased face, and Chung Myung shrugged.

“Anyway, we have to get there. People can’t truly understand each other under awkward politeness.”

“But won’t only bad feelings remain?”

“That’s why the following training is necessary.”

“Huh?”

Chung Myung grinned.

“Do you know when a person truly needs comrades?”

“...I don’t know.”

“When you don’t have the strength to even move a finger, but there’s an enemy in front of you that you can’t handle alone.”

Chung Myung said calmly.

“At that time, you desperately want anyone, someone. Whether it’s the guy you fought yesterday or the one you usually hate, you can’t be more glad and thankful.”

Tang Gunak quietly nodded. Though he hasn’t experienced it, he can understand. Just think about how the Namgung Family reacted to Mount Hua when trapped on Plum Blossom Island.

Since then, the Namgung Family regards Mount Hua as an unparalleled benefactor.

“Then they naturally come together.”

“...That means...”

Tang Gunak, understanding Chung Myung’s words, narrowed his eyes slightly.

“So you’re saying people need a common enemy to unite.”

“Yes. A powerful enemy that no one dares to face.”

Chung Myung slightly raised his head and looked at the distant sky.

When the Demonic Cult first appeared, the Central Plains were literally divided and chaotic. More precisely, even in the early war against the cult, it was a mess.

No need to look for reasons. For the martial artists of the time, the crazy cult members who suddenly appeared were less troublesome than the neighbors who had been growling at each other.

Isn’t that why Mount Hua couldn’t share the battlefield with Southern Edge?

But as the war continued, and they realized the cult’s power, and above all understood who the Heavenly Demon was, their relationships somewhat reconciled.

Except outside the battlefield, at least those at the front entrusted their backs to disciples of enemy sects without doubt.

That’s how comradeship is born.

‘Well... those guys all died at the summit of the Hundred Thousand Great Mountains.’

Probably this greatly influenced the later chaos in the martial world and Mount Hua’s destruction. If even one survivor remained, they would have become heroes and led the future murim world.

The cowards and incompetent who didn’t join the death squad survived and led the murim world, causing all these problems.

In any case, the conclusion is that the Heavenly Demon was the one who at least outwardly united the divided martial world.

“This time too, it will end up like that. Jang Ilso is here, and the Heavenly Demon might return.”

“Hmm.”

“But if you try to unite then, it will be too late. Then you won’t just get beaten like now, but pay with your life.”

How many sacrifices and blood were shed before the murim world moved as one?

“You have to experience it in advance. Seeing the bottom of your allies, fighting a powerful enemy, and even having those you fought with growling beside you as strength.”

“...”

“It can’t compare to experiencing it directly, but at least it’s better than not doing it.”

Chung Myung turned to look at Tang Gunak and Meng So. Meng So spoke before Chung Myung could.

“If we’re going to do it, do it properly. Don’t regret losing disciples later.”

“Yes.”

Chung Myung grinned.

“Well... we’ll hear some complaints, but hearing complaints is better than watching the kids die, right?”

Whether he said it with that intention or not, these words pierced Tang Gunak and Meng So like a sharp dagger.

As Chung Myung said, any sect leader’s top priority is to protect disciples. Not to fear complaints or lose authority, but to lead them to survival even if it means being cursed or seeing them rise up.

Yes. That was how it should be.

But until now, Tang Gunak and Meng So couldn’t do that. They just stepped back, saying it was impossible for them and only Mount Hua could do it.

“...It was this side then.”

“What do you mean?”

Tang Gunak gave a bitter smile as he saw Chung Myung feigning ignorance.

‘I thought he was angry at those kids.’

Actually, Chung Myung’s anger was directed at the sect leaders. Beating those kids today was punishment for their negligence and a warning to the sect leaders who allowed the situation to get this bad.

“...I understand what you mean.”

Tang Gunak sighed deeply.

The Tang family also knew they had to change. Considering what they would face, survival would be difficult otherwise. But somehow, he had excluded himself from the change he envisioned.

He believed that if Chung Myung could lead the Tang family’s kids well, the Tang family would change too.

‘No way.’

If the upper water isn’t clear, how can the lower water be clear? It’s obvious when you think about it.

“I’m ashamed.”

“You’re just saying nonsense now.”

Tang Gunak smiled bitterly watching Chung Myung shrug as if clueless. Meng So also nodded seriously, sharing the same thoughts.

‘Treating the Green Forest King and Namgung’s young lord the same as them wasn’t a punishment.’

They didn’t need to be whipped separately. They were already mixing with others and trying to change.

Understanding that meaning, he realized how pathetic he was to have laughed at them. His face flushed anew.

How shameful it was to mock those who tried to change while being stagnant oneself.

The kids got beaten today, but the real punishment was for Tang Gunak and Meng So.

“Ahem.”

Tang Gunak cleared his throat loudly, steeled his resolve, and looked at Chung Myung.

“...If what you say is true, we must become the most powerful enemy. Even if some bad feelings accumulate.”

“That’s better than fighting among themselves.”

When Tang Gunak and Meng So spoke as if understanding, Chung Myung grinned.

“If we’re going to do it, please do it thoroughly.”

“Understood.”

“Leave it to us.”

Nodding, Tang Gunak suddenly realized something.

‘In the end, all this training was about everyone becoming one mind.’

They resent, fight, and growl over minor differences. Then, facing a powerful enemy, they unite with those they fought yesterday to resist.

This is what they will have to face against the Demonic Cult someday. Chung Myung is creating the pain Heavenly Friends Alliance will endure in the future in a much milder way here.

The reason for making them go through this troublesome and frustrating process was very clear.

Chung Myung’s principle is one: to save even one more disciple of Mount Hua. Now, those to be saved are not only from Mount Hua.

Not just words, but sincerely.

“...I misunderstood you a bit.”

“What?”

“I’m embarrassed, but I really thought you beat the kids out of anger. I didn’t think you had a plan.”

“...”

“Now I see, even scolding those kids today was all your plan...”

Tang Gunak suddenly stopped speaking.

Because Chung Myung rolled his eyes and avoided eye contact.

“...You?”

“Ha... haha! Of course! It was supposed to be today! I had it all planned!”

“...”

“Oh dear, I’ve been caught. How embarrassing.”

Tang Gunak, staring blankly at Chung Myung’s retreating back, soon burst into laughter.

“Can’t be helped.”

Shaking his head, he straightened his shoulders.

He understood Chung Myung’s intention. Now his own task was clear.

“Then I’ll try being the evil sect leader for a while.”

Tang Gunak, full of enthusiasm, clenched his fist tightly.

The cracking sound from his knuckles seemed to foretell the future of the Heavenly Friends Alliance members who had no idea what was coming.

Chapter 1135: Let’s Try To Get Along Together ! (5)

Crack!

A worn coin, stained with hand grime, was slammed onto Baek Cheon’s forehead, who resisted until the very end.

Snap!

The wooden coin shattered into pieces as Baek Cheon foamed at the mouth and fell backward.

Buzz.

From his outstretched forehead, a white steam rose gently.

Tang Gunak, who had taken down even Baek Cheon who resisted until the end, lightly shook off the hand that threw the coin.

“Mount Hua’s people certainly have tenacity.”

“Compared to that, the Tang family guys have no persistence at all.”

“...Is that so?”

The Tang family members lying face down flinched at the brief conversation. Even without looking up, they could clearly imagine the eyes with which Tang Gunak was looking down on them.

“Well, it can’t be helped. Our kids are those who tumbled down those rough mountains, while the Tang family kids lived comfortably in the fertile Sichuan lands. How can they be compared?”

“...That sounds like you’re saying the Tang family lacks fighting spirit because they live in comfort?”

“Hahaha. That’s hilarious. Saying the Tang family has no fighting spirit. Hahaha.”

“...”

“Haha...”

“...”

“You weren’t joking?”

Gnash.

The sound of Tang Gunak grinding his teeth was clearly heard. At that chilling noise, those lying down trembled again.

‘No, that crazy bastard...’

‘Why does he have to provoke people to that extent?’

Those who had fallen felt like coughing up blood, but Chung Myung never cared about such things before, nor would he in the future.

“Ah, now that I think about it, maybe that’s not the case.”

“...What do you mean by that?”

Chung Myung clasped his hands and supported the back of his head, jokingly speaking.

“Now that I think about it, it’s probably not because they live comfortably. The Namgung Family kids, who grew up even more comfortably than the Tang family, definitely have persistence.”

“...”

“Then why is the Tang family like that? I just can’t figure it out. Is their land unlucky? Or is it something innate... Ahem.”

At that moment, Tang Gunak’s venomous gaze fixed on Tang Pae and Tang Zhan. Already cautious, the two quickly buried their heads into the floor.

“...The reason probably doesn’t matter. We just need to change the outcome.”

“Well, that’s true. But it’s not an easy task.”

“Even if it’s hard, it has to be done. Somehow.”

Unseen tears welled up at the corner of Tang Pae’s eyes.

Today’s Tang Gunak was different from yesterday. Of course, he had pushed them aggressively yesterday as well, but today, as if he had a knife in his mouth, he rampaged on people with fierce determination.

Just recalling the murderous aura Tang Gunak emitted made one’s pants wet; why provoke such a person even more? Why on earth!

That damn Taoist bastard!

Meanwhile, Chung Myung glanced at the sprawled figures and spoke.

“Fortunately, there should be plenty of time. It looks like they still have a long way to go to get beaten.”

“I don’t know if that’s fortunate or unfortunate.”

Chung Myung chuckled at Tang Gunak’s words and shouted toward the sprawled ones.

“We’ll have the same training tomorrow. Make sure to prepare properly and come out.”

“...”

“Though no matter how you prepare, the result will probably be the same. Hehehe.”

Chung Myung turned and left the training ground. The elders of each sect and Meng So followed behind him after glancing at the fallen.

The only one who did not follow Chung Myung and stayed behind was Tang Gunak, who frowned and spoke.

“Young lord.”

“...”

“Young lord.”

“Yes, yes! Family lord!”

Tang Pae suddenly raised his head. At that moment, he met Tang Gunak’s cold, subdued gaze. Tang Pae trembled. It was a look rarely seen recently, reminiscent of Tang Gunak’s eyes before meeting Chung Myung.

“Everyone must at least do their part.”

“S-sorry...”

“Is there anyone among you who showed the qualities worthy of being called the descendants of the Sichuan Tang family today?”

“...”

“Mount Hua’s Sword Saint might have said it as a joke, but to me, it doesn’t sound like a joke. Since you were given everything without earning it with your own hands, there’s no reason to be anxious or desperate.”

Tang Pae silently bowed his head deeply.

“As punishment... today, the entire Tang family fasts.”

“...Yes.”

Since they failed to earn their meal, there was no room for excuses about not eating.

Tang Gunak glanced around everyone with an emotionless gaze and soon left the training ground. At the same time, Tang Pae, who was barely holding on, collapsed and buried his head into the floor.

After a long time, Jo Gul staggered and got up.

“Ughhh. No... did everyone pick the same day?”

His voice was full of irritation and fatigue. Jo Gul glanced at the person lying beside him like a corpse and said.

“Sahyung.”

When there was still no response, he poked Yoon Jong’s side with his foot.

“Sahyung, are you alive? Sahyung.”

Poke. Poke.

“Dead?”

“...Not dead, you bastard.”

“Eh, what? I thought you were dead.”

“...Ugh.”

A groan escaped from Yoon Jong’s mouth.

If he had even a little energy left, he would have mercilessly dealt with that insolent Jo Gul, but now he was so exhausted that even if the Evil Sect rampaged, there was nothing he could do. Above all, the crown of his head hurt so much from Chung Myung’s blow that it was hard to even speak.

How does Jo Gul have the strength to tease that brat like that even after going through the same ordeal? He got hit twice as much.

“But isn’t this a bit too much?”

“What?”

“No... I’m just speaking the truth. Chung Myung and the sect leaders and elders have teamed up and charge at us. How can we possibly deal with that! What kind of training is this? It’s just beating disguised as training.”

“What’s new? We’ve always been on the receiving end.”

“Huh? Now that you mention it...”

Jo Gul tilted his head. Meanwhile, Yoon Jong sighed deeply. Though he didn’t say it, he shared the same thought.

By numbers, it would be natural for them to win, but as repeatedly emphasized, in the murim world, the difference in numbers doesn’t mean much.

An adult can exert at most ten times the strength of a six-year-old child. But that doesn’t mean the adult is exactly ten times stronger.

Though unlikely, if an adult truly charged at six-year-old children, it would be natural to defeat not ten but a hundred of them.

In the murim world, the gap between an absolute warrior and others is even greater.

Mount Hua, which has constantly faced Chung Myung, is familiar with such situations, but the problem is...

Yoon Jong glanced at those sprawled out. He couldn’t see the expressions of those face down, but he could see the faces of those lying on their backs looking at the sky.

Yoon Jong came to a clear conclusion.

‘They’re all done for.’

Wouldn’t someone who confidently left the exam hall believing they had the perfect answer, only to realize that night they were disqualified for not writing their name, have a face like that?

No, their faces looked even better compared to these utterly defeated ones.

Yoon Jong fully understood their feelings.

‘We were like that at first too.’

The murim world is full of warriors, but those recognized as absolute warriors are surprisingly rare. If a sect has even one or two, it can be considered prestigious.

So, many who call themselves martial artists never see the face of an absolute warrior in their lifetime.

Those belonging to prestigious Justice factions have some chance to see their elders, so their situation is somewhat better. But absolute masters, busy improving their own skills, rarely mingle with the many children of families like flies. At best, they might teach a few talented kids a few times as a game.

‘Having seen such gentlemen roll their eyes and charge with full force.’

If you don’t lose your soul after being hit head-on by the killing intent of an absolute warrior, that would be stranger.

“Ugh.”

At that moment, Baek Cheon, who had fallen last, staggered and raised his upper body.

“As expected of the sasuk, your recovery is fast.”

“He has gotten used to getting hit.”

“True. I’ve been hit quite a bit, but compared to the sasuk, it’s just a scratch.”

Hearing words that could be praise or insult, Baek Cheon grimaced.

“Damn...”

He ground his teeth fiercely.

“Just that Chung Myung bastard alone is driving me crazy.”

Facing Chung Myung, who single-handedly sweeps through Mount Hua, is no easy task. Moreover, Meng So, who draws more attention than Chung Myung because of his size, rampages like a beast, and between them, Tang Gunak’s hidden weapons fly.

The elders of each sect filled the gaps barely found, leaving no solution.

Of course, the number of trainees also increased, but...

“You’re a hindrance.”

At Yu Yiseol’s quiet words, Baek Cheon deeply sympathized.

‘It would be better if they weren’t here.’

He had never imagined that having allies could become a hindrance. Yet, such an absurd thing was happening in this training ground.

The formations they perfected to face Chung Myung were utterly useless. Others interfered in their movement directions, and the chaotic atmosphere of other sects spread to Mount Hua.

He even thought it would be better to face Chung Myung, Meng So, and Tang Gunak with only Mount Hua, without those others.

‘Is war always this complicated?’

He thought that just building a stronger force would win. But what he experienced today in this training ground was enough to fundamentally twist that thought.

‘If power is overly concentrated in one place like this, it could actually be harmful.’

Then what should be done? If gathering strength causes interference, but not gathering strength means you can’t face a powerful enemy?

“...In this case, each sect must move separately, and a few must go around supporting the places where they are being pushed back.”

At Yoon Jong’s words, Baek Cheon nodded heavily.

Not wrong. In this situation, that’s the best. But even so...

It was at that moment.

“Damn it!”

One of the Beast Palace Archers suddenly stood up and angrily shouted with a swollen face.

“Why do you keep poking around in front? The Palace Lord is not someone you can handle! Even real swords don’t pierce the Palace Lord’s body, so what do you think you can do with wooden swords!”

At those words, Jo Gul narrowed his eyes.

“Are you seriously talking to us now?”

“Yeah! You Mount Hua bastards! What do you think you can do with that poking swordplay, blocking our way! Because of you, we can’t fight properly!”

Mount Hua disciples gaped. Hindrance? Who exactly is hindering whom to say that?

“No...”

But there was no need to argue. Someone else would get angry at those words.

“Muscleheads spouting nonsense. If you hadn’t interfered and let us and Mount Hua unite, the situation would have been twice as good.”

“What? Because we use the same sword?”

“That’s just the truth. You guys are of no help at all!”

The Ice Palace immediately sided with Mount Hua.

At that moment, Im So-Byeong, who was listening, snorted.

“Then why don’t you two face each other yourselves?”

Then Namgung Do-Wei gritted his teeth and intervened.

“Do not blame our allies. Why create division!”

“Oh my. The esteemed Namgung Family members even talk to this filthy Evil Sect bastard. I’m honored.”

“...What?”

They openly mock and shift blame to each other. The emotional rift is so deep that even in this situation, they grind their teeth at one another.

Baek Cheon sighed deeply.

‘I don’t know if this is right, Chung Myung.’

His gaze turned toward the place where Chung Myung had gone.

Chapter 1136: Still, What Can We Do? (1)

“This, damned!”

A curse slipped out from Jo Gul’s mouth. Gritting his teeth, he swung his sword forward with all his might.

Kaaang!

The moment swords clashed, the muscles in his wrist twisted. He somehow suppressed a scream, but there was no stopping his body from bouncing backward from the impact.

‘Ugh!’

Someone caught and supported his back as he was shot backward like a cannonball.

There was no need to turn his head to confirm. Feeling Yoon Jong standing behind him, Jo Gul instinctively pulled back his foot. Then, as he stepped on Yoon Jong’s knee, Yoon Jong lightly flicked his knee, lifting him upward.

“Hyaaah!”

Jo Gul soared up and then fell down, while Yoon Jong charged straight ahead.

As he charged forward, Yoon Jong focused on the person ahead. The moment he saw Chung Myung’s two eyes, chillingly cold, a shiver ran through his chest.

“Ugh!”

Yoon Jong’s sword, enduring that pressure somehow, swung with utmost delicacy. Unlike his burning chest, it was a perfectly orthodox sword technique.

Jo Gul’s sword descended overlapping Yoon Jong’s sword. A fast and sharp sword, perfectly contrasting Yoon Jong’s.

But.

Bang!

Chung Myung’s sword moved like a flash and simultaneously struck both swords away. It was twice as fast as Jo Gul’s sword and twice as delicate as Yoon Jong’s.

And the power contained within it was incomparable.

“Ugh!”
